using System;using System.Collections;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using System.Threading.Tasks;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Integration smoke test for the map editor tool. The editor lives in the tools mod, which is compiled separately at runtime without the TOOLS define and without res:// script paths, so its types are reached by reflection like <see cref="SceneBuilderAutotest"/> does (a godot scene can only instantiate scripts from the core assembly). Checks the two things unit tests cannot: <list type="number"> <item>the authoring controller survives a JSON round-trip in a real process (region polygon, marker position, label position);</item> <item>the window constructs and enters the tree without touching Godot APIs incorrectly (custom chrome, draw callback, input subscriptions).</item> </list> Exit code 0 = pass. </summary>
public partial class MapEditorAutotest:Node{private const string WindowTypeName="Cthangover.Tools.MapEditor.MapEditorWindow";private const string ControllerTypeName="Cthangover.Tools.MapEditor.MapEditorController";private const string WidgetTypeName="Cthangover.Mods.Map.MapWidget";private const string ZonesLayerTypeName="Cthangover.Mods.Map.MapZonesLayer";private int _passed;private int _failed;private bool _finished;private Window _window;/// <summary> Arms the 25-second watchdog and starts the async smoke-test sequence when the test scene loads. </summary>
public override void _Ready(){var watchdog=GetTree().CreateTimer(25d);watchdog.Timeout+=OnWatchdogFired;Start();}private void OnWatchdogFired(){if(_finished)return;Fail("watchdog fired — aborting");Finish();}private async void Start(){try{// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready.
var controllerType=FindType(ControllerTypeName);var windowType=FindType(WindowTypeName);if(controllerType==null||windowType==null){Fail("tools assembly not loaded (map editor types not found)");Finish();return;}CheckController(controllerType);await CheckWindow(windowType,controllerType);Finish();}catch(Exception ex){Fail("exception: "+ex);Finish();}}/// <summary> Creates a region, a marker and a label through the controller's public API, serialises them, re-imports and verifies the structural properties survive the map file round-trip. </summary>
private void CheckController(Type controllerType){var controller=Activator.CreateInstance(controllerType);var region=controllerType.GetMethod("AddRegion").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());var marker=controllerType.GetMethod("AddMarker").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());var label=controllerType.GetMethod("AddLabel").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(region,"ID","test_region");SetProp(region,"Title","ui/map/test/region");SetProp(region,"Scene","test_scene");SetProp(marker,"ID","test_marker");SetProp(marker,"ImagePos",new Vector2(0.25f,0.75f));SetProp(label,"ID","test_label");SetProp(label,"LabelPos",new Vector2(0.6f,0.4f));var json=(string)controllerType.GetMethod("ToJson").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());Check(json.Contains("\"Items\""),"json envelope present");Check(json.Contains("\"points\""),"region polygon serialised");Check(json.Contains("\"image_pos\""),"marker position serialised");Check(json.Contains("\"label_pos\""),"label position serialised");var restored=Activator.CreateInstance(controllerType);controllerType.GetMethod("FromJson").Invoke(restored,new object[]{json});var zones=GetProp(restored,"Zones")as IEnumerable;Check(zones!=null&&zones.Cast<object>().Count()==3,"three zones restored");var restoredRegion=FindZone(zones,"test_region");var points=GetProp(restoredRegion,"Points")as IEnumerable;Check(restoredRegion!=null&&points!=null&&points.Cast<object>().Count()>=3,"region polygon restored");var restoredMarker=FindZone(zones,"test_marker");Check(restoredMarker!=null&&(Vector2)GetProp(restoredMarker,"ImagePos")==new Vector2(0.25f,0.75f),"marker position restored");var restoredLabel=FindZone(zones,"test_label");Check(restoredLabel!=null&&(Vector2)GetProp(restoredLabel,"LabelPos")==new Vector2(0.6f,0.4f),"label position restored");}/// <summary> Constructs the editor window in a live Godot process and verifies its interactive preview exists after a frame. The window is built entirely from code (no tscn), so construction is the risk this covers. Also checks that a fresh icon-less marker gets a placeholder handle, so it stays visible and draggable before an icon is chosen. </summary>
private async Task CheckWindow(Type windowType,Type controllerType){_window=(Window)Activator.CreateInstance(windowType);await NextFrame();GetTree().Root.AddChild(_window);await NextFrame();var canvas=GetField(windowType,_window,"_canvas")as Control;Check(canvas is not null,"map editor canvas created");var jsonEdit=GetField(windowType,_window,"_jsonEdit")as TextEdit;Check(jsonEdit!=null,"json preview created");if(canvas!=null){var controller=GetField(windowType,_window,"_controller");// The window opens the first real map file on start; the checks below count zones and labels, so they start from an empty map.
controllerType.GetMethod("Clear").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());controllerType.GetMethod("AddMarker").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());canvas.Size=new Vector2(800f,600f);canvas.GetType().GetMethod("RebuildHandles").Invoke(canvas,Array.Empty<object>());var handles=GetField(canvas.GetType(),canvas,"_handles")as ICollection;Check(handles is{Count:>0},"fresh marker gets a draggable placeholder handle");var label=controllerType.GetMethod("AddLabel").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(label,"ID","autotest_label");SetProp(label,"Title","ui/map/test/autotest_label");var hiddenLabel=controllerType.GetMethod("AddLabel").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(hiddenLabel,"ID","autotest_hidden_label");SetProp(hiddenLabel,"Title","ui/map/test/autotest_hidden_label");SetProp(hiddenLabel,"Hidden",true);var region=controllerType.GetMethod("AddRegion").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(region,"ID","autotest_region");SetProp(region,"Title","ui/map/test/autotest_region");SetProp(region,"Scene","home_outside");var lockedRegion=controllerType.GetMethod("AddRegion").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(lockedRegion,"ID","autotest_locked_region");SetProp(lockedRegion,"Title","ui/map/test/autotest_locked_region");SetProp(lockedRegion,"Scene","home_outside");SetProp(lockedRegion,"Locked",true);var noSceneRegion=controllerType.GetMethod("AddRegion").Invoke(controller,Array.Empty<object>());SetProp(noSceneRegion,"ID","autotest_no_scene_region");SetProp(noSceneRegion,"Title","ui/map/test/autotest_no_scene_region");await CheckPreview(windowType);}_window.QueueFree();_window=null;}/// <summary> Exercises the game-like preview: the editor must be able to host the runtime map widget fed with the edited zones, render a label from that data, report the preview state, and give the editor back when the preview stops. </summary>
private async Task CheckPreview(Type windowType){var widgetType=FindType(WidgetTypeName);var start=windowType.GetMethod("StartPreview");Check(start!=null,"preview entry point present");if(start==null)return;var started=(bool)start.Invoke(_window,Array.Empty<object>());Check(started,"game-like preview starts");var preview=FindChildOf(_window,widgetType);Check(preview!=null,"preview hosts the runtime map widget");if(preview!=null){var markers=preview.GetNodeOrNull<Control>("MapRoot/Sheet/MarkersLayer");Check(markers is{}&&markers.GetChildCount()==1,"preview renders only the visible zone label");var zonesLayer=preview.GetNodeOrNull("MapRoot/Sheet/ZonesLayer");var zonesLayerType=FindType(ZonesLayerTypeName);Check(zonesLayer!=null&&zonesLayerType!=null&&zonesLayerType.IsInstanceOfType(zonesLayer),"preview hosts the interactive zones layer");if(zonesLayer!=null&&zonesLayerType!=null&&zonesLayerType.IsInstanceOfType(zonesLayer)){var go=preview.GetNodeOrNull<Button>("ZoneCard/Column/GoButton");var lockedNote=preview.GetNodeOrNull<Control>("ZoneCard/Column/Message");// This engine build's C# signal generator emits PascalCase signal names (e.g. ZoneClicked, not zone_clicked), so resolve the generated constant instead of hardcoding a particular spelling.
var signalNameType=zonesLayerType.GetNestedType("SignalName");object signalNameValue=signalNameType?.GetField("ZoneClicked")?.GetValue(null)??signalNameType?.GetField("zone_clicked")?.GetValue(null);var zoneClickedSignal=signalNameValue?.ToString()??"ZoneClicked";zonesLayer.EmitSignal(zoneClickedSignal,"autotest_region");var card=preview.GetNodeOrNull<Control>("ZoneCard");Check(card is{Visible:true},"clicking a region opens the location card");var cardName=preview.GetNodeOrNull<Label>("ZoneCard/Column/Head/Info/Name");Check(cardName is{}&&cardName.Text=="ui/map/test/autotest_region","location card shows the edited zone title");Check(go is{Disabled:false},"open zone stays enterable in preview");Check(lockedNote is{Visible:false},"open zone shows no locked note");zonesLayer.EmitSignal(zoneClickedSignal,"autotest_locked_region");Check(go is{}&&go.Disabled,"locked zone blocks the transition in preview");Check(lockedNote is{Visible:true},"locked zone shows the locked note in preview");zonesLayer.EmitSignal(zoneClickedSignal,"autotest_no_scene_region");Check(go is{}&&go.Disabled,"zone without a scene stays blocked in preview");Check(lockedNote is{Visible:true},"zone without a scene shows the locked note in preview");}}var active=windowType.GetProperty("PreviewActive");Check(active!=null&&(bool)active.GetValue(_window),"preview reports active");windowType.GetMethod("StopPreview")?.Invoke(_window,Array.Empty<object>());await NextFrame();Check(FindChildOf(_window,widgetType)==null,"preview widget removed after stop");}private static void SetProp(object target,string name,object value){var property=target.GetType().GetProperty(name);if(property==null)throw new MissingMemberException(target.GetType().Name,name);property.SetValue(target,value);}private static object GetProp(object target,string name){var property=target.GetType().GetProperty(name);if(property==null)throw new MissingMemberException(target.GetType().Name,name);return property.GetValue(target);}private static object FindZone(IEnumerable zones,string id){if(zones==null)return null;foreach(var zone in zones){if(string.Equals((string)GetProp(zone,"ID"),id,StringComparison.Ordinal))return zone;}return null;}private static Node FindChildOf(Node root,Type type){if(root==null||type==null)return null;foreach(var child in root.GetChildren()){if(child!=null&&type.IsInstanceOfType(child))return child;}return null;}private Type FindType(string fullName){foreach(var assembly in AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()){var type=assembly.GetType(fullName);if(type!=null)return type;}return null;}private static object GetField(Type startType,object instance,string name){var type=startType;while(type!=null){var field=type.GetField(name,BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);if(field!=null)return field.GetValue(instance);type=type.BaseType;}throw new MissingFieldException(startType.Name,name);}private async Task NextFrame(){await ToSignal(GetTree(),SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);}private void Check(bool condition,string label){if(condition)Pass(label);else Fail(label);}private void Pass(string label){_passed++;GameLogger.Log("MAP_EDITOR_TEST",$"  [PASS] {label}");}private void Fail(string message){_failed++;GameLogger.Log("MAP_EDITOR_TEST",$"  [FAIL] {message}",LogLevel.Error);}private void Finish(){if(_finished)return;_finished=true;if(_window!=null&&GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(_window))_window.QueueFree();GameLogger.Log("MAP_EDITOR_TEST",$"MapEditorAutotest: {_passed} passed, {_failed} failed");GameLogger.Log("MAP_EDITOR_TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name=map_editor passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}}}

===== FILE Core/Autotest/SceneBuilderAutotest.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Globalization;using System.IO;using System.Reflection;using System.Threading.Tasks;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Integration check for the Scene Builder tool chain, run inside a real game process because most of the pipeline (mod assembly events, Timer signals, Variant marshaling) cannot execute under plain unit tests. Via reflection it instantiates the tools-mod SceneBuilderWindow, opens cooking/cooking_panel.tscn through the real dropdowns, pushes an inspector edit through the private write-back handler, waits out the debounced auto-save and asserts the change reached the file on disk, then restores the original file content. Exit code 0 = pass. Launch: godot --path . scenes/test/scene_builder_autotest.tscn </summary>
public partial class SceneBuilderAutotest:Node{private const string TargetModId="cooking";// Dropdown labels carry the file name incl. extension (Path.GetFileName).
private const string TargetSceneName="cooking_panel.tscn";private const string TargetSceneResPath="res://mods/cooking/scenes/cooking_panel.tscn";private const string WindowTypeName="Cthangover.Core.UI.Tool.SceneBuilder.SceneBuilderWindow";private const string ProbeKey="anchor_right";private const float ProbeValue=0.75f;private int _failures;private bool _finished;private string _originalText;private Window _window;/// <summary> Arms the 25-second watchdog and starts the async check sequence when the test scene loads. </summary>
public override void _Ready(){var watchdog=GetTree().CreateTimer(25d);watchdog.Timeout+=OnWatchdogFired;Start();}private void OnWatchdogFired(){if(_finished)return;GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST","watchdog fired — aborting",LogLevel.Error);Finish();}private async void Start(){try{if(!PrepareOriginalFile()){Finish();return;}var windowType=FindWindowType();if(windowType==null){Fail("tools assembly not loaded (window type not found)");Finish();return;}_window=(Window)Activator.CreateInstance(windowType);// Root is busy inside its own child setup during _Ready — defer.
await NextFrame();GetTree().Root.AddChild(_window);await NextFrame();if(!await SelectSceneInDropdowns(windowType)){Finish();return;}var node=FindDescendantByName(GetModRoot(windowType),"BgImage");if(node==null){Fail("preview node 'BgImage' not found");Finish();return;}InvokeCommit(windowType,node);var editor=(TextEdit)GetField(windowType,_window,"_tscnEditor");var editorText=editor.Text;Check(editorText!=null&&editorText.Contains(ProbeKey+" = "+ProbeLiteral()),$"editor text patched with '{ProbeKey} = {ProbeLiteral()}'");InvokeBatch(windowType,node);await WaitSeconds(1.0d);var onDisk=File.ReadAllText(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath));Check(onDisk.Contains(ProbeKey+" = "+ProbeLiteral()),"auto-save wrote inspector probe to disk");Check(onDisk.Contains("offset_left = -5.0")&&onDisk.Contains("offset_right = 5.0"),"auto-save wrote overlay batch to disk");// 4) node creation through the real UI handler (no selection → parent is scene root)
InvokeOnWindow(windowType,_window,"OnAddNodePressed");var added=FindDescendantByName(GetModRoot(windowType),"Control");Check(added!=null,"live node 'Control' created under scene root");var textAfterAdd=((TextEdit)GetField(windowType,_window,"_tscnEditor")).Text;Check(textAfterAdd.Contains("[node name=\"Control\" type=\"Control\" parent=\".\"]"),"tscn section inserted for new node");await WaitSeconds(1.0d);var diskAfterAdd=File.ReadAllText(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath));Check(diskAfterAdd.Contains("[node name=\"Control\" type=\"Control\" parent=\".\"]"),"auto-save wrote new node section to disk");// 5) deletion of that node
SetField(windowType,_window,"_selectedNode",added);InvokeOnWindow(windowType,_window,"OnDeleteNodePressed");Check(FindDescendantByName(GetModRoot(windowType),"Control")==null,"live node removed from preview");var textAfterDelete=((TextEdit)GetField(windowType,_window,"_tscnEditor")).Text;Check(!textAfterDelete.Contains("[node name=\"Control\" type=\"Control\" parent=\".\"]"),"tscn section removed from editor text");await WaitSeconds(1.0d);var diskAfterDelete=File.ReadAllText(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath));Check(!diskAfterDelete.Contains("[node name=\"Control\" type=\"Control\" parent=\".\"]"),"auto-save persisted the deletion to disk");Finish();}catch(Exception ex){Fail("exception: "+ex);Finish();}}private bool PrepareOriginalFile(){_originalText=File.ReadAllText(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath));if(_originalText.Contains(ProbeKey+" = "+ProbeLiteral())){Fail($"file already contains probe value '{ProbeLiteral()}' — pick another key");return false;}return true;}// Mirrors TscnValueFormatter.FormatFloat for the probe value: shortest float round-trip plus a guaranteed decimal point.
private static string ProbeLiteral(){var s=ProbeValue.ToString("R",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);if(!s.Contains('.'))s+=".0";return s;}private Type FindWindowType(){foreach(var asm in AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()){var t=asm.GetType(WindowTypeName);if(t!=null)return t;}return null;}private async Task<bool>SelectSceneInDropdowns(Type windowType){var modDropdown=(OptionButton)GetField(windowType,_window,"_modDropdown");var sceneDropdown=(OptionButton)GetField(windowType,_window,"_sceneDropdown");var modIdx=FindItem(modDropdown,TargetModId);if(modIdx<0){Fail($"mod '{TargetModId}' missing from dropdown");return false;}modDropdown.Selected=modIdx;modDropdown.EmitSignal(OptionButton.SignalName.ItemSelected,(long)modIdx);await NextFrame();var sceneIdx=FindItem(sceneDropdown,TargetSceneName);if(sceneIdx<0){Fail($"scene '{TargetSceneName}' missing from dropdown");return false;}sceneDropdown.Selected=sceneIdx;sceneDropdown.EmitSignal(OptionButton.SignalName.ItemSelected,(long)sceneIdx);await NextFrame();return true;}private Node GetModRoot(Type windowType){var controller=GetField<object>(windowType,_window,"_controller");return GetField<Node>(controller.GetType(),controller,"_modRoot");}private void InvokeCommit(Type windowType,Node node){var mi=windowType.GetMethod("OnInspectorPropertyEdited",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);mi.Invoke(_window,new object[]{node,ProbeKey,Variant.From(ProbeValue)});}private static void InvokeOnWindow(Type type,object instance,string methodName){var mi=type.GetMethod(methodName,BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);if(mi==null)throw new MissingMethodException(type.Name,methodName);mi.Invoke(instance,Array.Empty<object>());}private static void SetField(Type type,object instance,string name,object value){var current=type;while(current!=null){var fi=current.GetField(name,BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);if(fi!=null){fi.SetValue(instance,value);return;}current=current.BaseType;}throw new MissingFieldException(type.Name,name);}private void InvokeBatch(Type windowType,Node node){var mi=windowType.GetMethod("OnOverlayBatchCommitted",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);var batch=new List<KeyValuePair<string,Variant>>{new KeyValuePair<string,Variant>("layout_mode",Variant.From(1L)),new KeyValuePair<string,Variant>("offset_left",Variant.From(-5f)),new KeyValuePair<string,Variant>("offset_right",Variant.From(5f))};mi.Invoke(_window,new object[]{node,batch});}private static int FindItem(OptionButton dropdown,string text){for(var i=0;i<dropdown.ItemCount;i++){if(dropdown.GetItemText(i)==text)return i;}return-1;}private static Node FindDescendantByName(Node root,string name){if(root==null)return null;if(root.Name==name)return root;foreach(var child in root.GetChildren()){var found=FindDescendantByName(child,name);if(found!=null)return found;}return null;}private static object GetField(Type startType,object instance,string name){var type=startType;while(type!=null){var fi=type.GetField(name,BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);if(fi!=null)return fi.GetValue(instance);type=type.BaseType;}throw new MissingFieldException(startType.Name,name);}private static T GetField<T>(Type startType,object instance,string name){return(T)GetField(startType,instance,name);}private async Task NextFrame(){await ToSignal(GetTree(),SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);}private async Task WaitSeconds(double seconds){await ToSignal(GetTree().CreateTimer(seconds),SceneTreeTimer.SignalName.Timeout);}private void Check(bool condition,string label){if(condition)GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST",$"PASS {label}");else{_failures++;GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST",$"FAIL {label}",LogLevel.Error);}}private void Fail(string message){_failures++;GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST",$"FAIL {message}",LogLevel.Error);}private void Finish(){if(_finished)return;_finished=true;// Stop the debounced auto-save before restoring, otherwise it may rewrite edited content over the original file after we exit.
if(_window!=null&&GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(_window)){var timer=GetField(_window.GetType(),_window,"_autosaveTimer")as Timer;timer?.Stop();}if(_originalText!=null&&File.Exists(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath)))File.WriteAllText(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(TargetSceneResPath),_originalText);GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST",_failures==0?"RESULT: ALL PASSED":$"RESULT: {_failures} FAILURES",_failures==0?LogLevel.Message:LogLevel.Error);GameLogger.Log("SCENE_BUILDER_TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name=scene_builder passed={(_failures == 0 ? 1 : 0)} failed={_failures}");GetTree().Quit(_failures==0?0:1);}}}
#endif

===== FILE Core/Autotest/TscnTestEntryPoint.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Test stub implementing <see cref="ITscnSceneEntryPoint"/> for integration testing. Sets a static <see cref="WasCalled"/> flag when <see cref="OnSceneBuilt"/> is invoked, allowing tests to verify the entry-point lifecycle. </summary>
public partial class TscnTestEntryPoint:Node,ITscnSceneEntryPoint{/// <summary>Set when <see cref="OnSceneBuilt"/> fires; reset via <see cref="Reset"/> between tests.</summary>
public static bool WasCalled{get;set;}/// <summary> Marks the entry point as invoked and logs the built scene root so the test log shows which scene reached the callback. </summary>
public void OnSceneBuilt(Node sceneRoot){WasCalled=true;GameLogger.Log("TSCN_TEST",$"TscnTestEntryPoint.OnSceneBuilt: root='{sceneRoot?.Name}'");}/// <summary>Clears the invocation flag before a new test run.</summary>
public static void Reset(){WasCalled=false;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/GodotLocator.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Resolves the Godot .NET executable for the current OS. Priority: explicit <c>--godot</c> argument, <c>GODOT_BIN</c> environment variable, then the bare name so the OS resolves it from PATH. Keeping the bare name (with the .exe suffix on Windows) is what makes the runner portable between developer machines and CI images. </summary>
public static class GodotLocator{public static string Locate(string explicitPath){if(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(explicitPath))return explicitPath;var fromEnvironment=Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("GODOT_BIN");if(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fromEnvironment))return fromEnvironment;return OperatingSystem.IsWindows()?"godot.exe":"godot";}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/IntegrationTestDefinition.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> One integration scene from <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c>. The runner passes <see cref="Scene"/> plus <see cref="Args"/> to Godot, parses the scene's <c>[TEST_RESULT]</c> line and enforces <see cref="TimeoutSeconds"/>. Adding a test is a manifest edit only — no runner code changes. </summary>
public class IntegrationTestDefinition{public string Name{get;set;}public string Scene{get;set;}public string[]Args{get;set;}/// <summary> When true the scene runs with a real window instead of <c>--headless</c>. Headless uses a dummy rendering server, so viewport captures are empty — screenshot/CV tests need this flag. </summary>
public bool Windowed{get;set;}public int TimeoutSeconds{get;set;}=120;}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ParsedSceneResult.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Machine-readable outcome extracted from a scene's output: the uniform <c>[TEST_RESULT]</c> line (with optional battle <c>core=</c> detail) and, for the battle coverage scene, the <c>coverage: N/M</c> summary. </summary>
public class ParsedSceneResult{public bool HasResult{get;set;}public string Name{get;set;}public string Core{get;set;}public int Passed{get;set;}public int Failed{get;set;}public string Coverage{get;set;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ProcessResult.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Captured outcome of an external process: merged stdout/stderr lines (Godot writes its logs to stdout, MSBuild errors to stderr), exit code, duration and whether the timeout had to kill it. </summary>
public class ProcessResult{public int ExitCode{get;set;}public bool TimedOut{get;set;}public TimeSpan Duration{get;set;}public List<string>Output{get;set;}=new();public string OutputText=>string.Join(Environment.NewLine,Output);}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ProcessRunner.cs =====
using System.Diagnostics;using System.Text;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Cross-platform process launcher for the test runner. Uses <see cref="ProcessStartInfo.ArgumentList"/> so paths and arguments with spaces survive on every OS, reads stdout/stderr asynchronously in UTF-8 (Godot logs are UTF-8) and kills the whole process tree on timeout, so a hung Godot can never stall the global run. </summary>
public static class ProcessRunner{public static ProcessResult Run(string fileName,IEnumerable<string>arguments,string workingDirectory,int timeoutSeconds){var startInfo=new ProcessStartInfo{FileName=fileName,WorkingDirectory=workingDirectory,UseShellExecute=false,RedirectStandardOutput=true,RedirectStandardError=true,StandardOutputEncoding=Encoding.UTF8,StandardErrorEncoding=Encoding.UTF8};foreach(var argument in arguments)startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(argument);var result=new ProcessResult();var stopwatch=Stopwatch.StartNew();using var process=new Process{StartInfo=startInfo};process.OutputDataReceived+=(_,e)=>Append(result,e.Data);process.ErrorDataReceived+=(_,e)=>Append(result,e.Data);process.Start();process.BeginOutputReadLine();process.BeginErrorReadLine();if(!process.WaitForExit(timeoutSeconds*1000)){result.TimedOut=true;try{process.Kill(entireProcessTree:true);}catch{}process.WaitForExit(5000);}else{process.WaitForExit();}stopwatch.Stop();result.Duration=stopwatch.Elapsed;result.ExitCode=result.TimedOut? -1:process.ExitCode;return result;}private static void Append(ProcessResult result,string line){if(line==null)return;lock(result.Output)result.Output.Add(line);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/Program.cs =====
using System.Text;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Cross-platform entry point for the whole test suite: builds the game assembly, runs xUnit unit tests, then every integration scene from <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c> sequentially (they share the mod cache and write into <c>mods/</c>, so parallel runs are unsafe), and prints one summary with an aggregate exit code. Replaces the Windows-only PowerShell scripts; requires only the .NET 8 SDK and a Godot .NET executable on PATH, via <c>GODOT_BIN</c> or <c>--godot</c>. </summary>
public static class Program{public static int Main(string[]args){try{Console.OutputEncoding=Encoding.UTF8;}catch{}try{return Run(args);}catch(Exception ex){Console.Error.WriteLine($"test runner error: {ex.Message}");return 2;}}private static int Run(string[]args){var options=RunnerOptions.Parse(args);if(options.ShowHelp){Console.WriteLine(RunnerOptions.HelpText);return 0;}var root=ProjectLocator.Locate();var manifest=TestManifest.Load(Path.Combine(root,"tests","tests.manifest.json"));if(options.List){Console.WriteLine($"{"unit",-14} {manifest.Unit.Project}");foreach(var test in manifest.Integration)Console.WriteLine($"{test.Name,-14} {test.Scene}");return 0;}var godot=GodotLocator.Locate(options.Godot);var results=new List<TestResult>();if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.Switcher)){if(!EnsureBuild(root,options.NoBuild))return 1;results.Add(RunSwitcher(root,godot,options));return SummaryPrinter.Print(results,options.Json);}var selected=manifest.Integration.FirstOrDefault(t=>t.Name==options.Test);var runUnit=string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.Test)||options.Test=="unit";var runAllIntegration=string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.Test);if(!runUnit&&selected==null)throw new InvalidOperationException($"unknown test '{options.Test}'; use --list to see available tests");if((runAllIntegration||selected!=null)&& !EnsureBuild(root,options.NoBuild))return 1;if(runUnit){Console.WriteLine("Unit-тесты...");results.Add(RunUnit(root,manifest));}if(runAllIntegration){foreach(var test in manifest.Integration){Console.WriteLine($"Интеграционный тест: {test.Name}...");results.Add(RunIntegration(root,godot,test));}}else if(selected!=null){Console.WriteLine($"Интеграционный тест: {selected.Name}...");results.Add(RunIntegration(root,godot,selected));}return SummaryPrinter.Print(results,options.Json);}/// <summary> Builds the game assembly so Godot loads the current code (integration scenes and SceneSwitcher both run against it). Returns <c>false</c> after printing the compiler output when the build fails, so the caller can exit before reporting misleading test results. </summary>
private static bool EnsureBuild(string root,bool noBuild){if(noBuild)return true;Console.WriteLine("Сборка Cthangover.csproj...");var build=ProcessRunner.Run("dotnet",new[]{"build","Cthangover.csproj","--nologo","-v","q"},root,300);if(!build.TimedOut&&build.ExitCode==0)return true;Console.Error.WriteLine("Сборка не удалась:");Console.Error.WriteLine(build.OutputText);return false;}/// <summary> Runs the xUnit project through <c>dotnet test</c> with a TRX logger and reads exact counters from the report. TRX avoids parsing the localized console summary. </summary>
private static TestResult RunUnit(string root,TestManifest manifest){var trxDirectory=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"cthangover_trx_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));Directory.CreateDirectory(trxDirectory);try{var args=new List<string>{"test",manifest.Unit.Project,"--nologo","-v","q","--logger","trx;LogFileName=unit.trx","--results-directory",trxDirectory};var run=ProcessRunner.Run("dotnet",args,root,manifest.Unit.TimeoutSeconds);var counters=TrxParser.TryParseCounters(Path.Combine(trxDirectory,"unit.trx"));var result=new TestResult{Name="unit",Kind="unit",ExitCode=run.ExitCode,Duration=run.Duration,TimedOut=run.TimedOut,Passed= !run.TimedOut&&run.ExitCode==0,PassedChecks=counters?.Passed??0,FailedChecks=counters?.Failed??0,LogFile=TestLogs.Save(root,"unit",run),};if(counters?.Failed>0)result.Passed=false;if(counters==null)result.Note="trx counters unavailable";else if(counters.Value.Skipped>0)result.Note=$"skipped={counters.Value.Skipped}";if(!result.Passed)result.Diagnostics=run.Output.Where(l=>l.Contains("[FAIL]",StringComparison.Ordinal)||l.TrimStart().StartsWith("Failed ",StringComparison.Ordinal)).Select(l=>l.Trim()).Take(12).ToList();return result;}finally{try{Directory.Delete(trxDirectory,true);}catch{}}}/// <summary> A plain launch boots only the front-end (main menu + mod manager), so every integration test that does not name a game gets <c>--all-mods</c> after the <c>--</c> separator, keeping the scenes on the full mod set they were written for. </summary>
private static List<string>WithModSet(IReadOnlyList<string>testArgs){var args=new List<string>(testArgs??Array.Empty<string>());if(args.Any(a=>a.StartsWith("--game=",StringComparison.Ordinal)||a=="--all-mods"||a=="--frontend"))return args;if(!args.Contains("--"))args.Add("--");args.Add("--all-mods");return args;}/// <summary> Integration runs are automated: the mod hot reload watcher (on for any run from the editor binary) only costs a recursive watch of every mod root and could reload under a test that writes mod files, so it is switched off unless the test opts in with <c>--hot-reload</c> (HotReloadSettings.EnableFlag). </summary>
private static List<string>WithoutHotReload(List<string>args){if(!args.Contains("--hot-reload"))args.Add("--no-hot-reload");return args;}/// <summary> Runs one integration scene headless and evaluates it from three sources: process exit code, the scene's <c>[TEST_RESULT]</c> line (authoritative for check counts) and the timeout flag. </summary>
private static TestResult RunIntegration(string root,string godot,IntegrationTestDefinition test){// A wrong path (typically wrong case: fine on Windows, broken on Linux/CI and in exported packs) fails here with a precise message instead of a Godot run that ends in "no result marker".
var sceneProblem=ScenePathValidator.Check(root,test.Scene);if(sceneProblem!=null){return new TestResult{Name=test.Name,Kind="integration",Passed=false,ExitCode= -1,FailedChecks=1,Note=sceneProblem,};}var args=new List<string>();if(!test.Windowed)args.Add("--headless");args.Add("--path");args.Add(root);args.Add(test.Scene);args.AddRange(WithoutHotReload(WithModSet(test.Args)));var run=ProcessRunner.Run(godot,args,root,test.TimeoutSeconds);var parsed=ResultParser.Parse(run.Output);var passed= !run.TimedOut&&run.ExitCode==0&&(!parsed.HasResult||parsed.Failed==0);return new TestResult{Name=test.Name,Kind="integration",Passed=passed,TimedOut=run.TimedOut,ExitCode=run.ExitCode,PassedChecks=parsed.HasResult?parsed.Passed:(passed?1:0),FailedChecks=parsed.HasResult?parsed.Failed:(passed?0:1),Duration=run.Duration,Core=parsed.Core,Coverage=parsed.Coverage,Note=run.TimedOut?"timeout":(!parsed.HasResult?"no result marker":null),LogFile=TestLogs.Save(root,test.Name,run),Diagnostics=passed?new List<string>():TestLogs.Highlights(run.Output),};}/// <summary> Smoke run of an arbitrary scene through the native <c>--scene=</c> launch (kernel navigation) plus the <c>test_runner</c> engine mod, which auto-plays the dialog and prints the authoritative <c>[TEST_RESULT]</c>. A <c>.tscn</c> target is passed positionally (Godot loads it natively); a logical scene name goes through user args after <c>--</c>. The driver's flags are always user args — the mod reads <c>OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs()</c>, so they never spuriously activate it during regular integration entries. </summary>
private static TestResult RunSwitcher(string root,string godot,RunnerOptions options){var isTscn=options.Switcher.EndsWith(".tscn",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);var args=new List<string>{"--headless","--path",root};if(isTscn)args.Add(options.Switcher);args.Add("--");if(!isTscn)args.Add($"--scene={options.Switcher}");args.Add($"--timeout={options.TimeoutSeconds}");if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(options.Choices))args.Add($"--choices={options.Choices}");Console.WriteLine($"SceneSwitcher: {options.Switcher}...");var run=ProcessRunner.Run(godot,args,root,options.TimeoutSeconds+60);var parsed=ResultParser.Parse(run.Output);var passed= !run.TimedOut&&run.ExitCode==0&&parsed.HasResult&&parsed.Failed==0;return new TestResult{Name="switcher",Kind="smoke",Passed=passed,TimedOut=run.TimedOut,ExitCode=run.ExitCode,PassedChecks=parsed.HasResult?parsed.Passed:(passed?1:0),FailedChecks=parsed.HasResult?parsed.Failed:(passed?0:1),Duration=run.Duration,LogFile=TestLogs.Save(root,"switcher",run),Diagnostics=passed?new List<string>():TestLogs.Highlights(run.Output),Note=passed?$"scene={options.Switcher}":parsed.HasResult&&parsed.Failed>0?$"driver timeout/fail: scene={options.Switcher}":$"no result: scene={options.Switcher}"};}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ProjectLocator.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Finds the game project root by walking up from the current directory and, as a fallback, from the runner assembly location (covers <c>dotnet run</c> from any subdirectory and published binaries placed elsewhere). The root is identified by <c>project.godot</c> — the same anchor Godot itself uses via <c>--path</c>. </summary>
public static class ProjectLocator{public static string Locate(){return FindFrom(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory())??FindFrom(AppContext.BaseDirectory)??throw new InvalidOperationException("project.godot not found: run the test runner from inside the CThangover repository");}private static string FindFrom(string start){var directory=new DirectoryInfo(start);while(directory!=null){if(File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName,"project.godot")))return directory.FullName;directory=directory.Parent;}return null;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ResultParser.cs =====
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Parses the machine-readable markers every integration scene prints at the end of its run. The contract is deliberately tiny and stable: <c>[TEST_RESULT] name=... [core=...] passed=N failed=N</c> plus the battle coverage summary <c>[BATTLE_COVERAGE] coverage: N/M cores</c>. Exit code alone is not enough: a scene can exit 0 after a crash-free but failing run, and timeouts must be distinguishable from failures. </summary>
public static class ResultParser{private static readonly Regex ResultRegex=new(@"\[TEST_RESULT\]\s+name=(\S+)(?:\s+core=(\S+))?\s+passed=(\d+)\s+failed=(\d+)",RegexOptions.Compiled);private static readonly Regex CoverageRegex=new(@"\[BATTLE_COVERAGE\]\s+coverage:\s+(\d+)/(\d+)\s+cores",RegexOptions.Compiled);public static ParsedSceneResult Parse(IEnumerable<string>lines){var result=new ParsedSceneResult();foreach(var line in lines){var match=ResultRegex.Match(line);if(match.Success){result.HasResult=true;result.Name=match.Groups[1].Value;if(match.Groups[2].Success)result.Core=match.Groups[2].Value;result.Passed=int.Parse(match.Groups[3].Value);result.Failed=int.Parse(match.Groups[4].Value);continue;}var coverage=CoverageRegex.Match(line);if(coverage.Success)result.Coverage=$"{coverage.Groups[1].Value}/{coverage.Groups[2].Value}";}return result;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/RunnerOptions.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Parsed command-line options of the global test runner. Kept as a plain class with a hand-written parser: the runner must build and run with nothing but the .NET 8 SDK, so no CLI framework dependency is added. </summary>
public class RunnerOptions{/// <summary>Run only this test (unit or an integration name from the manifest).</summary>
public string Test{get;private set;}/// <summary>Print available tests and exit.</summary>
public bool List{get;private set;}/// <summary>Explicit path to the Godot executable; falls back to GODOT_BIN or PATH.</summary>
public string Godot{get;private set;}/// <summary>Skip the game assembly build before integration tests.</summary>
public bool NoBuild{get;private set;}/// <summary>Write a machine-readable JSON report to this path.</summary>
public string Json{get;private set;}/// <summary>Run SceneSwitcher against this scene path or logical scene name.</summary>
public string Switcher{get;private set;}/// <summary>Comma-separated dialog choice indexes for --switcher.</summary>
public string Choices{get;private set;}/// <summary>SceneSwitcher timeout in seconds.</summary>
public int TimeoutSeconds{get;private set;}=60;/// <summary>Print usage and exit.</summary>
public bool ShowHelp{get;private set;}public static RunnerOptions Parse(string[]args){var options=new RunnerOptions();for(var i=0;i<args.Length;i++){switch(args[i]){case"--test":options.Test=Next(args,ref i);break;case"--list":options.List=true;break;case"--godot":options.Godot=Next(args,ref i);break;case"--no-build":options.NoBuild=true;break;case"--json":options.Json=Next(args,ref i);break;case"--switcher":options.Switcher=Next(args,ref i);break;case"--choices":options.Choices=Next(args,ref i);break;case"--timeout":if(!int.TryParse(Next(args,ref i),out var timeout)||timeout<=0)throw new ArgumentException("--timeout expects a positive number of seconds");options.TimeoutSeconds=timeout;break;case"--help":case"-h":options.ShowHelp=true;break;default:throw new ArgumentException($"unknown option '{args[i]}'");}}return options;}private static string Next(string[]args,ref int i){if(i+1>=args.Length)throw new ArgumentException($"option '{args[i]}' requires a value");return args[++i];}public static string HelpText=>@"CThangover — глобальный раннер тестов (кроссплатформенный, .NET 8 + Godot .NET).

Использование:
  dotnet run --project tests/Cthangover.TestRunner -- [опции]

Без опций: сборка игры → unit-тесты → все интеграционные сцены из
tests/tests.manifest.json → сводка. Код выхода 0 только если всё зелёное.

Опции:
  --test <name>        выполнить только один тест: unit или имя из манифеста
                       (mod, actioncontext, tscn, scene_builder, coverage,
                        battle, battle_ff, menu)
  --list               показать список тестов
  --godot <path>       путь к Godot (по умолчанию GODOT_BIN или godot из PATH)
  --no-build           не собирать Cthangover.csproj перед интеграционными тестами
  --json <path>        записать машинный отчёт (JSON)
  --switcher <target>  smoke-прогон сцены через SceneSwitcher (путь .tscn или
                       логическое имя), с авто-диалогами
  --choices <0,1,...>  индексы выборов для --switcher
  --timeout <sec>      таймаут SceneSwitcher (по умолчанию 60)
  --help               эта справка

Примеры:
  dotnet run --project tests/Cthangover.TestRunner
  dotnet run --project tests/Cthangover.TestRunner -- --test battle_ff
  dotnet run --project tests/Cthangover.TestRunner -- --switcher home_kitchen --choices 0 --timeout 30
  dotnet run --project tests/Cthangover.TestRunner -- --json test-results.json";}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/ScenePathValidator.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Checks a manifest <c>res://</c> scene path against the file system with exact casing before Godot is started. Windows resolves a wrong-case path silently, Linux/CI and exported packs do not — there the scene simply fails to open and the run ends with "no result marker". Validating up front turns that into a precise message on every OS. </summary>
public static class ScenePathValidator{/// <summary> Returns null when <paramref name="resPath"/> exists under <paramref name="root"/> with exactly this casing; otherwise a human-readable problem, naming the on-disk spelling when only the case differs. </summary>
public static string Check(string root,string resPath){if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resPath))return"scene path is empty";if(!resPath.StartsWith("res://",StringComparison.Ordinal))return null;// uid:// and absolute paths are left to Godot
var current=root;var segments=resPath.Substring("res://".Length).Split('/',StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);for(var i=0;i<segments.Length;i++){var segment=segments[i];var isLast=i==segments.Length-1;var entries=isLast?Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(current):Directory.EnumerateDirectories(current);string exact=null,caseOnly=null;foreach(var entry in entries){var name=Path.GetFileName(entry);if(string.Equals(name,segment,StringComparison.Ordinal)){exact=name;break;}if(string.Equals(name,segment,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))caseOnly=name;}if(exact==null){return caseOnly!=null?$"scene path case mismatch: '{segment}' is '{caseOnly}' on disk ({resPath})":$"scene not found: {resPath}";}current=Path.Combine(current,exact);}return null;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/SummaryPrinter.cs =====
using System.Text.Json;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Prints the single human-readable summary of the whole run and, when requested, writes the same data as JSON for machine consumption. Returns the aggregate exit code (0 only when every entry passed), which makes the runner usable from any CI or pre-commit hook without parsing console text. </summary>
public static class SummaryPrinter{public static int Print(IReadOnlyList<TestResult>results,string jsonPath){var allPassed=results.Count>0&&results.All(r=>r.Passed);Console.WriteLine();Console.WriteLine("=== ИТОГИ ТЕСТИРОВАНИЯ ===");foreach(var result in results){var status=result.Passed?"PASS":result.TimedOut?"TIMEOUT":"FAIL";var checks=result.PassedChecks+result.FailedChecks>0?$"{result.PassedChecks}/{result.PassedChecks + result.FailedChecks}":"-";var details=BuildDetails(result);var line=$"  {result.Name,-14} {status,-8} {checks,-10} {result.Duration.TotalSeconds,6:F1}s  {details}";WriteStatus(line,result.Passed);if(!result.Passed){foreach(var diagnostic in result.Diagnostics)Console.WriteLine($"      | {diagnostic}");if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(result.LogFile))Console.WriteLine($"      лог: {result.LogFile}");}}Console.WriteLine();WriteStatus($"Успешно: {results.Count(r => r.Passed)}/{results.Count}",allPassed);WriteStatus(allPassed?"ВСЁ УСПЕШНО":"ЕСТЬ ПАДЕНИЯ",allPassed);if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(jsonPath))WriteJson(jsonPath,results,allPassed);return allPassed?0:1;}private static string BuildDetails(TestResult result){var parts=new List<string>();if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(result.Core))parts.Add($"core={result.Core}");if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(result.Coverage))parts.Add($"coverage={result.Coverage}");if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(result.Note))parts.Add(result.Note);if(!result.Passed&& !result.TimedOut)parts.Add($"exit={result.ExitCode}");return string.Join("  ",parts);}private static void WriteStatus(string text,bool ok){if(Console.IsOutputRedirected){Console.WriteLine(text);return;}var previous=Console.ForegroundColor;Console.ForegroundColor=ok?ConsoleColor.Green:ConsoleColor.Red;Console.WriteLine(text);Console.ForegroundColor=previous;}private static void WriteJson(string path,IReadOnlyList<TestResult>results,bool allPassed){var report=new{success=allPassed,finishedAt=DateTimeOffset.Now,totalSeconds=Math.Round(results.Sum(r=>r.Duration.TotalSeconds),2),results=results.Select(r=>new{name=r.Name,kind=r.Kind,status=r.Passed?"pass":r.TimedOut?"timeout":"fail",passed=r.PassedChecks,failed=r.FailedChecks,exitCode=r.ExitCode,durationSeconds=Math.Round(r.Duration.TotalSeconds,2),core=r.Core,coverage=r.Coverage,note=r.Note,logFile=r.LogFile,diagnostics=r.Diagnostics})};var json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(report,new JsonSerializerOptions{WriteIndented=true});File.WriteAllText(path,json);Console.WriteLine($"Отчёт записан: {path}");}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/TestLogs.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Keeps the full process output of every test for post-mortem debugging and extracts the lines worth showing in the summary. Without it a failed scene leaves only "no result marker" behind: the Godot output that explains the failure (missing scene, mod compile error, exception) is gone with the process. </summary>
public static class TestLogs{private const int MaxHighlights=12;private const int TailLines=8;/// <summary>Directory the logs land in: <c>tests/artifacts/logs/</c> (ignored by git via <c>*.log</c>).</summary>
public static string Directory(string root)=>Path.Combine(root,"tests","artifacts","logs");/// <summary> Writes the output to <c>tests/artifacts/logs/&lt;name&gt;.log</c>, overwriting the previous run. Returns the path, or null when the write failed (logging must never fail the test run). </summary>
public static string Save(string root,string name,ProcessResult run){try{var dir=Directory(root);System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);var path=Path.Combine(dir,name+".log");File.WriteAllLines(path,run.Output);return path;}catch{return null;}}/// <summary> Lines that explain a failure: engine/script errors, exceptions, mod compile errors and failed checks, capped to a few lines. Falls back to the tail of the output when nothing matched (a hang or a silent exit still shows where the process stopped). </summary>
public static List<string>Highlights(IReadOnlyList<string>output){var hits=output.Where(IsDiagnostic).Select(StripAnsi).Distinct().Take(MaxHighlights).ToList();if(hits.Count>0)return hits;return output.Skip(Math.Max(0,output.Count-TailLines)).Select(StripAnsi).ToList();}private static bool IsDiagnostic(string line){if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))return false;// Engine shutdown noise is reported on every exit and explains nothing.
if(line.Contains("Unreferenced static string")||line.Contains("leaked at exit")||line.Contains("Pages in use exist at exit"))return false;// GameLogger marks LogLevel.Error with red ANSI on non-Windows consoles.
if(line.Contains("\x1b[31m[",StringComparison.Ordinal))return true;line=StripAnsi(line);return line.StartsWith("ERROR:",StringComparison.Ordinal)||line.StartsWith("SCRIPT ERROR:",StringComparison.Ordinal)||line.Contains("Exception:",StringComparison.Ordinal)||line.Contains("[ERROR]",StringComparison.Ordinal)||line.Contains("[FAIL]",StringComparison.Ordinal)||line.Contains("[TIMEOUT]",StringComparison.Ordinal)||(line.Contains("[MODS_COMPILE]",StringComparison.Ordinal)&&(line.Contains("FAILED")||line.Contains(": error CS")))||line.Contains("Cannot open file",StringComparison.Ordinal);}private static string StripAnsi(string line)=>System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(line,@"\x1b\[[0-9;]*m","");}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/TestManifest.cs =====
using System.Text.Json;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Deserialized <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c> — the single source of truth for which tests the global runner executes and in what order. Previously the same list was duplicated across PowerShell scripts and the coverage map, which drifted; now scripts and code read this file. </summary>
public class TestManifest{public UnitManifest Unit{get;set;}=new();public List<IntegrationTestDefinition>Integration{get;set;}=new();public static TestManifest Load(string path){if(!File.Exists(path))throw new FileNotFoundException($"test manifest not found: {path}");var json=File.ReadAllText(path);var manifest=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<TestManifest>(json,new JsonSerializerOptions{PropertyNameCaseInsensitive=true});if(manifest==null)throw new InvalidOperationException($"test manifest is empty or invalid: {path}");return manifest;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/TestResult.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Aggregated result of one suite entry (unit or a single integration scene) shown in the final summary and serialized to JSON. Check counts come from TRX for xUnit and from <c>[TEST_RESULT]</c> for scenes; <see cref="ExitCode"/>, <see cref="TimedOut"/> and <see cref="Note"/> keep the diagnostic context when a run fails. </summary>
public class TestResult{public string Name{get;set;}public string Kind{get;set;}public bool Passed{get;set;}public bool TimedOut{get;set;}public int ExitCode{get;set;}public int PassedChecks{get;set;}public int FailedChecks{get;set;}public TimeSpan Duration{get;set;}public string Core{get;set;}public string Coverage{get;set;}public string Note{get;set;}/// <summary>Full process output of this test (<c>tests/artifacts/logs/&lt;name&gt;.log</c>), null when not saved.</summary>
public string LogFile{get;set;}/// <summary>Error lines (or the output tail) shown under a failed test in the summary.</summary>

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/TrxParser.cs =====
using System.Xml.Linq;namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Reads xUnit counters from a Visual Studio TRX report produced by <c>dotnet test --logger trx</c>. TRX is used instead of parsing console output because the CLI summary text is localized (the project runs on Russian Windows and would break English-only parsers). </summary>
public static class TrxParser{public static(int Passed,int Failed,int Skipped)?TryParseCounters(string trxPath){if(!File.Exists(trxPath))return null;var document=XDocument.Load(trxPath);var counters=document.Descendants().FirstOrDefault(e=>e.Name.LocalName=="Counters");if(counters==null)return null;return(Read(counters,"passed"),Read(counters,"failed"),Read(counters,"skipped"));}private static int Read(XElement element,string attribute){return int.TryParse(element.Attribute(attribute)?.Value,out var value)?value:0;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.TestRunner/UnitManifest.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.TestRunner{/// <summary> Unit-test section of <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c>: the xUnit project to run via <c>dotnet test</c> and its timeout. </summary>
public class UnitManifest{public string Project{get;set;}public int TimeoutSeconds{get;set;}=300;}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/AudioCoverageTestSetup.cs =====

namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Audio;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage of the audio subsystem migrated into <c>audio_base</c>: the engine mod — not the core — provides the audio service and the music player at their well-known <c>/root</c> paths, and the service plays sounds and ambience from mod files without throwing. </summary>

namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Characters;using Cthangover.Core.Factories;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the characters subsystem migrated into the characters_base engine mod. ===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/DialogAutoDriver.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.UI.Dialog;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Auto-plays dialog scenes for integration tests and the AI workflow. Attached by <c>SceneSwitcher</c> and <c>MainMenuTestSetup</c>, it clicks <c>NextAction</c> at a fixed interval and answers selection prompts from <c>--choices=</c> (first variant when unspecified). The DialogBox is discovered lazily through the core <see cref="IDialogBox"/> contract, so the driver never references the dialog_base mod assembly; end-of-dialog quits the process, optionally switching to <c>--scene2=</c> first. </summary>
public partial class DialogAutoDriver:Node{[Export]public float AdvanceInterval{get;set;}=0.1f;[Export]public bool AutoQuit{get;set;}=true;/// <summary> When set, a successful end of dialog emits <c>[TEST_RESULT] name=&lt;ResultName&gt; passed=1 failed=0</c> before quitting, so the global test runner can attribute the result to the calling test. Left empty by SceneSwitcher, whose smoke runs are evaluated by log markers instead. </summary>
public string ResultName{get;set;}/// <summary> How long the driver tolerates a discovered DialogBox staying idle (<see cref="IDialogBox.IsDialogEnded"/>) before treating it as an empty scene and quitting. Covers the gap between scene load and the first scenario queue. </summary>
private const ulong FirstDialogGraceMs=3000;private IDialogBox _dialogBox;private float _timer;private ulong _dialogFoundAtMs;private List<int>_choices=new();private int _choiceIndex;private string _sceneOnEnd;private bool _hasSeenRunningDialog;public override void _Ready(){ParseCmdArgs();}/// <summary> Looks the DialogBox up lazily through the tree root instead of the cached <c>SceneContextNode.FindNode</c>: the driver is often added before the dialog scene exists, and the context cache would poison the name as not-found until the next scene-exit event. The cast to the core IDialogBox contract keeps this file free of mod types. </summary>
private IDialogBox FindDialogBox(){var tree=GetTree();if(tree?.Root==null)return null;return tree.Root.FindChild("DialogBox",true,false)as IDialogBox;}private void ParseCmdArgs(){var args=OS.GetCmdlineArgs();foreach(var arg in args){if(arg.StartsWith("--choices=")){var val=arg.Substring("--choices=".Length);_choices=val.Split(',').Select(s=>int.TryParse(s.Trim(),out var n)?n:-1).Where(n=>n>=0).ToList();break;}if(arg.StartsWith("--scene2="))_sceneOnEnd=arg.Substring("--scene2=".Length).Trim();}}public override void _Process(double delta){if(_dialogBox is not GodotObject liveBox|| !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(liveBox)||liveBox is not Node liveNode|| !liveNode.IsInsideTree()){_dialogBox=FindDialogBox();if(_dialogBox==null)return;_dialogFoundAtMs=Time.GetTicksMsec();_hasSeenRunningDialog=false;var choicesInfo=_choices.Count>0?"choices=["+string.Join(",",_choices)+"]":"no choices, will pick first variant each time";GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"DialogAutoDriver started, interval={AdvanceInterval}, {choicesInfo}");}_timer+=(float)delta;if(_timer<AdvanceInterval)return;_timer=0;if(_dialogBox.IsRunning)_hasSeenRunningDialog=true;// IsDialogEnded is also true while the very first queue has not been loaded yet, so end-handling waits until a dialog was actually observed running. In --scene2 mode the wait is indefinite (the second entry legitimately starts no dialog); otherwise a short grace period lets dialog-less scenes finish.
var waitingForFirstDialog= !_hasSeenRunningDialog&&(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_sceneOnEnd)||Time.GetTicksMsec()-_dialogFoundAtMs<FirstDialogGraceMs);if(_dialogBox.IsDialogEnded&& !waitingForFirstDialog){if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_sceneOnEnd)){var next=_sceneOnEnd;_sceneOnEnd=null;GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"DialogAutoDriver: dialog ended, switching to '{next}'");SceneContextNode.FindNode<SceneManager>("SceneManager")?.SwitchScene(next);return;}if(AutoQuit){if(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(ResultName))GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name={ResultName} passed=1 failed=0");GameLogger.Log("TEST","DialogAutoDriver: dialog ended, quitting");GetTree().Quit();}SetProcess(false);return;}if(_dialogBox.IsWaitingAnswer){HandleSelection();return;}_dialogBox.NextAction();}private void HandleSelection(){var count=_dialogBox.AnswerVariantCount;if(count<=0)return;var pick=0;if(_choiceIndex<_choices.Count){pick=_choices[_choiceIndex];if(pick>=count)pick=0;_choiceIndex++;}GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"DialogAutoDriver: choice #{_choiceIndex} picking index={pick} out of {count}",LogLevel.Debug);_dialogBox.TrySelectVariant(pick);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/EntityPipelineTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Factories;using Cthangover.Core.Items;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Resolvers;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Integration test for the kind-routed entity pipeline. It locks the core invariant of the <c>EntityResolver</c>: a file is collected only under the DTO whose <c>[EntityKind]</c> matches the file's declared <c>"Kind"</c>, no matter which directory it lives in. The old directory-typed pipeline fed characters, enemies, actions and skills through the same <c>characters/</c> folder, and each factory absorbed its neighbours' definitions; these assertions fail loudly if that cross-type bleed ever returns. <para/> The test also covers DTOs that live in Roslyn-compiled mod assemblies: the <c>status_effect</c> kind must be visible to the registry after mod loading, otherwise the scan would reject the file as unknown. </summary>
public partial class EntityPipelineTestSetup:Node{private int _passed;private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){GameLogger.Log("ENTITY_TEST","EntityPipelineTestSetup: starting entity pipeline tests");// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready.
ModInitializerRegistry.NotifyResourcesReady();TestCharacterKind();TestActionKind();TestSkillKind();TestItemAndRecipeKinds();TestQuestKind();TestModAssemblyKind();GameLogger.Log("ENTITY_TEST",$"EntityPipelineTestSetup: {_passed} passed, {_failed} failed");GameLogger.Log("ENTITY_TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name=entities passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}private void TestCharacterKind(){// The character DTO lives in the characters engine mod, so the kind-to-DTO routing itself is part of the assertion: the kind must resolve to a Roslyn-compiled mod assembly type and still collect exactly the character files.
var characters=CollectByKind("character");AssertTrue(characters!=null&&characters.Contains("Marao"),"player character resolves through the 'character' kind");AssertTrue(characters!=null&&characters.Contains("wolf_1"),"enemy resolves through the same 'character' kind");AssertTrue(characters!=null&& !characters.Contains("slave_master"),"skill is not collected as a character");AssertTrue(characters!=null&& !characters.Contains("physics/attack"),"action is not collected as a character");}private void TestActionKind(){var actions=CollectByKind("action");AssertTrue(actions!=null&&actions.Contains("physics/attack"),"action resolves through the 'action' kind");AssertTrue(actions!=null&& !actions.Contains("Marao"),"character is not collected as an action");AssertTrue(actions!=null&& !actions.Contains("slave_master"),"skill is not collected as an action");}/// <summary> Collects every entity of the kind's DTO through reflection: the DTO type may come from a Roslyn-compiled mod assembly that this compile-time-referenced test code cannot name directly. </summary>
private System.Collections.IDictionary CollectByKind(string kind){if(!EntityKindRegistry.TryResolve(kind,out var dtoType)){AssertTrue(false,$"kind '{kind}' resolves to a DTO type");return null;}var resolver=ModManager.Instance.GetResolver<EntityResolver>();var collect=typeof(EntityResolver).GetMethods().First(m=>m.Name==nameof(Entities.Collect)&&m.IsGenericMethodDefinition&&m.GetParameters().Length==0).MakeGenericMethod(dtoType);return(System.Collections.IDictionary)collect.Invoke(resolver,null);}private void TestSkillKind(){// The skill DTO lives in the skills_base engine mod, so the kind routing is asserted through the same reflection path as the character/item/recipe kinds above.
var skills=CollectByKind("skill");AssertTrue(skills!=null&&skills.Contains("slave_master"),"skill resolves through the 'skill' kind");AssertTrue(skills==null|| !skills.Contains("Marao"),"character is not collected as a skill");AssertTrue(skills==null|| !skills.Contains("wolf_1"),"enemy is not collected as a skill");}private void TestItemAndRecipeKinds(){// The item/recipe DTOs live in the items engine mod, so the kind routing is asserted through the same reflection path as the character kind above.
var items=CollectByKind("item");var recipes=CollectByKind("recipe");AssertTrue(items!=null&&items.Contains("food/ration"),"item resolves through the 'item' kind");AssertTrue(items!=null&&items.Contains("recipe/wolf_meat_to_ration"),"recipe item is an item");AssertTrue(recipes!=null&&recipes.Contains("recipe/wolf_meat_to_ration"),"recipe resolves through the 'recipe' kind");AssertTrue(items!=null&& !items.Contains("AwakeningQuest"),"quest is not collected as an item");}private void TestQuestKind(){// The quest DTO lives in the quests engine mod, so the kind routing is asserted through the same reflection path as the character kind above.
var quests=CollectByKind("quest");AssertTrue(quests!=null&&quests.Contains("AwakeningQuest"),"quest resolves through the 'quest' kind");AssertTrue(quests!=null&&quests.Contains("MeetingMurakamiQuest"),"quest from another mod is collected too");}private void TestModAssemblyKind(){AssertTrue(EntityKindRegistry.IsKnown("status_effect"),"status_effect kind from a Roslyn-compiled mod assembly is registered");var statusText=ModManager.Instance.ReadFileText("turn_battle_base","status_effects/status_effects.json");var peeked=EntityKindPeek.TryRead(statusText,out var kind);AssertTrue(peeked&&kind=="status_effect","status effect file self-describes as 'status_effect'");}private void AssertTrue(bool condition,string label){if(condition){_passed++;GameLogger.Log("ENTITY_TEST",$"  [PASS] {label}");}else{_failed++;GameLogger.Log("ENTITY_TEST",$"  [FAIL] {label}",LogLevel.Error);}}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/GDActionContextCoreTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Actions;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Quests;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{public namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Interactive;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the interactive subsystem migrated into the interactive_base engine mod. The DialogRunner node is parked with CallDeferred though — notify the registry (idempotent) and give the deferred add a frame to flush before the checks below resolve it.

assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Core.Factories.Impls.InteractiveFactory",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("migrated type InteractiveObject is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Core.Interactive.InteractiveObject",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("core assembly no longer defines InteractiveManager",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Interactive.InteractiveManager",throwOnError:false)==null);Check("core assembly no longer defines InteractiveFactory",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Factories.Impls.InteractiveFactory",throwOnError:false)==null);var namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the items subsystem migrated into the items_base engine mod. The core keeps only the contracts (<c>IInventory</c>, <c>IRecipeData</c>, the item interfaces plus the GD wrappers and <c>ItemActionBridge</c>); the factories, the concrete inventory/recipe state and the inventory UI live in the mod assembly, so every check here proves the core-to-mod wiring at engine runtime: <list type="bullet"> <item>the mod initializer installed the <c>RuntimeData</c> factory hooks, so the state bag serves a mod-owned inventory and recipe state;</item> <item>an id-string add resolves the item through the mod's <c>ItemFactory</c> and removes again through the core contract;</item> <item>migrated types (ItemFactory/ItemActionFactory) are loaded from a mod assembly, and the core assembly no longer defines them;</item> <item>a foreign mod's <c>IItemAction</c> (cooking) reached the items factory through the mod's <c>ItemActionConsumer</c>.</item> </list> The machine-readable line is parsed by <c>tests/Cthangover.TestRunner</c>. </summary>
public partial class ItemsCoverageTestSetup:Node{/// <summary>Log category for all lines of this scene.</summary>
private const string Category="ITEMS_COVERAGE";private int _passed;private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready, so ItemsModInitializer.OnModResourcesReady has already installed the factory hooks and bound the action bridge.
var kernelAssembly=typeof(GameData).Assembly;var inventory=SafeGet(()=>GameData.Instance.Runtime.Inventory);Check("RuntimeData.Inventory is produced by the items mod factory hook",inventory!=null);Check("inventory implementation comes from a mod assembly, not the core",inventory!=null&&inventory.GetType().Assembly!=kernelAssembly);var recipes=SafeGet(()=>GameData.Instance.Runtime.RecipeData);Check("RuntimeData.RecipeData is produced by the items mod factory hook",recipes!=null);Check("recipe state implementation comes from a mod assembly, not the core",recipes!=null&&recipes.GetType().Assembly!=kernelAssembly);Check("recipe state answers Has through the core contract",recipes!=null&& !recipes.Has("recipe/never_unlocked"));// The id-string path resolves the item through the mod's ItemFactory; the mutation is throwaway scene-local state.
SafeRun(()=>inventory.Add("food/ration",2));Check("id-string add resolves the item through the mod ItemFactory",SafeInt(()=>inventory.CheckCount("food/ration"))==2);Check("remove through the core contract drops the stack",SafeBool(()=>inventory.Remove("food/ration",2)));// The migrated sources keep their core namespace, but the types must be loaded from mod assemblies compiled at runtime, not from the core.
Check("migrated type ItemFactory is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Core.Factories.Impls.ItemFactory",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("migrated type Inventory is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Core.Items.Inventory",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("core assembly no longer defines ItemFactory",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Factories.Impls.ItemFactory",throwOnError:false)==null);Check("core assembly no longer defines Inventory",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Items.Inventory",throwOnError:false)==null);// A foreign mod's IItemAction (cooking) reaches the items factory through the items mod's ItemActionConsumer.
Check("cooking mod's IItemAction was delivered through ItemActionConsumer",SafeGet(()=>ResolveAction("action/recipe/wolf_meat_to_ration"))!=null);// ─── Inventory panel scene contract (tscn migration) ─── The panel layout lives in inventory_panel.tscn; the behaviour resolves its nodes with hard GetNode paths in OnceConstruct.
CallDeferred(nameof(RunPanelSceneCheck));}private void RunPanelSceneCheck(){var ok=false;var error="";try{var panel=new ModScenePrefab("scenes/ui/inventory_panel.tscn").Instantiate<Control>();AddChild(panel);// Widget.Show is hidden behind the Control reference — invoke it on the actual mod type so the widget lifecycle runs.
panel.GetType().GetMethod("Show",Type.EmptyTypes)?.Invoke(panel,null);var hasGrid=panel.GetNodeOrNull("Panel/Margin/Root/Body/Left/GridScroll/GridContent")!=null;var passport=panel.GetNodeOrNull<VBoxContainer>("Panel/Margin/Root/Body/Right/PassportScroll/Passport");var passportBuilt=passport!=null&&passport.GetChildCount()>0;var tabsBuilt=panel.GetNode<HBoxContainer>("Panel/Margin/Root/Header/Tabs").GetChildCount()>0;panel.GetType().GetMethod("Hide",Type.EmptyTypes)?.Invoke(panel,null);panel.QueueFree();error=$"grid={hasGrid} passportBuilt={passportBuilt} tabsBuilt={tabsBuilt}";ok=hasGrid&&passportBuilt&&tabsBuilt;}catch(Exception e){error=e.Message;}Check("inventory panel scene instantiates and builds: "+error,ok);GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=coverage_items passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}/// <summary>Resolves a registered item action through the mod-owned ItemActionFactory singleton (reflection only).</summary>

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/Live2DAvatarTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Bootstrap интеграционного теста Live2D-аватара. Сам аватар управляется сценой тестового набора live2d_base__avatar (набор мода live2d_base, монтируется флагом --itest=live2d_avatar; единственное место с компайл-тайм доступом к Live2D-обёртке), поэтому эта сцена только проверяет загрузку набора, инстанцирует мод-сцену через рантайм tscn-пайплайн и передаёт управление драйверу: он печатает [TEST_RESULT] и завершает дерево с кодом выхода. </summary>
public partial class Live2DAvatarTestSetup:Node{public override void _Ready(){GameLogger.Log("TEST","Live2DAvatarTestSetup: starting");if(ModManager.Instance?.GetMod("live2d_base__avatar")==null){TryHealDisabledMod();FailBootstrap("test suite 'live2d_base__avatar' is not mounted (needs --itest=live2d_avatar)");return;}var driver=TscnScenes.LoadAndBuild("scenes/live2d_avatar_test.tscn");if(driver==null){FailBootstrap("mod scene 'scenes/live2d_avatar_test.tscn' did not build");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST","Live2DAvatarTestSetup: driver instantiated, handing control over");AddChild(driver);}/// <summary> Мод live2d_base (хозяин набора live2d_base__avatar) может быть выключен в user://disabled_mods.json (например, после ручной проверки тумблера в UI модов). Загрузка модов к этому моменту уже завершена, поэтому текущий прогон падает, но перед выходом тест включает моды обратно — следующий запуск будет зелёным. </summary>
private void TryHealDisabledMod(){var disabled=DisabledModsStore.Load();var healed=false;foreach(var id in new[]{"live2d_base"}){if(!disabled.Contains(id))continue;disabled.Remove(id);healed=true;GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"  [HEAL] mod '{id}' was disabled, enabling it — rerun the test to apply");}if(healed)DisabledModsStore.Save(disabled);}private void FailBootstrap(string reason){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"  [FAIL] {reason}",LogLevel.Error);GameLogger.Log("TEST","[TEST_RESULT] name=live2d_avatar passed=0 failed=1");GetTree().Quit(1);}}}
