===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/MainMenuTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Automated smoke runs of the main menu. Three modes share the same setup: <list type="bullet"> <item><c>--test=menu</c> — waits for the menu to settle, clicks "New Game", then attaches a <see cref="DialogAutoDriver"/> to the tree root so it survives the scene change and auto-advances the opening scenario until it ends (exit code 0).</item> <item><c>--test=mod_manager</c> — clicks the single hub button from the mod <c>main_menu</c> registry, then verifies the hub opened on the mods tab with the embedded manager: its grid exists, the tiles have one size and every status label shows its whole text; a tile opens the mod's card, the hub's back button returns from it to the grid (not to the menu) and only a second press leaves the hub (exit code 0). <c>--shots=&lt;dir&gt;</c> saves the grid and the card (windowed runs only).</item> <item><c>--test=mod_collections</c> — opens the same hub, switches to the collections tab, verifies the games gallery, opens the scheme editor from the first game card and checks the game picker is populated and the canvas contains at least one group card and one mod chip (exit code 0).</item> </list> A watchdog quits with exit code 1 if the flow does not finish within <c>--timeout=</c> seconds (default 120), so a run can never hang. The hub hosts the manager/gallery/editor screens, so every check below addresses them through type-name searches inside <c>ModHubScreen</c> — the test assembly cannot reference mod types directly. </summary>
public partial class MainMenuTestSetup:Node{private const float ClickDelaySeconds=0.5f;private const float DriverAttachDelaySeconds=1.0f;private const float ModManagerSettleSeconds=1.0f;private const float DefaultTimeoutSeconds=120f;private Node _menu;private float _timer=ClickDelaySeconds;private float _timeoutSeconds=DefaultTimeoutSeconds;private string _testName;private string _shotsDir;private Node _screen;private bool _enabled;private bool _started;public override void _Ready(){foreach(var arg in OS.GetCmdlineArgs()){if(arg.StartsWith("--test=")){var testName=arg.Substring("--test=".Length).ToLower();if(testName=="menu"||testName=="mod_manager"||testName=="mod_collections"){_testName=testName;_enabled=true;GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: auto-test enabled via --test={testName}");}}else if(arg.StartsWith("--shots=")){_shotsDir=arg.Substring("--shots=".Length);}else if(arg.StartsWith("--timeout=")&&float.TryParse(arg.Substring("--timeout=".Length),out var t)&&t>0){_timeoutSeconds=t;}}}public override void _Process(double delta){if(!_enabled||_started)return;if(_menu==null)BuildMenu();_timer-=(float)delta;if(_timer>0)return;SetProcess(false);_started=true;if(_testName=="mod_manager")RunModManagerTest();else if(_testName=="mod_collections")RunModCollectionsTest();else RunNewGameTest();}/// <summary> Builds the main menu from the menu_base mod at runtime. The old native res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn no longer exists, so the test scene cannot instance it statically; the mod-resolved scene is attached as a child of this setup node and all lookups go through <c>_menu</c>. </summary>
private void BuildMenu(){_menu=TscnScenes.LoadAndBuild("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn");if(_menu==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: failed to build the main menu scene from mods",LogLevel.Error);Fail("menu");return;}_menu.Name="MainMenuScene";AddChild(_menu);GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: main menu scene built from mods");}private void RunNewGameTest(){var newGameBtn=_menu?.GetNodeOrNull<Button>("MenuContainer/GameButtons/new_game");if(newGameBtn==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: new_game button not found, quitting",LogLevel.Error);Fail("menu");return;}var tree=GetTree();tree.CreateTimer(DriverAttachDelaySeconds).Timeout+=()=>{var driver=new DialogAutoDriver{ResultName="menu"};tree.Root.AddChild(driver);};tree.CreateTimer(_timeoutSeconds).Timeout+=()=>{GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: timeout ({_timeoutSeconds}s) reached without dialog end, quitting",LogLevel.Error);Fail("menu");};GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: clicking 'New Game' button");newGameBtn.EmitSignal("pressed");}/// <summary> Clicks the single hub button (the only <c>main_menu</c> entry since the mods and collections screens merged into one hub). </summary>
private Button FindHubButton(){var container=_menu?.GetNodeOrNull<Control>("MenuContainer/ModMenuButtons");return container!=null&&container.GetChildCount()>0?container.GetChild(0)as Button:null;}private Node FindHub()=>_menu?.GetNodeOrNull<Node>("ModHubScreen");private void RunModManagerTest(){var button=FindHubButton();if(button==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: no mod main-menu buttons found, quitting",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: clicking mods & collections hub button");button.EmitSignal("pressed");GetTree().CreateTimer(ModManagerSettleSeconds).Timeout+=VerifyModManagerScreen;GetTree().CreateTimer(_timeoutSeconds).Timeout+=()=>{GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: timeout ({_timeoutSeconds}s) reached in mod_manager test, quitting",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");};}private void VerifyModManagerScreen(){if(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this))return;// The hub opens on the mods tab with the manager embedded inside.
var hub=FindHub();var screen=hub!=null?FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(hub,"ModManagerScreen"):null;if(screen==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","ModManagerScreen not found inside the hub after button click",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}var grid=FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(screen,"ModGridView");var cards=CountDescendantsByTypeName(screen,"ModCard");if(grid==null||cards==0){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: mod manager built incompletely (grid={grid != null}, cards={cards})",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: mod manager OK, {cards} card(s)");GetTree().CreateTimer(ModManagerSettleSeconds).Timeout+=()=>VerifyCardSizes(screen);}/// <summary> All tiles must render at exactly the same size regardless of whether a mod has an author, catalog entry, versions or an action button, so the grid stays visually even. </summary>
private void VerifyCardSizes(Node screen){if(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this)|| !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(screen))return;var cards=new List<Control>();CollectDescendantsByTypeName(screen,"ModCard",cards);if(cards.Count==0){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: no mod cards after layout",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}var expected=cards[0].Size;foreach(var card in cards){if(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(card.Size.X,expected.X)&&Mathf.IsEqualApprox(card.Size.Y,expected.Y))continue;GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: tile sizes differ ({card.Name}: {card.Size.X}x{card.Size.Y} vs {expected.X}x{expected.Y})",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: all {cards.Count} tiles are {expected.X}x{expected.Y}");// The status ("Installed", "Modified locally") must not be squeezed out by its neighbours in the row (it once shared the row with "in N collections").
foreach(var card in cards){var status=card.GetNodeOrNull<Label>("VBox/Body/Box/MetaRow/Status");if(status==null||string.IsNullOrEmpty(status.Text))continue;var width=status.GetThemeFont("font").GetStringSize(status.Text,HorizontalAlignment.Left,-1,status.GetThemeFontSize("font_size")).X;if(width<=status.Size.X+0.5f)continue;GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: status '{status.Text}' of {card.Name} is cut ({width:0} px text in a {status.Size.X:0} px label)",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}SaveShot("mod_grid");// The cover art must be shifted 1px up and 1px wider than the card body (negative content margins of the panel style), per the design spec.
var panelStyle=cards[0].GetThemeStylebox("panel")as StyleBoxFlat;if(panelStyle==null|| !Mathf.IsEqualApprox(panelStyle.ContentMarginTop,-1f)|| !Mathf.IsEqualApprox(panelStyle.ContentMarginLeft,-1f)){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: cover offset style missing (top={panelStyle?.ContentMarginTop}, left={panelStyle?.ContentMarginLeft})",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}var click=new InputEventMouseButton{ButtonIndex=MouseButton.Left,Pressed=true,};cards[0].EmitSignal("gui_input",click);GetTree().CreateTimer(ModManagerSettleSeconds).Timeout+=()=>VerifyModDetail(screen);}private void VerifyModDetail(Node screen){if(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this)|| !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(screen))return;var detail=FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(screen,"ModDetailView")as Control;if(detail==null|| !detail.Visible){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: detail page did not open (found={detail != null}, visible={detail?.Visible})",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: mod detail page opened");SaveShot("mod_detail");var back=FindButtonByText(FindHub(),TranslationServer.Translate("mod_manager/hub_back_to_mods"));if(back==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: with a mod open the hub's back button does not lead to the mods",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}_screen=screen;back.EmitSignal("pressed");GetTree().CreateTimer(ModManagerSettleSeconds).Timeout+=VerifyBackToMods;}/// <summary>Back from a mod's card shows the grid again inside the still open hub.</summary>
private void VerifyBackToMods(){if(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this))return;var hub=FindHub();var detail=GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(_screen)?FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(_screen,"ModDetailView")as Control:null;var grid=GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(_screen)?FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(_screen,"ModGridView")as Control:null;if(hub==null||hub.IsQueuedForDeletion()||detail==null||detail.Visible||grid==null|| !grid.Visible){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"MainMenuTestSetup: back from a mod did not return to the grid (hub={hub != null}, detail visible={detail?.Visible}, grid visible={grid?.Visible})",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}var back=FindButtonByText(hub,TranslationServer.Translate("mod_manager/hub_back_to_menu"));if(back==null){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: on the grid the back button does not lead to the menu",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: back from the mod card returned to the mods grid");back.EmitSignal("pressed");GetTree().CreateTimer(ModManagerSettleSeconds).Timeout+=VerifyBackToMenu;}/// <summary>The second back leaves the hub to the menu.</summary>
private void VerifyBackToMenu(){if(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this))return;var hub=FindHub();if(hub!=null&& !hub.IsQueuedForDeletion()){GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: back on the mods grid did not leave the hub",LogLevel.Error);Fail("mod_manager");return;}GameLogger.Log("TEST","MainMenuTestSetup: the second back returned to the menu");GameLogger.Log("TEST","[TEST_RESULT] name=mod_manager passed=1 failed=0");GetTree().Quit(0);}/// <summary>With <c>--shots=&lt;dir&gt;</c> saves the window as <c>&lt;name&gt;.png</c> (windowed runs only).</summary>

private static int CountDescendantsByTypeName(Node root,string typeName){var count=0;foreach(var child in root.GetChildren()){if(child.GetType().Name==typeName)count++;count+=CountDescendantsByTypeName(child,typeName);}return count;}/// <summary>Collects descendants whose runtime C# type has the given name.</summary>

private static Node FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(Node root,string typeName){foreach(var child in root.GetChildren()){if(child.GetType().Name==typeName)return child;var found=FindFirstDescendantByTypeName(child,typeName);if(found!=null)return found;}return null;}private void Fail(string testName){GameLogger.Log("TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name={testName} passed=0 failed=1");if(GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(this))GetTree().Quit(1);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/MapCoverageTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the world map subsystem migrated into the map_base engine mod. The zone service, the map widget and the zone layers live in the mod assembly, the core keeps the save DTO (MapZoneStateData), the MapZoneChangedEvent contract and reaches the state through MapStateBridge, so every check here proves the core-to-mod wiring at engine runtime. The machine-readable line is parsed by <c>tests/Cthangover.TestRunner</c>. </summary>
public partial class MapCoverageTestSetup:Node{/// <summary>Log category for all lines of this scene.</summary>
private const string Category="MAP_COVERAGE";private int _passed;private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready, so the subsystem's OnModResourcesReady has already installed its hooks.
var kernelAssembly=typeof(GameData).Assembly;Check("migrated type MapZoneService is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Mods.Map.MapZoneService",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("core assembly no longer defines MapZoneService",KernelLacksTypeNamed(kernelAssembly,"MapZoneService"));Check("migrated type MapWidget is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Mods.Map.MapWidget",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("core assembly no longer defines MapWidget",KernelLacksTypeNamed(kernelAssembly,"MapWidget"));Check("migrated type MapZonesLayer is loaded from a mod assembly",FindOutsideKernel("Cthangover.Mods.Map.MapZonesLayer",kernelAssembly)!=null);Check("MapZoneChangedEvent stays a kernel contract",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Events.MapZoneChangedEvent")!=null);var exportState=SafeGet(()=>Cthangover.Core.Actions.MapStateBridge.ExportState());Check("MapStateBridge.ExportState returns a live delta from the map_base mod",exportState!=null);SafeRun(()=>Cthangover.Core.Actions.MapStateBridge.ResetOverrides());Check("MapStateBridge.ResetOverrides is a safe no-throw reset",true);GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=map_coverage passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}/// <summary>Resolves a mod type by full name outside the kernel assembly (reflection only).</summary>
private static Type FindOutsideKernel(string fullName,System.Reflection.Assembly kernelAssembly){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().Where(a=>a!=kernelAssembly).SelectMany(SafeTypes).FirstOrDefault(t=>t.FullName==fullName);}/// <summary>True when the kernel assembly holds no type with the given simple name.</summary>
private static bool KernelLacksTypeNamed(System.Reflection.Assembly kernelAssembly,string simpleName){return!kernelAssembly.GetTypes().Any(t=>t.Name==simpleName);}/// <summary>Exception-safe getter that logs and degrades to null.</summary>
private static T SafeGet<T>(Func<T>f)where T:class{try{return f();}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"lookup failed: {e.Message}",LogLevel.Error);return null;}}/// <summary>Exception-safe runner for probe mutations.</summary>
private static void SafeRun(Action f){try{f();}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"action failed: {e.Message}",LogLevel.Error);}}/// <summary>Tolerates ReflectionTypeLoadException while scanning assemblies.</summary>
private static Type[]SafeTypes(System.Reflection.Assembly assembly){try{return assembly.GetTypes();}catch{return Array.Empty<Type>();}}private void Check(string what,bool ok){if(ok){_passed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,"PASS "+what);}else{_failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,"FAIL "+what,LogLevel.Error);}}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/QuestsCoverageTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Collections;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Quests;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the quests subsystem migrated into the quests_base engine mod. The core keeps only the kernel contract (<c>IQuest</c> / <c>QuestStatus</c> plus <see cref="QuestServiceRegistry"/>); the factory and runtime state, the IQuestService implementation, the quest actions, the scene conditions with the QuestParser leaf parser and the journal UI live in the mod assembly, so every check here proves the core-to-mod wiring at engine runtime: <list type="bullet"> <item>the mod initializer published the quest service provider into the core registry during <c>OnModLoaded</c>;</item> <item>migrated types (QuestFactory, QuestParser, the journal) are loaded from a mod assembly, and the core assembly no longer defines them;</item> <item>quest state is drivable end-to-end through the core contract: SetStatus/AddTag on the provider are visible to scenario conditions evaluated by the core (proving the mod registered the QuestParser and the EvaluationContext wiring works);</item> <item>the save round-trip (GetAll/SetAll) and the new-game reset behave as before the migration;</item> <item>the quest journal panel opens from the mod's own scene.</item> </list> The machine-readable line is parsed by <c>tests/Cthangover.TestRunner</c>. </summary>
public partial class QuestsCoverageTestSetup:Node{/// <summary>Log category for all lines of this scene.</summary>
private const string Category="QUESTS_COVERAGE";/// <summary>Journal scene now shipped by the quests_base mod itself.</summary>
private const string JournalScenePath="res://mods/quests_base/scenes/ui/quest_journal.tscn";private int _passed;private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready, so QuestsModInitializer.OnModLoaded has already run and published the provider.
var kernelAssembly=typeof(GameData).Assembly;Check("quests mod publishes a quest service provider",QuestServiceRegistry.Provider!=null);Check("quest service provider is registered from a mod assembly, not the core",QuestServiceRegistry.Provider!=null&&QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.GetType().Assembly!=kernelAssembly);var questFactoryType=FindModType(kernelAssembly,"Cthangover.Core.Quests.QuestFactory");Check("migrated type QuestFactory is loaded from a mod assembly",questFactoryType!=null);Check("migrated type QuestParser is loaded from a mod assembly",FindModType(kernelAssembly,"Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Parsing.Impls.QuestParser")!=null);Check("core assembly no longer defines QuestFactory",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Quests.QuestFactory",throwOnError:false)==null);Check("core assembly no longer defines QuestJournalBehaviour",kernelAssembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.UI.Quests.QuestJournalBehaviour",throwOnError:false)==null);// End-to-end state: seed a quest through the mod factory, drive it through the core-side contract, read it back through conditions.
var injected=InjectQuest(questFactoryType,"CoverageQ");Check("quest seeded through the mod factory is visible via the provider",injected&&SafeBool(()=>QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.Exists("CoverageQ")));SafeAction(()=>QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.SetStatus("CoverageQ","Progress"));Check("provider SetStatus flips the quest status",SafeBool(()=>QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.Get("CoverageQ").Status==QuestStatus.Progress));Check("quest condition parses via the mod-registered QuestParser and evaluates",SafeBool(()=>ScenarioCondition.Evaluate("CoverageQ.IsActive")));SafeAction(()=>QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.AddTag("CoverageQ","coverage_tag"));Check("provider AddTag satisfies the hasTag condition",SafeBool(()=>ScenarioCondition.Evaluate("CoverageQ.hasTag(\"coverage_tag\")")));// Save round-trip through the factory (the quests_base saveable serialises exactly this list into the mod slot).
var snapshot=SafeObj(()=>{var instance=GetFactoryInstance(questFactoryType);return questFactoryType.GetMethod("GetAll").Invoke(instance,null);});SafeAction(()=>{var instance=GetFactoryInstance(questFactoryType);questFactoryType.GetMethod("SetAll").Invoke(instance,new[]{snapshot});});Check("save round-trip (GetAll/SetAll) keeps quest state",SafeBool(()=>{var quest=QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.Get("CoverageQ");return quest.Status==QuestStatus.Progress&&ScenarioCondition.Evaluate("CoverageQ.hasTag(\"coverage_tag\")");}));// The journal is a mod-owned panel: load its scene, put it into the tree and confirm the widget comes up from mod sources.
OpenJournal();// New-game reset returns the quest to its fresh state.
SafeAction(()=>questFactoryType.GetMethod("ResetAll").Invoke(GetFactoryInstance(questFactoryType),null));Check("new-game reset returns the quest to the fresh state",SafeBool(()=>{var quest=QuestServiceRegistry.Provider.Get("CoverageQ");return quest.Status==QuestStatus.NotStarted&& !ScenarioCondition.Evaluate("CoverageQ.hasTag(\"coverage_tag\")");}));GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=coverage_quests passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}/// <summary> Opens the quest journal from the mod scene and verifies it comes up inside the tree. Mod scenes must go through the game's runtime tscn pipeline (ModScenePrefab → TscnScenes): native Godot loading cannot see mods/ (gdignore'd) and cannot bind runtime-compiled mod scripts — the SceneBuilder resolves behaviours through ScriptTypeRegistry. </summary>
private void OpenJournal(){Node journal=null;try{journal=new ModScenePrefab(JournalScenePath).Instantiate();Check("quest journal instantiates from the quests_base mod",journal!=null);var behaviourType=journal?.GetType();Check("journal behaviour resolves to the quests_base mod sources",behaviourType!=null&&behaviourType.Name=="QuestJournalBehaviour"&&behaviourType.Assembly!=typeof(GameData).Assembly);if(journal!=null){AddChild(journal);Check("quest journal opens inside the tree",journal.IsInsideTree());// The folio layout lives in quest_journal.tscn and the behaviour resolves it with hard GetNode paths in OnceConstruct — run the widget lifecycle so a broken scene<->code contract fails here.
var built=false;try{journal.GetType().GetMethod("Show",Type.EmptyTypes)?.Invoke(journal,null);built=journal.GetNodeOrNull("BookArea/Book/LeftPage/Content/Column/Scroll/QuestListContainer")!=null&&journal.GetNodeOrNull("BookArea/Book/RightPage/Content/Scroll/Column/EntryText")!=null;journal.GetType().GetMethod("Hide",Type.EmptyTypes)?.Invoke(journal,null);}catch(Exception e){var inner=e.InnerException??e;GameLogger.Log(Category,"journal build threw: "+inner.Message+"\n"+(inner.StackTrace?.Split('\n').FirstOrDefault()??""),LogLevel.Error);}Check("quest journal scene builds (OnceConstruct resolves the folio)",built);}}catch(Exception e){Fail("quest journal open threw: "+e.Message);}finally{journal?.QueueFree();}}/// <summary>Seeds a quest with the given id into the mod factory's internal dictionary via reflection.</summary>
private bool InjectQuest(Type questFactoryType,string id){try{var questBaseType=questFactoryType.Assembly.GetType("Cthangover.Core.Quests.QuestBase",throwOnError:true);var quest=Activator.CreateInstance(questBaseType);questBaseType.GetProperty("ID").SetValue(quest,id);questBaseType.GetProperty("Name").SetValue(quest,id);var instance=GetFactoryInstance(questFactoryType);// Load the current data snapshot first: the factory rebuilds its quests from each new snapshot, which would drop an earlier injection.
questFactoryType.GetMethod("GetAll").Invoke(instance,null);var dataField=questFactoryType.GetField("dataByID",BindingFlags.Instance|BindingFlags.NonPublic);var dict=(IDictionary)dataField.GetValue(instance);dict[id]=quest;return true;}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,"quest injection failed: "+e.Message,LogLevel.Error);return false;}}/// <summary>The process-wide factory singleton (public <c>Instance</c> facade on the mod type).</summary>
private static object GetFactoryInstance(Type questFactoryType){return questFactoryType.GetProperty("Instance").GetValue(null);}/// <summary>Finds a migrated type by full name in any non-kernel assembly.</summary>
private static Type FindModType(Assembly kernelAssembly,string fullName){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().Where(a=>a!=kernelAssembly&& !a.IsDynamic).SelectMany(a=>SafeTypes(a)).FirstOrDefault(t=>t.FullName==fullName);}private void Check(string what,bool ok){if(ok){_passed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {what}");}else{_failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {what}",LogLevel.Error);}}private void Fail(string what){_failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {what}",LogLevel.Error);}private bool SafeBool(Func<bool>f){try{return f();}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,"check failed: "+e.Message,LogLevel.Error);return false;}}private object SafeObj(Func<object>f){try{return f();}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,"lookup failed: "+e.Message,LogLevel.Error);return null;}}private void SafeAction(Action a){try{a();}catch(Exception e){GameLogger.Log(Category,"action failed: "+e.Message,LogLevel.Error);_failed++;}}private static Type[]SafeTypes(Assembly assembly){try{return assembly.GetTypes();}catch{return Array.Empty<Type>();}}}}

namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Integration coverage for the skills subsystem migrated into the skills_base engine mod. The machine-readable line is parsed by <c>tests/Cthangover.TestRunner</c>. </summary>

private static Type[]SafeTypes(System.Reflection.Assembly assembly){try{return assembly.GetTypes();}catch{return Array.Empty<Type>();}}private void Check(string what,bool ok){if(ok){_passed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,"PASS "+what);}else{_failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,"FAIL "+what,LogLevel.Error);}}}}

value");Assert(key!=null&&key.Text==TranslationServer.Translate(Convert.ToString(ReadProperty(first,"Key")??string.Empty)).ToString(),"effect row shows the translated effect name");}// ---- reflection helpers over the skills_base mod assembly ---------- <summary>Resolves a mod type by simple name in the Cthangover.Mods.Skills namespace.</summary>
private static Type ModType(string simpleName){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().Where(a=> !a.IsDynamic).Select(a=>a.GetType("Cthangover.Mods.Skills."+simpleName)).FirstOrDefault(t=>t!=null);}private static object ReadProperty(object instance,string name){return instance.GetType().GetProperty(name)?.GetValue(instance);}private static object InvokeOn(object instance,string method){return instance.GetType().GetMethod(method)?.Invoke(instance,null);}/// <summary> All owned skill models, resolved through the mod's SkillFactory in exactly the order the runtime stores them. </summary>
private static List<object>OwnedSkills(){var result=new List<object>();var factory=ModType("SkillFactory")?.GetProperty("Instance")?.GetValue(null);if(factory==null)return result;var get=factory.GetType().GetMethod("Get");foreach(var id in GameData.Instance.Runtime.SkillData.Skills){var skill=get?.Invoke(factory,new object[]{id});if(skill!=null)result.Add(skill);}return result;}/// <summary>Invokes the mod's SkillPalette.RarityColor for the model's rarity enum value.</summary>

private static List<object>AggregateEffects(List<object>skills){var result=new List<object>();var infoType=ModType("SkillInfo");var aggregator=ModType("SkillEffectAggregator");if(infoType==null||aggregator==null)return result;// Aggregate takes IEnumerable<SkillInfo>; rebuild the list with the mod-visible element type the reflection binder can match.

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/SubsystemsBridge.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;/// <summary> Reflection bridge between <see cref="SubsystemsCoverageTestSetup"/> and the engine mods. Migrated subsystems live in mod assemblies that the core test code cannot reference at compile time, so every access goes through reflection here (same pattern as <c>TurnBattleBridge</c>). <para/> Registration contract for a migrated subsystem: its mod ships exactly one public static class named <c>SubsystemRegistration</c> exposing the subsystem id either as a static property or a public static field: <code> public static class SubsystemRegistration { public static string SubsystemId => "audio"; } </code> The registry/marker is the source of truth: a registration found in the loaded mod set declares the subsystem migrated, and the coverage test then requires a declared integration test for it. </summary>
internal static class SubsystemsBridge{/// <summary>Exact simple name of the marker class every migrated subsystem ships.</summary>
private const string RegistrationTypeName="SubsystemRegistration";/// <summary> All <c>SubsystemRegistration</c> types found in the currently loaded assemblies. A registration with an unreadable <c>SubsystemId</c> is returned with a null id so the caller can fail with a clear message. </summary>
public static List<Registration>FindRegistrations(){var registrations=new List<Registration>();foreach(var assembly in AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()){if(assembly.IsDynamic)continue;Type registrationType;try{registrationType=assembly.GetTypes().FirstOrDefault(t=>t.Name==RegistrationTypeName);}catch(Exception ex){GameLogger.Log("SUBSYSTEM_COVERAGE","SubsystemsBridge: failed to scan assembly '"+assembly.GetName().Name+"': "+ex.Message,LogLevel.Warning);continue;}if(registrationType==null)continue;registrations.Add(new Registration{AssemblyName=assembly.GetName().Name,SubsystemId=ReadSubsystemId(registrationType),});}return registrations.OrderBy(r=>r.SubsystemId,StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();}/// <summary>Reads the SubsystemId member (static property or public static field), null when missing.</summary>
private static string ReadSubsystemId(Type registrationType){const BindingFlags flags=BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static;var value=registrationType.GetProperty("SubsystemId",flags)?.GetValue(null)??registrationType.GetField("SubsystemId",flags)?.GetValue(null);return value as string;}/// <summary>One discovered <c>SubsystemRegistration</c> marker in a mod assembly.</summary>
public sealed class Registration{/// <summary>Assembly that ships the marker (diagnostics only).</summary>
public string AssemblyName;/// <summary>Subsystem id, e.g. "audio". Null when the marker is broken.</summary>
public string SubsystemId;}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/SubsystemsCoverageTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Reports integration-test coverage of the migrated gameplay subsystems. Generalizes <see cref="BattleCoverageTestSetup"/> from battle cores to every subsystem that leaves the core assembly during the "core = mod player" migration: a subsystem's mod registry (the <c>SubsystemRegistration</c> marker, see <see cref="SubsystemsBridge"/>) is the single source of truth for what is already migrated, and <see cref="DeclaredTests"/> maps each subsystem to the runner test that covers it. A migrated subsystem without a declared test fails the run, so moving a subsystem forces adding its test. The machine-readable line is parsed by <c>tests/Cthangover.TestRunner</c>. <para/> Transitional state: while a declared subsystem has not moved yet, its registration is simply absent — the run stays green and the subsystem is reported as skipped, so an empty or partially migrated mod set passes. </summary>
public partial class SubsystemsCoverageTestSetup:Node{/// <summary>Log category for all lines of this scene.</summary>
private const string Category="SUBSYSTEM_COVERAGE";/// <summary> Subsystem id to the runner test name covering it. Update together with <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c> when a subsystem finishes its migration (the id must match the marker's SubsystemId). </summary>
private static readonly Dictionary<string,string>DeclaredTests=new(){["items"]="coverage_items",["quests"]="coverage_quests",["characters"]="coverage_characters",["interactive"]="coverage_interactive",["skills"]="coverage_skills",["time"]="coverage_time",["map"]="coverage_map",["effects"]="coverage_effects",["dialog"]="coverage_dialog",["audio"]="coverage_audio",};private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready.
var registrations=SubsystemsBridge.FindRegistrations();GameLogger.Log(Category,$"registered subsystems: {registrations.Count}");GameLogger.Log(Category,$"declared tests: {DeclaredTests.Count}");var manifestTests=TestManifestNames.Load();var migrated=new HashSet<string>();foreach(var registration in registrations){if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(registration.SubsystemId)){Fail($"mod assembly '{registration.AssemblyName}' ships SubsystemRegistration without a readable SubsystemId");continue;}if(!migrated.Add(registration.SubsystemId)){Fail($"duplicate SubsystemRegistration for subsystem='{registration.SubsystemId}' in '{registration.AssemblyName}'");continue;}if(!DeclaredTests.TryGetValue(registration.SubsystemId,out var testName)){Fail($"subsystem='{registration.SubsystemId}' (mod '{registration.AssemblyName}') is registered but has no declared integration test — "+"add it to DeclaredTests and tests/tests.manifest.json");continue;}if(!manifestTests.Contains(testName)){Fail($"subsystem='{registration.SubsystemId}' declares test '{testName}', which tests/tests.manifest.json does not run");continue;}Pass($"subsystem={registration.SubsystemId} test={testName} mod={registration.AssemblyName}");}foreach(var declared in DeclaredTests){if(!migrated.Contains(declared.Key))GameLogger.Log(Category,$"SKIP subsystem={declared.Key} test={declared.Value}: not migrated yet (no SubsystemRegistration in loaded mods)");}GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=coverage_subsystems passed={(_failed == 0 ? 1 : 0)} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}private void Pass(string message){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {message}");}private void Fail(string message){_failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {message}",LogLevel.Error);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Autotest/TestManifestNames.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Text.Json;using Godot;/// <summary> Test names of <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c>. ===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Battle/BattleTestDriver.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Headless driver for the battle integration test. Both shipped battle cores wait for player input, so the driver periodically presses the "end turn" button of the active core, letting the enemy AI act until the battle scene reports <c>IsDestroyed</c> (victory or defeat). The core is selected by <c>--core=</c> (card_battle by default) and only affects how the end-turn button is located: card_battle nests it under a named node, ff_battle adds a plain Button directly to ToolPanel. A missing battle scene or an endless battle is reported as a failure and quits with exit code 1, so the CI run fails instead of hanging. <para/> The battle scene, core registry and autoplay contract live in the turn-battle mod, so all typed access goes through <see cref="TurnBattleBridge"/>. </summary>
public partial class BattleTestDriver:Node{private const string CardBattleCore="card_battle";private const string EndTurnButtonPath="ToolPanel/EndTurnButton/BtnEndTurn";private const double PollIntervalSeconds=0.25d;private const double SetupTimeoutSeconds=15d;private const double BattleTimeoutSeconds=90d;private const int MaxEndTurnPresses=30;private string _core=BattleTestSetup.DefaultBattleCore;private double _elapsed;private double _nextPoll;private int _turnPresses;private bool _finished;private bool _speedChecked;public override void _Ready(){foreach(var arg in OS.GetCmdlineArgs()){if(arg.StartsWith("--core="))_core=arg.Substring("--core=".Length).Trim();}}/// <summary> Preferred universal path: cores implementing the mod's IBattleAutoPlayable report when they wait for the player and accept a turn advance directly, so the driver does not depend on core-specific UI layout. The UI lookup in <see cref="FindEndTurnButton"/> remains as a fallback for cores without the contract. </summary>
private bool TryAdvanceAutoPlayable(Node battle){var core=TurnBattleBridge.GetActiveCore(battle);var autoType=TurnBattleBridge.AutoPlayableType();if(core==null||autoType==null|| !autoType.IsInstanceOfType(core))return false;var waiting=(bool?)autoType.GetProperty("IsWaitingForPlayerInput")?.GetValue(core)??false;if(!waiting)return false;_turnPresses++;GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"BattleTestDriver: auto turn #{_turnPresses}");autoType.GetMethod("AdvancePlayerTurn")?.Invoke(core,null);return true;}/// <summary> Locates the active core's end-turn button. card_battle builds a named widget at a fixed path; ff_battle creates an unnamed Button and adds it as a direct child of ToolPanel. </summary>
private Button FindEndTurnButton(Node battle){if(_core!=CardBattleCore){var toolPanel=battle.GetNodeOrNull<Control>("ToolPanel");if(toolPanel==null)return null;foreach(var child in toolPanel.GetChildren()){if(child is Button button)return button;}return null;}return battle.GetNodeOrNull<Button>(EndTurnButtonPath);}public override void _Process(double delta){if(_finished)return;_elapsed+=delta;if(_elapsed<_nextPoll)return;_nextPoll=_elapsed+PollIntervalSeconds;var battle=TurnBattleBridge.GetSceneContext();if(battle==null){if(_elapsed>=SetupTimeoutSeconds)Finish(false,"battle scene never became ready");return;}if(!_speedChecked){_speedChecked=true;if(Engine.TimeScale<BattleTestSetup.MaxBattleSpeed){GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"BattleTestDriver: core={_core}, battle speed was x{Engine.TimeScale}, forcing x{BattleTestSetup.MaxBattleSpeed}",LogLevel.Warning);Engine.TimeScale=BattleTestSetup.MaxBattleSpeed;}else{GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"BattleTestDriver: core={_core}, battle speed x{Engine.TimeScale}");}}if(TurnBattleBridge.IsDestroyed(battle)){_finished=true;FinishAfterBattle($"battle finished in {_elapsed:F1}s, end-turn presses={_turnPresses}");return;}if(_turnPresses>=MaxEndTurnPresses||_elapsed>=BattleTimeoutSeconds){Finish(false,$"battle did not finish (presses={_turnPresses}, elapsed={_elapsed:F1}s)");return;}if(TryAdvanceAutoPlayable(battle))return;var endTurn=FindEndTurnButton(battle);if(endTurn==null|| !endTurn.Visible)return;_turnPresses++;GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"BattleTestDriver: end turn #{_turnPresses}");endTurn.EmitSignal("pressed");}/// <summary> Lets the battle scene's nodes be freed, then changes the party's health the way an item does between battles: party attributes outlive the battle, so a battle UI still subscribed to them would throw from its freed widgets into whatever changed the value. </summary>
private async void FinishAfterBattle(string message){await ToSignal(GetTree(),SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);await ToSignal(GetTree(),SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);try{foreach(var member in Settings.GameData.Instance.Runtime.CharacterData.Characters.Values){var health=member.Attributes?.Health;if(health!=null)health.Value=health.Value;}}catch(System.Exception ex){Finish(false,$"party health change after the battle threw {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}");return;}Finish(true,message);}private void Finish(bool passed,string message){_finished=true;GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"BattleTestDriver: {(passed ? "PASS" : "FAIL")} - {message}",passed?LogLevel.Message:LogLevel.Error);GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name=battle core={_core} passed={(passed ? 1 : 0)} failed={(passed ? 0 : 1)}");GetTree().Quit(passed?0:1);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Battle/BattleTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Resolvers;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Prepares a deterministic battle encounter for <c>BattleTestScene</c>. The node is placed as the first sibling before the instantiated <c>battle.tscn</c>, so Godot runs its <c>_Ready</c> before the battle scene reads the pending encounter and the battle core registry. Mods are guaranteed to be loaded before any scene _Ready by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload runs Boot.RunSync). No starter-party initializer runs in this direct scene launch, so the party consists of exactly the characters added here. <para/> The encounter lives in the turn-battle mod assembly, so the bridge prepares it through reflection: build BattleData with a caller-supplied background, select the active core, and store it in the mod's BattleRequest slot. </summary>
public partial class BattleTestSetup:Node{/// <summary> Battle speed applied to the run. The battle speed control restores <see cref="Settings.SettingsData.BattleSpeed"/> into <c>Engine.TimeScale</c> while the battle scene is being readied (after this node), so writing the setting here is the supported way to accelerate the whole test: battle timers and action animations both scale from <c>Engine.TimeScale</c>. The value is intentionally not persisted to disk. </summary>
public const int MaxBattleSpeed=8;public const string DefaultBattleCore="card_battle";private const string PartyMember="Murakami";private const string ReturnScene="MainMenu";public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready.

The value is passed to the mod's BattleData.ActiveBattleCore, which the battle scene resolves through the core registry before Init. </summary>
private static string ParseBattleCore(){foreach(var arg in OS.GetCmdlineArgs()){if(arg.StartsWith("--core="))return arg.Substring("--core=".Length).Trim();}return DefaultBattleCore;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Battle/TurnBattleBridge.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Reflection bridge between the core integration tests and the turn-battle mod. The battle platform (BattleData, BattleCoreRegistry, BattleRequest, BattleSceneContext, IBattleAutoPlayable) now lives in the <c>turn_battle_base</c> mod assembly, which the core test project cannot reference at compile time — so every access goes through reflection here. Keeping the reflection in one place lets the test setup and driver read almost like plain code and keeps the odd member names out of the tests. </summary>
internal static class TurnBattleBridge{/// <summary>Assembly name of the mod that owns the battle platform (matches its manifest id).</summary>
private const string BattleAssemblyName="turn_battle_base";private static Assembly FindBattleAssembly(){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().FirstOrDefault(a=>a.GetName().Name==BattleAssemblyName);}private static Type FindType(string fullName){return FindBattleAssembly()?.GetType(fullName);}private static object GetRegistryInstance(){var type=FindType("Cthangover.Core.Battle.BattleCoreRegistry");return type?.GetField("Instance",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static)?.GetValue(null);}/// <summary>Checks whether a battle core id was registered by the loaded mods.</summary>
public static bool HasCore(string coreId){var instance=GetRegistryInstance();if(instance==null){GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"TurnBattleBridge.HasCore('{coreId}'): registry instance not found, assembly={FindBattleAssembly()?.GetName().Name ?? "NULL"}",LogLevel.Warning);return false;}return(bool?)instance.GetType().GetMethod("HasCore")?.Invoke(instance,new object[]{coreId})??false;}/// <summary>Selects the active battle core by id (throws when unknown, same as production).</summary>
public static void SetActiveCore(string coreId){var instance=GetRegistryInstance();instance?.GetType().GetMethod("SetActive")?.Invoke(instance,new object[]{coreId});}/// <summary>Registered core ids, sorted, for the coverage check.</summary>
public static IReadOnlyList<string>CoreIds(){var instance=GetRegistryInstance();var ids=instance?.GetType().GetProperty("CoreIds")?.GetValue(instance)as IReadOnlyList<string>;return ids??Array.Empty<string>();}/// <summary>Registered <see cref="Type"/> of a core so tooling can check its interfaces.</summary>
public static Type GetCoreType(string coreId){var instance=GetRegistryInstance();return instance?.GetType().GetMethod("GetCoreType")?.Invoke(instance,new object[]{coreId})as Type;}/// <summary>The mod's IBattleAutoPlayable contract type, or null when the mod is not loaded.</summary>
public static Type AutoPlayableType(){return FindType("Cthangover.Core.Battle.IBattleAutoPlayable");}/// <summary> Builds a pending encounter exactly like BattleService does, but with a caller-supplied background so the test does not depend on scene state. Returns the mod's BattleData instance (typed as object). </summary>
public static object CreateBattleData(Texture2D background,string returnScene,string coreId,params string[]enemies){var dataType=FindType("Cthangover.Core.Settings.BattleData");var init=dataType?.GetMethod("InitBattle",new[]{typeof(Texture2D),typeof(string),typeof(string[])});var data=init?.Invoke(null,new object[]{background,returnScene,enemies});if(data==null){GameLogger.Log("BATTLE_TEST",$"TurnBattleBridge: failed to create BattleData via reflection (asm={FindBattleAssembly()?.GetName().Name ?? "NULL"}, dataType={dataType != null}, init={init != null})",LogLevel.Error);return null;}dataType.GetProperty("ActiveBattleCore")?.SetValue(data,coreId);return data;}/// <summary>Stores a prepared encounter in the mod's static BattleRequest slot.</summary>
public static void SetPendingBattle(object data){var requestType=FindType("Cthangover.Core.Battle.BattleRequest");requestType?.GetProperty("Current",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static)?.SetValue(null,data);}/// <summary>The active <see cref="BattleSceneContext"/> node, or null before it enters the tree.</summary>
public static Node GetSceneContext(){var type=FindType("Cthangover.Core.Battle.BattleSceneContext");return type?.GetProperty("Instance",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static)?.GetValue(null)as Node;}/// <summary>True once the battle scene flushed victory or defeat state.</summary>
public static bool IsDestroyed(Node battle){return(bool?)battle?.GetType().GetProperty("IsDestroyed")?.GetValue(battle)??false;}/// <summary>The live battle core instance driving the scene.</summary>
public static object GetActiveCore(Node battle){return battle?.GetType().GetProperty("ActiveBattleCore")?.GetValue(battle);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Events/EventBusTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Events;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Runtime checks of the <see cref="ModEventBus"/> GDScript bridge, which needs the autoload and Godot variants and so cannot run under xUnit: a string-layer subscriber (what a GDScript mod registers) must receive typed events whatever their payload, and a payload the bridge cannot convert must never throw into the code that published the event. </summary>
public partial class EventBusTestSetup:Node{private const string Category="EVENT_BUS_TEST";private readonly List<Godot.Collections.Dictionary>received=new();private int failed;public override void _Ready(){var bus=ModEventBus.Instance;var handler=new Callable(this,MethodName.OnBridged);bus.Subscribe("scene_composed",handler);bus.Subscribe("characters_defeated",handler);CheckPublish("scene_composed",new SceneComposedEvent{SceneName="bus_scene",Candidates=new List<SceneCandidateReport>{new(){Id="evt",Outcome=SceneCandidateOutcome.Queued}},},"scene_name","bus_scene");CheckNested("candidates",0,"outcome","Queued");CheckPublish("characters_defeated",new CharactersDefeatedEvent{Source="bus_test",CharacterIds=new[]{"wolf_1","wolf_2"},},"source","bus_test");CheckNested("character_ids",1,null,"wolf_2");bus.Unsubscribe("scene_composed",handler);bus.Unsubscribe("characters_defeated",handler);GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=event_bus passed={(failed == 0 ? 1 : 0)} failed={failed}");GetTree().Quit(failed==0?0:1);}private void OnBridged(Godot.Collections.Dictionary data)=>received.Add(data);private void CheckPublish<T>(string key,T evt,string field,string expected)where T:ModEvent{received.Clear();try{ModEventBus.Publish(evt);}catch(System.Exception ex){Fail($"{key}: Publish threw into the publisher: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}");return;}if(received.Count!=1){Fail($"{key}: string subscriber got {received.Count} call(s), expected 1");return;}var actual=received[0].ContainsKey(field)?received[0][field].AsString():null;if(actual!=expected)Fail($"{key}: '{field}' = '{actual}', expected '{expected}'");else GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {key}: delivered with {received[0].Count} field(s)");}/// <summary>Checks one element of an array field of the last delivery (a nested object's field when <paramref name="field"/> is set).</summary>
private void CheckNested(string arrayKey,int index,string field,string expected){if(received.Count!=1|| !received[0].ContainsKey(arrayKey)||received[0][arrayKey].VariantType!=Variant.Type.Array){Fail($"{arrayKey}: not delivered as an array");return;}var items=received[0][arrayKey].AsGodotArray();var item=index<items.Count?items[index]:default;var actual=field==null?item.AsString():item.AsGodotDictionary().ContainsKey(field)?item.AsGodotDictionary()[field].AsString():null;if(actual!=expected)Fail($"{arrayKey}[{index}]{(field == null ? "" : "." + field)} = '{actual}', expected '{expected}'");else GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {arrayKey}[{index}] = '{actual}'");}private void Fail(string message){failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {message}",LogLevel.Error);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Map/MapZonePersistenceTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System;using System.Collections;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Integration test for world map zone persistence. Exercises the real save pipeline (<see cref="SaveService"/>) with the map_base mod's MapZoneService singleton to prove that runtime open/hidden overrides survive a full save → new state → load cycle through the core <c>__core__</c> slot, and that zone IDs unknown to the loaded data do not break restoration. Zones are injected directly (no mod map files are required), so the test is independent of the map content that happens to ship with the mods. <para/> The zone service lives in the map_base engine mod, so every access to its types (MapZoneService, MapZone) goes through reflection here — the same rule every core-side setup follows. </summary>
public partial class MapZonePersistenceTestSetup:Node{private const string SaveName="map_zones_test";private int _passed;private int _failed;public override void _Ready(){// Mods are loaded by the boot pipeline (SceneManager autoload -> Boot.RunSync) before any scene _Ready.
var serviceType=ModType("MapZoneService");var service=serviceType?.GetProperty("Instance")?.GetValue(null);var zoneType=ModType("MapZone");if(service==null||zoneType==null){Fail("map_base mod types are not loaded");Finish();return;}var zones=(IList)Activator.CreateInstance(typeof(List<>).MakeGenericType(zoneType));zones.Add(MakeZone(zoneType,"map_test_plain",locked:false,hidden:false));zones.Add(MakeZone(zoneType,"map_test_locked",locked:true,hidden:false));zones.Add(MakeZone(zoneType,"map_test_hidden",locked:false,hidden:true));InvokeOn(service,"LoadZones",zones);InvokeOn(service,"Open","map_test_locked");InvokeOn(service,"Hide","map_test_plain");AssertTrue(IsOpen(service),"opened zone is open before save");AssertFalse(IsVisible(service,"map_test_plain"),"hidden zone is hidden before save");SaveService.Save(SaveName);Pass("save written");InvokeOn(service,"ResetOverrides");AssertFalse(IsOpen(service),"reset drops the open override");AssertTrue(IsVisible(service,"map_test_plain"),"reset drops the hidden override");var loaded=SaveService.Load(SaveName);AssertTrue(loaded,"SaveService.Load returns true");AssertTrue(IsOpen(service),"open override restored from the core slot");AssertFalse(IsVisible(service,"map_test_plain"),"hidden override restored from the core slot");AssertTrue(IsOpen(service,"map_test_hidden"),"untouched zone keeps its open authoring default");AssertFalse(IsVisible(service,"map_test_hidden"),"hidden authoring default survives the load");SaveService.DeleteSave(SaveName);Finish();}// ---- reflection helpers over the map_base mod assembly -------------- <summary>Resolves a mod type by simple name in the Cthangover.Mods.Map namespace.</summary>
private static Type ModType(string simpleName){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().Where(a=> !a.IsDynamic).Select(a=>a.GetType("Cthangover.Mods.Map."+simpleName)).FirstOrDefault(t=>t!=null);}private static object MakeZone(Type zoneType,string id,bool locked,bool hidden){var zone=Activator.CreateInstance(zoneType);zoneType.GetProperty("ID")?.SetValue(zone,id);zoneType.GetProperty("Locked")?.SetValue(zone,locked);zoneType.GetProperty("Hidden")?.SetValue(zone,hidden);return zone;}private static object InvokeOn(object instance,string method,params object[]args){return instance.GetType().GetMethod(method)?.Invoke(instance,args);}private bool IsOpen(object service,string id="map_test_locked"){return Equals(InvokeOn(service,"IsOpen",id),true);}private bool IsVisible(object service,string id){return Equals(InvokeOn(service,"IsVisible",id),true);}private void AssertTrue(bool condition,string label){if(condition)Pass(label);else Fail($"expected true: {label}");}private void AssertFalse(bool condition,string label){if(!condition)Pass(label);else Fail($"expected false: {label}");}private void Pass(string message){_passed++;GameLogger.Log("MAP_TEST",$"  [PASS] {message}");}private void Fail(string message){_failed++;GameLogger.Log("MAP_TEST",$"  [FAIL] {message}",LogLevel.Error);}private void Finish(){GameLogger.Log("MAP_TEST",$"MapZonePersistenceTestSetup: {_passed} passed, {_failed} failed");GameLogger.Log("MAP_TEST",$"[TEST_RESULT] name=map_zones passed={_passed} failed={_failed}");GetTree().Quit(_failed==0?0:1);}}}

public static bool IsLoaded=>FindAssembly()!=null;private static Assembly FindAssembly(){return AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies().FirstOrDefault(a=>a.GetName().Name==ModAssemblyName);}private static object GetRoster(){var type=FindAssembly()?.GetType(RosterTypeName);return type?.GetField("Instance",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static)?.GetValue(null);}private static object GetRecruit(string id){var roster=GetRoster();return roster?.GetType().GetMethod("GetByID")?.Invoke(roster,new object[]{id});}private static Type GetPartyBridgeType(){return FindAssembly()?.GetType(PartyBridgeTypeName);}/// <summary>Number of entries in the session roster, or -1 when the mod is not loaded.</summary>

public static void SetHealth(string id,int health){var recruit=GetRecruit(id);if(recruit?.GetType().GetProperty("Properties")?.GetValue(recruit)is PropertyData properties)properties.SetInt("Health",health);}/// <summary>Reads the recruit's own health, or -1 when the recruit is missing.</summary>

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Recruiting/RecruitingPersistenceTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
using System;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Events;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{/// <summary> Integration test for the recruiting mod's contracts. ===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests.Integration/Saves/SaveIntegrityTestSetup.cs =====
#if TOOLS
namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using System.IO;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Runtime checks of <see cref="SaveService"/> against damaged slot files (a crash or a full disk mid-write leaves one behind): loading one must report failure to the menu that asked, never throw into it. </summary>
public partial class SaveIntegrityTestSetup:Node{private const string Category="SAVE_TEST";private const string BrokenSlot=SaveService.HiddenSlotPrefix+"test_broken";private int failed;public override void _Ready()=>Callable.From(Run).CallDeferred();private void Run(){Directory.CreateDirectory(SaveService.Path);var path=Path.Combine(SaveService.Path,BrokenSlot+".json");File.WriteAllText(path,"{\"SaveId\":\"test_broken\",\"ModData\":{\"__core__\":{\"Ver");try{Check("loading a truncated slot reports failure",!SaveService.Load(BrokenSlot));}catch(System.Exception ex){Check($"loading a truncated slot threw {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}",false);}finally{File.Delete(path);}GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=save_integrity passed={(failed == 0 ? 1 : 0)} failed={failed}");GetTree().Quit(failed==0?0:1);}private void Check(string what,bool ok){if(ok){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {what}");return;}failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {what}",LogLevel.Error);}}}

namespace Cthangover.Core.Autotest{using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Godot;/// <summary> Runtime checks of the scene lookup cache (<see cref="SceneContextNode"/>) and of <see cref="SceneManager.SwitchScene"/> for a scene without any event: lookups by name are typed, so one type's miss or hit must not decide another type's answer, and every scene switch refreshes the save icon, whatever the scene holds. </summary>
public partial class SceneContextTestSetup:Node{private const string Category="SCENE_CTX_TEST";private int failed;public override void _Ready()=>Callable.From(Run).CallDeferred();private void Run(){var probe=new SaveIconProbe{Name="TopView"};GetTree().Root.AddChild(probe);var label=new Label{Name="SceneCtxTestLabel"};AddChild(label);SceneContextNode.Instance.ClearData();Check("typed miss does not hide the node from a matching type",SceneContextNode.FindNode<Button>("SceneCtxTestLabel")==null&&SceneContextNode.FindNode<Label>("SceneCtxTestLabel")==label);Check("cached node asked as another type is a miss, not an exception",TryFind<Button>("SceneCtxTestLabel")&&SceneContextNode.FindNode<Control>("SceneCtxTestLabel")==label);var sceneManager=GetTree().Root.GetNode<SceneManager>("SceneManager");var before=probe.Updates;sceneManager.SwitchScene("scene_ctx_test_empty");Check("switch into a scene without events refreshes the save icon",probe.Updates>before);probe.QueueFree();GameLogger.Log(Category,$"[TEST_RESULT] name=scene_context passed={(failed == 0 ? 1 : 0)} failed={failed}");GetTree().Quit(failed==0?0:1);}private static bool TryFind<T>(string name)where T:Node{try{return SceneContextNode.FindNode<T>(name)==null;}catch(System.Exception ex){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FindNode<{typeof(T).Name}>('{name}') threw {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}",LogLevel.Error);return false;}}private void Check(string what,bool ok){if(ok){GameLogger.Log(Category,$"PASS {what}");return;}failed++;GameLogger.Log(Category,$"FAIL {what}",LogLevel.Error);}}}
#endif

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Arch/KernelPurityTests.cs =====
namespace Engine.Tests.Arch{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Xunit;/// <summary> Architecture guard for the "core = mod player" migration. The core assembly must not own gameplay subsystems — those live in engine mods (mods/&lt;name&gt;) compiled at runtime. This test scans the compiled core assembly by reflection and fails when a type sits in one of the banned subsystem namespaces. <para/> Both the ban list and the allowlist are data-driven: edit <see cref="Rules"/> and <see cref="AllowedTypes"/> at the top of the file. <para/> Transitional procedure: while a subsystem still has types in the core assembly its rule stays <c>Enforced: false</c> (types are reported, run stays green). Once the subsystem has fully moved to its mod, flip the rule to <c>Enforced: true</c> — from then on any type reappearing in the namespace fails the build, so dependencies cannot grow back. </summary>
public class KernelPurityTests{/// <summary>One rule per subsystem namespace that must leave the core assembly.</summary>
private sealed record SubsystemRule(string Namespace,bool Enforced,string Note);/// <summary> Ban list of subsystem namespaces. Matching is by namespace prefix: <c>Cthangover.Core.UI.Dialog</c> also covers <c>Cthangover.Core.UI.Dialog.Action</c> and so on. Flip <c>Enforced</c> to <c>true</c> when the subsystem has finished moving into its engine mod. </summary>
private static readonly SubsystemRule[]Rules={new("Cthangover.Core.Items",Enforced:true,"предметы/инвентарь перенесены в engine-мод items_base; в ядре остаются контракты (IItem/IItemAction/IItemContainer/IIngredient/IRecipe/IInventory), enum'ы ItemType/WorkbenchType, GD-обёртки GDItem/GDItemAction/GDInventoryService и мост ItemActionBridge — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Quests",Enforced:true,"квесты перенесены в engine-мод quests_base; в ядре остаётся контракт доступа (IQuest, QuestStatus, QuestServiceRegistry) — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Characters",Enforced:true,"персонажи перенесены в engine-мод characters_base; в ядре остаются модель данных (Character/ActionCharacter/CharacterData и др.) и контракты провайдеров — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Interactive",Enforced:true,"интерактивы перенесены в engine-мод interactive_base; в ядре остаётся контракт переходного периода IInteractiveDialogRunner (резолв /root/DialogRunner) — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Skills",Enforced:true,"скиллы перенесены в engine-мод skills_base; в ядре остаётся переходная DTO SkillData, которую читают RuntimeData и RuntimeSerializer, — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Lights",Enforced:true,"свет перенесён в engine-мод time_base (UiLightController/LampBehaviour/LightDef); SceneManager работает через LightingBridge"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Dialog",Enforced:true,"диалоги перенесены в engine-мод dialog_base; в ядре остаётся контракт переходного периода IDialogBox — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Executable",Enforced:true,"executable-события перенесены в engine-мод dialog_base; в ядре остаются контракты IExecutableEvent/IExecutableEventChain/IConditionedEvent/IScenarioEventFactory/IEventObject — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Scenarios",Enforced:true,"сценарный DSL перенесён в engine-мод dialog_base; в ядре остаются ScenarioMetadata (метаданные .scenario) — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.Audio",Enforced:true,"аудио перенесено в engine-мод audio_base; контракт переходного периода IAudioService + enum'ы MixerType/MusicType/SoundType остаются в ядре"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.View",Enforced:true,"видовой экран фона перенесён в engine-мод interface (ViewBox/ViewBoxNavigator/ZoomViewBoxController/AlignType); SceneManager и SaveScreenshotService работают через контракт IBackgroundView"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Theming",Enforced:true,"дизайн-система перенесена в engine-мод interface (UiKit); в ядре остаются CthangoverTheme/UiTokens — их использует Launcher в boot-окне до компиляции модов — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Messages",Enforced:true,"уведомления перенесены в engine-мод interface (MessagesHelper/NotificationPanel/UiMessage); в ядре остаётся чистый C# буфер NotificationBuffer (unit-тестируемый) — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Event",Enforced:true,"ввод перенесён в engine-мод interface (DeviceInput/TouchPhase); в ядре остаётся SceneEventController — Godot-autoload, тикающий с первых кадров до компиляции модов, и контракты IOnUpdateEvent/IOnTimeEvent/IEventPriority — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Tool",Enforced:true,"тулбар перенесён в engine-мод interface (ToolBox/ToolWindow/ToolMenuButton/icon-loaders); в ядре остаются контракты IToolProvider/IToolBoxButton, фабрики ToolFactory/ToolBoxButtonFactory и DTO MapZoneStateData (слот __core__ RuntimeSerializer) — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu",Enforced:true,"меню перенесено в engine-мод interface (GameMenu/MenuBuilder/SaveIcon/EmptyBlackBehaviour); в ядре остаются Launcher/BootLogPanel (главная сцена и boot-оверлей до компиляции модов) и контракты реестра MenuRegistry/MenuButtonEntry/MenuPanelEntry/IMenuPanel — точный список в AllowedTypes"),new("Cthangover.Core.UI.Base",Enforced:true,"виджет-тулкит перенесён в engine-мод interface (Widget/ModWidget/списки/ModControl/ModNode/InputHandlerNode); в ядре остаётся ModSpriteLoader — скрипт boot-сцен launcher.tscn/main_menu.tscn, инстанцируемых до компиляции модов — точный список в AllowedTypes"),};/// <summary> Types explicitly exempted from the ban (exact full name). Three families live here. The transitional audio contract that stays in the core assembly: the <c>IAudioService</c> interface and the MusicType/SoundType/MixerType enums referenced by its signatures. The characters contract set: the data model (<c>Character</c>, <c>ActionCharacter</c>, <c>CharacterData</c>, attributes, loot) still consumed by the core itself (GDCharacterService, inventory, battle wiring) and by foreign mods, plus the provider contracts (<c>ICharacterTemplateProvider</c> / <c>IActionTemplateProvider</c> / <c>IAvatarProvider</c> side registries) through which the characters_base mod serves templates to the core player. And the quests contract set: the <c>IQuest</c> interface and <c>QuestStatus</c> enum referenced by the core-side <c>IQuestService</c> signatures, plus the <c>QuestServiceRegistry</c> through which the quests_base mod serves quest operations to the core player. Remove entries together with their owning rule when the contract itself leaves the core (enforced by <see cref="KernelPurity_AllowlistIsCurrent"/>). </summary>
private static readonly string[]AllowedTypes={"Cthangover.Core.Audio.IAudioService","Cthangover.Core.Audio.MixerType","Cthangover.Core.Audio.MusicType","Cthangover.Core.Audio.SoundType",// items_base: kernel contract that stays in the core. Item/recipe data (Item/ItemInfo/ItemContainer/Ingredient/IngredientInfo/ Recipe/RecipeInfo/Inventory), the factories and the inventory UI live in the items_base engine mod; the core keeps the interface contracts its own services (RuntimeData, ScenarioActionContext, GDInventoryService) are written against, plus the GDScript wrappers and the ItemActionBridge delivery point.
"Cthangover.Core.Items.IItem","Cthangover.Core.Items.IItemAction","Cthangover.Core.Items.IItemContainer","Cthangover.Core.Items.IIngredient","Cthangover.Core.Items.IRecipe","Cthangover.Core.Items.IInventory","Cthangover.Core.Items.ItemType","Cthangover.Core.Items.WorkbenchType","Cthangover.Core.Items.GDItem","Cthangover.Core.Items.GDItemAction","Cthangover.Core.Items.GDInventoryService","Cthangover.Core.Items.ItemActionBridge",// interactive_base: kernel contract that stays in the core. "Cthangover.Core.UI.Base.ModSpriteLoader+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Base.ModSpriteLoader+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Base.ModSpriteLoader+SignalName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.Launcher+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.Launcher+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.Launcher+SignalName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.ModSetTransitionScreen+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.ModSetTransitionScreen+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.ModSetTransitionScreen+SignalName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootLogPanel+LogLine","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootProgressBar+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootLogPanel+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootLogPanel+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootProgressBar+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootLogPanel+SignalName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu.BootProgressBar+SignalName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Event.SceneEventController+MethodName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Event.SceneEventController+PropertyName","Cthangover.Core.UI.Event.SceneEventController+SignalName",};[Fact]public void KernelPurity_CoreAssemblyContainsNoSubsystemTypes(){var kernelTypes=KernelTypes();var violations=new List<string>();var transition=new List<string>();foreach(var rule in Rules){var offenders=kernelTypes.Where(t=>InNamespace(t,rule.Namespace)&& !AllowedTypes.Contains(t.FullName)&& !CompilerGenerated(t)).OrderBy(t=>t.FullName,StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();if(offenders.Count==0)continue;if(rule.Enforced){violations.AddRange(offenders.Select(t=>"- "+t.FullName+": тип подсистемы остался в сборке ядра (namespace '"+t.Namespace+"'). "+rule.Note+" — перенесите тип в engine-мод; namespace '"+rule.Namespace+"' объявлен enforced."));}else{transition.Add("- "+rule.Namespace+": "+offenders.Count+" типов в ядре (переходное состояние, не enforced) — "+rule.Note);}}foreach(var line in transition)Console.WriteLine("[KERNEL_PURITY] transition: "+line);Assert.True(violations.Count==0,"Ядро содержит типы подсистем (сборка ядра должна быть только проигрывателем модов):\n"+string.Join("\n",violations)+"\nСписок запретов и allowlist — в KernelPurityTests.Rules / AllowedTypes.");}[Fact]public void KernelPurity_AllowlistIsCurrent(){var kernelTypeNames=KernelTypes().Select(t=>t.FullName).ToHashSet(StringComparer.Ordinal);var enforcedRules=Rules.Where(r=>r.Enforced).ToList();var problems=new List<string>();var notes=new List<string>();foreach(var allowed in AllowedTypes){if(kernelTypeNames.Contains(allowed))continue;var separator=allowed.LastIndexOf('.');var typeNamespace=separator>0?allowed[..separator]:allowed;var owningRule=enforcedRules.FirstOrDefault(r=>InNamespace(typeNamespace,r.Namespace));if(owningRule!=null){problems.Add("- "+allowed+": записи нет в сборке ядра, а namespace '"+owningRule.Namespace+"' уже enforced — удалите устаревшую запись из AllowedTypes.");}else{notes.Add("- "+allowed+": записи нет в сборке ядра (namespace ещё не enforced) — переходное состояние, "+"запись можно удалить вместе с переводом подсистемы в enforced.");}}foreach(var note in notes)Console.WriteLine("[KERNEL_PURITY] allowlist: "+note);Assert.True(problems.Count==0,"Allowlist чистоты ядра устарел:\n"+string.Join("\n",problems));}/// <summary> All types of the core assembly (the one that owns <c>Cthangover.Core.Settings.GameData</c>). Load errors are tolerated: only the types that resolved are scanned. </summary>
private static Type[]KernelTypes(){var assembly=typeof(Cthangover.Core.Settings.GameData).Assembly;try{return assembly.GetTypes();}catch(ReflectionTypeLoadException ex){return ex.Types.Where(t=>t!=null).Select(t=>t!).ToArray();}}/// <summary> True for compiler-generated closure and lambda classes (<c>&lt;&gt;c</c>, <c>&lt;Get...&gt;b__0</c>): they appear in any namespace that uses lambdas and are not types a developer placed there, so they never count as purity violations. </summary>
private static bool CompilerGenerated(Type type)=>type.FullName.Contains("<",StringComparison.Ordinal);/// <summary>True when the type's namespace equals <paramref name="ruleNamespace"/> or sits under it.</summary>
private static bool InNamespace(Type type,string ruleNamespace)=>InNamespace(type.Namespace,ruleNamespace);private static bool InNamespace(string typeNamespace,string ruleNamespace){if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(typeNamespace))return false;return typeNamespace==ruleNamespace||typeNamespace.StartsWith(ruleNamespace+".",StringComparison.Ordinal);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Arch/PathPortabilityTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Linq;using System.Text.Json;using System.Text.RegularExpressions;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Arch{/// <summary> Project assets must stay portable: no machine-specific absolute paths and no references whose casing differs from the file on disk. Windows forgives both, Linux/CI and exported packs do not — so these checks run in xUnit on every platform and fail on the author's machine first. <c>res://</c> references inside a mod resolve against the project root or the mod root, matching the TSCN resolver. </summary>
public class PathPortabilityTests{private static readonly string[]AssetExtensions={".tscn",".tres",".json",".scenario",".gd",".gdshader",".godot",".cfg",".csproj",".sln",};private static readonly Regex AbsolutePath=new(@"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])[A-Za-z]:(\\\\|\\|/)[A-Za-z]|(?<![\w.\-/])(/home/[a-z]|/Users/[A-Za-z]|/workspace/|/mnt/[a-z]/)",RegexOptions.Compiled);private static readonly Regex ResReference=new(@"res://([^""'\s)\]>,;]+)",RegexOptions.Compiled);[Fact]public void ProjectAssets_HaveNoAbsolutePaths(){var root=RepoPaths.FindRepoRoot();var problems=new List<string>();foreach(var file in AssetFiles(root)){var lines=File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(root,file));for(var i=0;i<lines.Length;i++){if(AbsolutePath.IsMatch(lines[i]))problems.Add($"{file}:{i + 1}: {lines[i].Trim()}");}}Assert.True(problems.Count==0,"machine-specific absolute paths (use res:// or a relative path):\n"+string.Join("\n",problems));}[Fact]public void ResReferences_ExistWithExactCase(){var root=RepoPaths.FindRepoRoot();var problems=new List<string>();foreach(var file in AssetFiles(root)){var modRoot=ModRootOf(file);var lines=File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(root,file));for(var i=0;i<lines.Length;i++){foreach(Match match in ResReference.Matches(lines[i])){var target=match.Groups[1].Value.Split("::")[0];if(target.Contains('*')||target.Contains('{'))continue;var candidates=modRoot==null?new[]{target}:new[]{target,$"{modRoot}/{target}"};if(candidates.Any(c=>RepoPaths.ExistsWithExactCase(root,c)))continue;var kind=candidates.Any(c=>RepoPaths.ExistsIgnoringCase(root,c))?"wrong case":"missing";problems.Add($"{file}:{i + 1}: res://{target} ({kind})");}}}Assert.True(problems.Count==0,"broken res:// references:\n"+string.Join("\n",problems));}[Fact]public void ModManifestScenePaths_ExistWithExactCase(){var root=RepoPaths.FindRepoRoot();var problems=new List<string>();foreach(var manifest in RepoPaths.ProjectFiles(root).Where(f=>Regex.IsMatch(f,@"^mods/[^/]+/manifest\.json$"))){var modRoot=ModRootOf(manifest);using var json=JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,manifest)));foreach(var section in new[]{"ui","main_menu"}){if(!json.RootElement.TryGetProperty(section,out var entries)||entries.ValueKind!=JsonValueKind.Array)continue;foreach(var entry in entries.EnumerateArray()){if(!entry.TryGetProperty("scene_path",out var scenePath))continue;var relative=$"{modRoot}/{scenePath.GetString()}";if(!RepoPaths.ExistsWithExactCase(root,relative)){var kind=RepoPaths.ExistsIgnoringCase(root,relative)?"wrong case":"missing";problems.Add($"{manifest}: {section}.scene_path '{scenePath.GetString()}' ({kind})");}}}}Assert.True(problems.Count==0,"broken manifest scene paths:\n"+string.Join("\n",problems));}[Fact]public void ProjectFiles_HaveNoCaseOnlyCollisions(){var root=RepoPaths.FindRepoRoot();var collisions=RepoPaths.ProjectFiles(root).GroupBy(f=>f,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).Where(g=>g.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count()>1).Select(g=>string.Join(" | ",g)).ToList();Assert.True(collisions.Count==0,"paths that differ only by case collide on Windows/macOS:\n"+string.Join("\n",collisions));}private static IEnumerable<string>AssetFiles(string root)=>RepoPaths.ProjectFiles(root).Where(f=>AssetExtensions.Contains(Path.GetExtension(f),StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase));/// <summary>Returns <c>mods/&lt;id&gt;</c> for files inside a mod, null otherwise.</summary>
private static string ModRootOf(string file){var parts=file.Split('/');return parts.Length>2&&parts[0]=="mods"?$"mods/{parts[1]}":null;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Arch/RepoPaths.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Linq;namespace Engine.Tests.Arch{/// <summary> Repository walking helpers shared by the path-portability tests: locating the repo root, enumerating project files outside tool and vendor folders, and checking a relative path with exact casing. </summary>
internal static class RepoPaths{/// <summary> Folders that hold tooling, caches, build output (releases/) or vendored code rather than project assets. Agent folders (.claude, .dsh) also hold symlinks made in WSL, which Windows cannot open; jobs/ is the host job bridge, whose results carry the host's absolute paths. </summary>
private static readonly HashSet<string>SkippedDirectories=new(StringComparer.Ordinal){".git",".godot",".idea",".opencode",".omo",".dsh",".claude","jobs","node_modules","bin","obj","sdk","site","mockups","gen","artifacts","releases","source_code","undefined",};public static string FindRepoRoot(){var dir=new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);while(dir!=null&& !File.Exists(Path.Combine(dir.FullName,"AGENTS.md")))dir=dir.Parent;return dir?.FullName??throw new InvalidOperationException("Repository root (AGENTS.md) not found");}/// <summary>Repo-relative, '/'-separated paths of every project file.</summary>
public static List<string>ProjectFiles(string root){var result=new List<string>();var pending=new Stack<string>();pending.Push(root);while(pending.Count>0){var dir=pending.Pop();foreach(var sub in Directory.EnumerateDirectories(dir)){if(!SkippedDirectories.Contains(Path.GetFileName(sub)))pending.Push(sub);}result.AddRange(Directory.EnumerateFiles(dir).Select(f=>Path.GetRelativePath(root,f).Replace('\\','/')));}return result;}public static bool ExistsWithExactCase(string root,string relative){var current=root;foreach(var segment in relative.Split('/',StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)){if(!Directory.Exists(current))return false;var match=Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(current).FirstOrDefault(e=>string.Equals(Path.GetFileName(e),segment,StringComparison.Ordinal));if(match==null)return false;current=match;}return true;}public static bool ExistsIgnoringCase(string root,string relative){var current=root;foreach(var segment in relative.Split('/',StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)){if(!Directory.Exists(current))return false;var match=Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(current).FirstOrDefault(e=>string.Equals(Path.GetFileName(e),segment,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));if(match==null)return false;current=match;}return true;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Arch/TestManifestPathsTests.cs =====
using System;using System.IO;using System.Linq;using System.Text.Json;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Arch{/// <summary> Every scene in <c>tests/tests.manifest.json</c> must exist with exactly the spelled casing. AppServices.Current.RegisterResettable(new RecordingResettable("after_publish"));Assert.Contains(AppServices.Current.Resettables,r=>r.Id=="after_publish");// A replacement instance starts from the map_zones default only.
