using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Threading.Tasks;using Cthangover.Core.Bootstrap;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Boot{/// <summary> Lifecycle contracts of the converted registries (auditor follow-up): imperative registrations vs forced rebuilds, and thread-safety of the AppServices registration loop. Deliberately no SceneDefinitions.Collect call here: outside the Godot runtime it would trigger a full lazy mod load; only the cheap Clear/Rebuild paths are exercised. </summary>
[Collection("appservices")]public class RegistryLifecycleTests{[Fact]public void SceneEventRegistry_RegisterAfterClear_WorksUntilNextRebuild(){var saved=AppServices.Current;try{AppServices.SetCurrent(new AppServices());SceneEventRegistry.Register(typeof(string),"lifecycle_scene");Assert.Single(SceneEventRegistry.GetEvents("lifecycle_scene"));// Clear wipes imperative registrations; the next GetEvents re-scans only the attribute-decorated types.
((IGameRegistry)AppServices.Current.SceneEvents).Clear();Assert.Empty(SceneEventRegistry.GetEvents("lifecycle_scene"));SceneEventRegistry.Register(typeof(string),"lifecycle_scene");Assert.Single(SceneEventRegistry.GetEvents("lifecycle_scene"));// A forced rebuild resets imperative registrations by contract (mod initializers must re-register afterwards).
AppServices.Current.SceneEvents.Rebuild();Assert.Empty(SceneEventRegistry.GetEvents("lifecycle_scene"));// Re-registration after a rebuild works exactly as before.
SceneEventRegistry.Register(typeof(string),"lifecycle_scene");Assert.Single(SceneEventRegistry.GetEvents("lifecycle_scene"));}finally{AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void SceneDefinitions_ClearAndFacadeIdentity_NoCollectOutsideRuntime(){var saved=AppServices.Current;try{AppServices.SetCurrent(new AppServices());var owned=AppServices.Current.SceneDefs;Assert.Equal("scene_defs",owned.Id);Assert.Contains(owned,AppServices.Current.Registries);// Clear and Rebuild paths stay no-throw outside the runtime (Collect itself is intentionally not called here: it would trigger a full lazy mod load in unit tests).
((IGameRegistry)owned).Clear();owned.Rebuild();}finally{AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public async Task RegisterResettable_ConcurrentWithReset_IsSnapshotSafe(){var saved=AppServices.Current;try{AppServices.SetCurrent(new AppServices());var ids=Enumerable.Range(0,200).Select(i=>"concurrent_"+i).ToList();var registerTasks=ids.Select(id=>Task.Run(()=>AppServices.Current.RegisterResettable(new CountingResettable(id)))).ToList();var resetTask=Task.Run(()=>{for(var i=0;i<20;i++)AppServices.Current.ResetForNewGame();});await Task.WhenAll(registerTasks.Concat(new[]{resetTask}));// Every registration survives the concurrent resets (the map_zones default + 200 concurrent ones) and the reset loop never threw (a thrown Task.WhenAll would fail this test).
Assert.Equal(201,AppServices.Current.Resettables.Count);}finally{AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}private sealed class CountingResettable:IResettable{public CountingResettable(string id){Id=id;}public string Id{get;}public void ResetForNewGame(){// Intentionally empty: the contract under test is the snapshot and registration loop, not any particular state owner.
}}}}

using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Bootstrap;namespace Engine.Tests.Boot{/// <summary> Contract tests for IResettable registration through AppServices.RegisterResettable — the entry point a mod's IModInitializer uses to hook new-game state resets — plus the duplicate-Id dedup rules of the registration list. <para/> Same static-state discipline as AppServicesTests: fresh SetCurrent per test inside the shared "appservices" collection, restore in finally. </summary>

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Characters/StubCharacterTemplateProvider.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Characters;namespace Engine.Tests.Characters{/// <summary> Deterministic <see cref="ICharacterTemplateProvider"/> for unit tests around the party roster. Every ID resolves to a small template with preset stats, except the IDs listed in <see cref="UnknownIds"/> — they model the "typo in scenario content" case that the refusal contract in <see cref="CharacterData.AddCharacterToParty"/> must survive. Copy semantics of the real factory are irrelevant here: the roster code treats the returned <see cref="Character"/> as read-only source data for the runtime card. </summary>
public class StubCharacterTemplateProvider:ICharacterTemplateProvider{/// <summary>IDs that resolve to null — missing declarations or typos.</summary>
public HashSet<string>UnknownIds{get;}=new HashSet<string>();/// <summary>Health value stamped into every declared template; tests assert it reaches the roster card.</summary>
public const int TemplateHealth=42;/// <inheritdoc />
public Character Get(string id){if(UnknownIds.Contains(id))return null;return new Character{ID=id,Name=id,Level=3,Exp=30,Attributes=new CharacterAttributes{Health=new Cthangover.Core.Characters.Attribute{Value=TemplateHealth,BaseValue=TemplateHealth},},};}}}

namespace Cthangover.Tests.Events{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Events;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Unload;using Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload;/// <summary>Typed subscriptions: explicit unsubscribe and the unload pass by code ownership.</summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class ModEventBusTests{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace BusMod
{
    public static class Handlers
    {
        public static int Hits;

        public static void OnDefeated(Cthangover.Core.Events.CharactersDefeatedEvent e)
        {
            if (e.Source == ""bus_test"")
                Hits++;
        }
    }
}
";private int probeHits;private void OnProbe(BusProbeEvent e)=>probeHits++;[Fact]public void Unsubscribe_StopsDelivery(){ModEventBus.Subscribe<BusProbeEvent>(OnProbe);ModEventBus.Publish(new BusProbeEvent());ModEventBus.Unsubscribe<BusProbeEvent>(OnProbe);ModEventBus.Publish(new BusProbeEvent());Assert.Equal(1,probeHits);}[Fact]public void LifecycleEvent_OrdersByPriority_KeepingSubscriptionOrderForTies(){var log=new List<int>();var probes=new List<BusOrderProbe>();for(var i=0;i<40;i++){var probe=new BusOrderProbe(i,log);probes.Add(probe);ModEventBus.Subscribe<BusLifecycleProbeEvent>(probe.Handle,priority:i%3);}ModEventBus.Publish(new BusLifecycleProbeEvent());foreach(var probe in probes)ModEventBus.Unsubscribe<BusLifecycleProbeEvent>(probe.Handle);var expected=Enumerable.Range(0,40).OrderBy(i=>i%3).ToList();Assert.Equal(expected,log);}[Fact]public void RemoveOwned_DropsHandlersOfTheDyingAssembly(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_bus");var mod=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);var handlers=mod.GetType("BusMod.Handlers");var handler=(Action<CharactersDefeatedEvent>)Delegate.CreateDelegate(typeof(Action<CharactersDefeatedEvent>),handlers.GetMethod("OnDefeated"));var hits=handlers.GetField("Hits",BindingFlags.Public|BindingFlags.Static);try{ModEventBus.Subscribe(handler);ModEventBus.Publish(new CharactersDefeatedEvent{Source="bus_test"});var removed=ModEventBus.RemoveOwned(new AssemblyOwnership(new[]{mod}));ModEventBus.Publish(new CharactersDefeatedEvent{Source="bus_test"});Assert.Equal(1,removed);Assert.Equal(1,(int)hits.GetValue(null));}finally{ModEventBus.Unsubscribe(handler);ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}}[Fact]public void RemoveOwned_EmptyOwnership_RemovesNothing(){Assert.Equal(0,ModEventBus.RemoveOwned(new AssemblyOwnership(null)));Assert.Equal(0,ModEventBus.RemoveOwned(null));}}}

namespace Cthangover.Tests.Games{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Games;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;/// <summary>A computed mod-set choice becomes the process-wide active game only when applied.</summary>

void Collection_DeserializesEntrySettingsCoverAndFrontendKind(){const string json=@"{ ""id"": ""g"", ""kind"": ""frontend"", ""entry"": { ""scene"": ""intro"" },

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismCoreTestAccess.cs =====
using System;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Safe (pointer-free) access to the native CubismCore arrays of a loaded model for unit tests. The mod's own bindings return raw float*/IntPtr-pointer arrays, which cannot be dereferenced from safe test code, so these P/Invoke declarations use IntPtr and marshal through Marshal.Copy/Marshal.ReadIntPtr. Signatures mirror CubismCoreBindings one-to-one; the native library is resolved by the DllImport resolver installed in Live2DTestRuntime. </summary>
internal static class CubismCoreTestAccess{[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetParameterIds(IntPtr model);[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetParameterValues(IntPtr model);[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetParameterMinimumValues(IntPtr model);[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetParameterMaximumValues(IntPtr model);[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetPartIds(IntPtr model);[DllImport("Live2DCubismCore",CallingConvention=CallingConvention.StdCall)]private static extern IntPtr csmGetPartOpacities(IntPtr model);/// <summary>Reads a copy of the current parameter values of the model.</summary>
public static float[]ReadParameterValues(CubismNativeModel model)=>ReadFloats(csmGetParameterValues(model.ModelPtr),model.ParameterCount);/// <summary>Reads a copy of the parameter minimum bounds of the model.</summary>
public static float[]ReadParameterMinimums(CubismNativeModel model)=>ReadFloats(csmGetParameterMinimumValues(model.ModelPtr),model.ParameterCount);/// <summary>Reads a copy of the parameter maximum bounds of the model.</summary>
public static float[]ReadParameterMaximums(CubismNativeModel model)=>ReadFloats(csmGetParameterMaximumValues(model.ModelPtr),model.ParameterCount);/// <summary>Reads a copy of the current part opacities of the model.</summary>
public static float[]ReadPartOpacities(CubismNativeModel model)=>ReadFloats(csmGetPartOpacities(model.ModelPtr),model.GetPartCount());/// <summary>Writes one parameter value back into the native model.</summary>
public static void WriteParameterValue(CubismNativeModel model,int parameterIndex,float value){var values=ReadParameterValues(model);values[parameterIndex]=value;Marshal.Copy(values,0,csmGetParameterValues(model.ModelPtr),values.Length);}/// <summary>Reads one parameter value from the native model.</summary>
public static float ReadParameterValue(CubismNativeModel model,int parameterIndex)=>ReadParameterValues(model)[parameterIndex];/// <summary>Returns all parameter ids of the model, in native order.</summary>
public static string[]GetParameterIds(CubismNativeModel model){var idsPtr=csmGetParameterIds(model.ModelPtr);var ids=new string[model.ParameterCount];for(int i=0;i<ids.Length;i++)ids[i]=Marshal.PtrToStringAnsi(Marshal.ReadIntPtr(idsPtr,i*IntPtr.Size));return ids;}/// <summary>Returns all part ids of the model, in native order.</summary>
public static string[]GetPartIds(CubismNativeModel model){var idsPtr=csmGetPartIds(model.ModelPtr);var count=model.GetPartCount();var ids=new string[count];for(int i=0;i<count;i++)ids[i]=Marshal.PtrToStringAnsi(Marshal.ReadIntPtr(idsPtr,i*IntPtr.Size));return ids;}/// <summary>Resolves a parameter id to its native index, or -1 when absent.</summary>
public static int FindParameterIndex(CubismNativeModel model,string parameterId){var ids=GetParameterIds(model);for(int i=0;i<ids.Length;i++){if(ids[i]==parameterId)return i;}return-1;}/// <summary>Resolves a part id to its native index, or -1 when absent.</summary>
public static int FindPartIndex(CubismNativeModel model,string partId){var ids=GetPartIds(model);for(int i=0;i<ids.Length;i++){if(ids[i]==partId)return i;}return-1;}private static float[]ReadFloats(IntPtr ptr,int count){var values=new float[count];Marshal.Copy(ptr,values,0,count);return values;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismExpressionBlendTests.cs =====
using System;using System.IO;using System.Text;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Unit tests for expression (.exp3.json) parsing and the three blend formulas against the native Cubism model (Haru.moc3 fixture). The expected values are computed independently from the SDK 5-r.5 semantics of CubismExpressionMotion::DoUpdateParameters combined with CubismModel::Set/Add/MultiplyParameterValue: <list type="bullet"> <item>Additive: clamp(value + expValue * weight)</item> <item>Multiply: clamp(value * (1 + (expValue - 1) * weight))</item> <item>Overwrite: value * (1 - weight) + clamp(expValue) * weight</item> </list> The clamp bounds are the model parameter's [min, max] read from the native core. </summary>
public class CubismExpressionBlendTests{private const float FloatTolerance=0.0001f;private const string TestParameterId="ParamMouthForm";// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Synthetic expressions on the real Haru model ------------------------------------------------------------------ <summary>Additive with a mid weight adds expValue * weight.</summary>
[Fact]public void Additive_AppliesFormula(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,0.5f,"Add"),TestParameterId,v0:0.2f,weight:0.5f);Assert.Equal(Clamp(0.2f+0.5f*0.5f,GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId)),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Additive results clamp to the parameter maximum.</summary>
[Fact]public void Additive_ClampsToParameterMaximum(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,0.5f,"Add"),TestParameterId,v0:0.95f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(GetMax(TestParameterId),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Additive results clamp to the parameter minimum.</summary>
[Fact]public void Additive_ClampsToParameterMinimum(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,-0.5f,"Add"),TestParameterId,v0:-0.95f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(GetMin(TestParameterId),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Additive with weight 1 and zero expression value keeps the value.</summary>
[Fact]public void Additive_WeightOne_ZeroValue_KeepsValue(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,0f,"Add"),TestParameterId,v0:0.3f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(0.3f,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Multiply with a mid weight follows value * (1 + (expValue - 1) * weight).</summary>
[Fact]public void Multiply_AppliesFormula(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,1.2f,"Multiply"),TestParameterId,v0:0.5f,weight:0.5f);Assert.Equal(Clamp(0.5f*(1f+0.2f*0.5f),GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId)),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Multiply with expValue = 1 is an exact identity.</summary>
[Fact]public void Multiply_ValueOne_IsIdentity(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,1f,"Multiply"),TestParameterId,v0:0.35f,weight:0.7f);Assert.Equal(0.35f,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Multiply overshoot clamps to the parameter maximum.</summary>
[Fact]public void Multiply_ClampsToParameterMaximum(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,3f,"Multiply"),TestParameterId,v0:0.5f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(GetMax(TestParameterId),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Overwrite lerps between the current value and the clamped expression value.</summary>
[Fact]public void Overwrite_AppliesLerp(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,-0.2f,"Overwrite"),TestParameterId,v0:0.8f,weight:0.25f);float expected=0.8f*0.75f+Clamp(-0.2f,GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId))*0.25f;Assert.Equal(expected,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Overwrite clamps the expression value itself, not the lerp result.</summary>
[Fact]public void Overwrite_ClampsExpressionValue(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,99f,"Overwrite"),TestParameterId,v0:0.6f,weight:0.5f);float expected=0.6f*0.5f+GetMax(TestParameterId)*0.5f;Assert.Equal(expected,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Overwrite with weight 1 assigns the clamped expression value.</summary>
[Fact]public void Overwrite_WeightOne_AssignsClampedValue(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,-99f,"Overwrite"),TestParameterId,v0:0.6f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(GetMin(TestParameterId),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>A missing Blend key behaves as Additive (SDK ExpressionBlendType default).</summary>
[Fact]public void MissingBlend_FallsBackToAdditive(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,0.4f,null),TestParameterId,v0:0.1f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(Clamp(0.1f+0.4f,GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId)),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>An unknown Blend tag also behaves as Additive (SDK Parse switch default).</summary>
[Fact]public void UnknownBlend_FallsBackToAdditive(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson(TestParameterId,0.4f,"Weird"),TestParameterId,v0:0.1f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(Clamp(0.1f+0.4f,GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId)),actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Expression entries targeting unknown parameter ids are silently skipped.</summary>
[Fact]public void UnknownParameterId_IsIgnored(){float actual=ApplySingle(ExpJson("Param__Does__Not__Exist",1f,"Overwrite"),TestParameterId,v0:0.42f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(0.42f,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>With several entries for one id the later entry wins (apply order).</summary>
[Fact]public void LastEntryWinsForSameId(){var entries=new[]{new{Id=TestParameterId,Value=0.3f,Blend="Add"},new{Id=TestParameterId,Value= -0.2f,Blend="Overwrite"},};string json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(new{FadeInTime=0.5f,Parameters=entries});float actual=ApplySingle(json,TestParameterId,v0:0f,weight:1f);Assert.Equal(Clamp(-0.2f,GetMin(TestParameterId),GetMax(TestParameterId)),actual,FloatTolerance);}// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Real SDK sample expressions ------------------------------------------------------------------ <summary>F01.exp3.json: Add blend applies with the SDK formula on every entry.</summary>
[Fact]public void RealFixture_F01_Additive(){ApplyRealFixture("F01.exp3.json",v0:0f,weight:1f);}/// <summary>F04.exp3.json: Multiply blend applies with the SDK formula on every entry.</summary>
[Fact]public void RealFixture_F04_Multiply(){ApplyRealFixture("F04.exp3.json",v0:0.8f,weight:1f);}/// <summary>F06.exp3.json: Multiply with value 2 exercises the max clamp on eye parameters.</summary>
[Fact]public void RealFixture_F06_MultiplyClamp(){ApplyRealFixture("F06.exp3.json",v0:0.5f,weight:1f);}/// <summary> Applies a real exp3.json to the Haru model and checks every listed entry against the independently computed SDK expectation. </summary>
private void ApplyRealFixture(string fileName,float v0,float weight){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,"Haru","expressions",fileName));using var document=JsonDocument.Parse(bytes);var parameters=document.RootElement.GetProperty("Parameters");using var model=Live2DTestRuntime.LoadHaruModel();var minimums=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterMinimums(model);var maximums=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterMaximums(model);var values=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterValues(model);// Seed every target parameter with the deterministic start value.
foreach(var entry in parameters.EnumerateArray()){int index=CubismCoreTestAccess.FindParameterIndex(model,entry.GetProperty("Id").GetString());Assert.True(index>=0,"Parameter missing in Haru.moc3");values[index]=v0;CubismCoreTestAccess.WriteParameterValue(model,index,v0);}var expression=new CubismExpressionMotion(bytes);expression.DoUpdateParameters(model,0f,weight,null);foreach(var entry in parameters.EnumerateArray()){string id=entry.GetProperty("Id").GetString();float expValue=entry.GetProperty("Value").GetSingle();string blend=entry.TryGetProperty("Blend",out var b)?b.GetString():null;int index=CubismCoreTestAccess.FindParameterIndex(model,id);float actual=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterValue(model,index);float expected;switch(blend){case"Multiply":expected=Clamp(v0*(1f+(expValue-1f)*weight),minimums[index],maximums[index]);break;case"Overwrite":expected=v0*(1f-weight)+Clamp(expValue,minimums[index],maximums[index])*weight;break;default:// "Add" and the SDK default
expected=Clamp(v0+expValue*weight,minimums[index],maximums[index]);break;}Assert.True(MathF.Abs(expected-actual)<=FloatTolerance,$"{fileName} [{id}]: expected {expected}, got {actual}");}}// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------ <summary>Builds a minimal .exp3.json with a single parameter entry.</summary>
private static string ExpJson(string id,float value,string blend){var blendPart=blend==null?string.Empty:", \"Blend\": \""+blend+"\"";return"{ \"FadeInTime\": 0.5, \"Parameters\": [ { \"Id\": \""+id+"\", \"Value\": "+value.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)+blendPart+" } ] }";}private static float GetMin(string id)=>LoadBounds(id).min;private static float GetMax(string id)=>LoadBounds(id).max;private static(float min,float max)LoadBounds(string id){using var model=Live2DTestRuntime.LoadHaruModel();int index=CubismCoreTestAccess.FindParameterIndex(model,id);Assert.True(index>=0,id+" not found in Haru.moc3");return(CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterMinimums(model)[index],CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterMaximums(model)[index]);}/// <summary> Loads the Haru model, seeds the target parameter with v0, applies the expression with the given fade weight and returns the parameter value. </summary>
private static float ApplySingle(string expJson,string targetId,float v0,float weight){using var model=Live2DTestRuntime.LoadHaruModel();int index=CubismCoreTestAccess.FindParameterIndex(model,targetId);Assert.True(index>=0,targetId+" not found in Haru.moc3");CubismCoreTestAccess.WriteParameterValue(model,index,v0);var expression=new CubismExpressionMotion(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(expJson));expression.DoUpdateParameters(model,0f,weight,null);return CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadParameterValue(model,index);}private static float Clamp(float value,float min,float max){if(value<min)return min;if(value>max)return max;return value;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismModelMatrixTests.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Unit tests for CubismModelMatrix against the Cubism SDK 5-r.5 source Framework/src/Math/CubismModelMatrix.cpp. Key semantics under test: SetWidth/SetHeight MULTIPLY the scale, the (width, height) constructor performs SetHeight(2), translation helpers are incremental, and the alignment helpers offset by the full scaled canvas size. </summary>
public class CubismModelMatrixTests{private const float FloatTolerance=0.0001f;/// <summary>The default constructor is the identity transform.</summary>
[Fact]public void DefaultCtor_IsIdentity(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix();Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>The canvas constructor calls SetHeight(2), which for a 2-high canvas yields scale 1.</summary>
[Fact]public void CanvasCtor_AppliesSetHeightTwo(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>SetWidth multiplies the current scale (SDK Scale semantics).</summary>
[Fact]public void SetWidth_MultipliesScale(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.SetWidth(2f);// scale *= 2/4
Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);matrix.SetWidth(4f);// scale *= 4/4 — no change, proving multiply not assign
Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>SetHeight multiplies the current scale.</summary>
[Fact]public void SetHeight_MultipliesScale(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.SetHeight(1f);// scale *= 1/2
Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>An unknown canvas size makes SetWidth/SetHeight no-ops (SDK divide guard).</summary>
[Fact]public void UnknownCanvas_IgnoresWidthHeight(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix();matrix.SetWidth(3f);matrix.SetHeight(3f);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Top/Left are incremental translations (SDK SetY/SetX add).</summary>
[Fact]public void TopAndLeft_AreIncremental(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.Top(1f);matrix.Top(1f);matrix.Left(0.5f);matrix.Left(0.5f);Assert.Equal(2f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Bottom offsets by y minus the scaled canvas height, incrementally.</summary>
[Fact]public void Bottom_UsesScaledCanvasHeight(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.Bottom(0f);// translateY += 0 - (2 * 1)
Assert.Equal(-2f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);matrix.Bottom(0f);// second call adds again
Assert.Equal(-4f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Right offsets by x minus the scaled canvas width.</summary>
[Fact]public void Right_UsesScaledCanvasWidth(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.Right(0f);Assert.Equal(-4f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>CenterX/CenterY offset by the point minus half the scaled canvas size.</summary>
[Fact]public void Center_UsesHalfScaledCanvas(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.CenterX(0f);matrix.CenterY(0f);Assert.Equal(-2f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(-1f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>SetPosition adds to the current translation.</summary>
[Fact]public void SetPosition_IsIncremental(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.SetPosition(1f,2f);matrix.SetPosition(1f,2f);Assert.Equal(2f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(4f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>SetCenterPosition centers on both axes.</summary>
[Fact]public void SetCenterPosition_CombinesCenterXAndY(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.SetCenterPosition(0f,0f);Assert.Equal(-2f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(-1f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary> SetupFromLayout applies width/height (scale) before the position keys, and matches the lowercased SDK layout keys. </summary>
[Fact]public void SetupFromLayout_AppliesScaleThenPosition(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);var layout=new Dictionary<string,float>{{"width",8f},// scale *= 8/4
{"y",1f},{"center_x",0f},// translateX += 0 - (4 * 2)/2 = -4
};matrix.SetupFromLayout(layout);Assert.Equal(2f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(2f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,matrix.TranslateY,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(-4f,matrix.TranslateX,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Scale multiplies both axes directly.</summary>
[Fact]public void Scale_MultipliesBothAxes(){var matrix=new CubismModelMatrix(4f,2f);matrix.Scale(0.5f,2f);Assert.Equal(0.5f,matrix.ScaleX,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(2f,matrix.ScaleY,FloatTolerance);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismMotionParseTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Unit tests for motion3.json parsing and curve evaluation against the Cubism SDK 5-r.5 native sources (Framework/src/Motion/CubismMotion.cpp and Framework/src/Math/CubismMath.cpp). Real sample motion files from the SDK are used as fixtures, and meta counts are re-derived from the raw JSON with an independent segment scanner instead of trusting the mod. </summary>
public class CubismMotionParseTests{private const float FloatTolerance=0.0001f;public static TheoryData<string>SampleMotionRelativePaths=>new TheoryData<string>{Path.Combine("Haru","motions","haru_g_idle.motion3.json"),Path.Combine("Haru","motions","haru_g_m01.motion3.json"),Path.Combine("Haru","motions","haru_g_m04.motion3.json"),Path.Combine("Hiyori","motions","Hiyori_m01.motion3.json"),Path.Combine("Wanko","motions","idle_01.motion3.json"),Path.Combine("Mark","motions","mark_m01.motion3.json"),};// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Parsing: meta block versus independent raw-JSON scan ------------------------------------------------------------------ <summary> CubismMotionJson meta getters must agree with the raw motion3.json for duration, fps, loop, bezier restriction and the fade defaults. </summary>
[Theory][MemberData(nameof(SampleMotionRelativePaths))]public void CubismMotionJson_MetaGetters_MatchRawJson(string relativePath){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,relativePath));var json=CubismMotionJsonTestsHelper.ParseRawJson(bytes);var motionJson=new CubismMotionJson(bytes);Assert.Equal(json.Meta.Duration,motionJson.GetDuration(),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(json.Meta.Fps,motionJson.GetFps(),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(json.Meta.Loop,motionJson.IsLoop());Assert.Equal(json.Meta.AreBeziersRestricted,motionJson.AreBeziersRestricted());Assert.Equal(json.Meta.CurveCount,motionJson.GetCurveCount());Assert.Equal(json.Meta.TotalSegmentCount,motionJson.GetTotalSegmentCount());Assert.Equal(json.Meta.TotalPointCount,motionJson.GetTotalPointCount());Assert.Equal(json.Meta.UserDataCount,motionJson.GetEventCount());// FadeInTime is optional per motion; the SDK getter returns -1 when absent (CubismMotionJson.cpp GetMotionFadeInTimeValue).
if(json.Meta.FadeInTime==null)Assert.Equal(-1f,motionJson.GetFadeInTime(),FloatTolerance);else Assert.Equal(json.Meta.FadeInTime.Value,motionJson.GetFadeInTime(),FloatTolerance);if(json.Meta.FadeOutTime==null)Assert.Equal(-1f,motionJson.GetFadeOutTime(),FloatTolerance);else Assert.Equal(json.Meta.FadeOutTime.Value,motionJson.GetFadeOutTime(),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary> The mod-reported segment and point counts must equal an independent scan over every curve's Curves[].Segments array. Layout per the native parse loop (CubismMotion.cpp ~line 720): the first two floats are the start point (time, value) — there is no leading point index — and each segment is a type marker plus data: bezier (type 1) adds 3 points over 7 floats, linear/stepped/inverseStepped (0/2/3) add 1 point over 3. </summary>
[Theory][MemberData(nameof(SampleMotionRelativePaths))]public void CubismMotionJson_SegmentAndPointCounts_MatchIndependentScan(string relativePath){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,relativePath));var json=CubismMotionJsonTestsHelper.ParseRawJson(bytes);var motionJson=new CubismMotionJson(bytes);int totalSegments=0;int totalPoints=0;for(int c=0;c<json.Meta.CurveCount;c++){var segments=json.Curves[c].Segments;int pointCount=1;int segmentCount=0;int i=2;// first two floats are the start point (time, value)
while(i<segments.Length){int type=(int)segments[i];if(type==1){pointCount+=3;i+=7;}else if(type==0||type==2||type==3){pointCount+=1;i+=3;}else{throw new InvalidOperationException($"Unknown segment type {type} at index {i} of curve {c}");}segmentCount++;}Assert.Equal(segments.Length,i);// scanner must consume the full array
totalSegments+=segmentCount;totalPoints+=pointCount;}Assert.Equal(totalSegments,motionJson.GetTotalSegmentCount());Assert.Equal(totalPoints,motionJson.GetTotalPointCount());}/// <summary>Looping motions report duration -1 for GetDuration but keep the loop duration.</summary>
[Theory][MemberData(nameof(SampleMotionRelativePaths))]public void CubismMotion_LoopDuration_MatchesRawMeta(string relativePath){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,relativePath));var json=CubismMotionJsonTestsHelper.ParseRawJson(bytes);var motion=new CubismMotion(bytes);if(json.Meta.Loop){Assert.Equal(-1f,motion.GetDuration(),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(json.Meta.Duration,motion.GetLoopDuration(),FloatTolerance);}else{Assert.Equal(json.Meta.Duration,motion.GetDuration(),FloatTolerance);}}/// <summary>mark_m01 carries user data events that GetFiredEvent returns in order.</summary>
[Fact]public void CubismMotion_GetFiredEvent_MarkSample(){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,"Mark","motions","mark_m01.motion3.json"));var motion=new CubismMotion(bytes);// Event window semantics of CubismMotion::GetFiredEvent: before < t <= after.
var fired=motion.GetFiredEvent(-1f,1.133f+0.001f);Assert.Single(fired);Assert.Equal("timing1",fired[0]);fired=motion.GetFiredEvent(1.133f+0.001f,3.667f+0.001f);Assert.Single(fired);Assert.Equal("timing2",fired[0]);// Nothing fires again inside the same window.
fired=motion.GetFiredEvent(3.667f+0.001f,3.7f);Assert.Empty(fired);}// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Segment parsing and evaluation on synthetic curves ------------------------------------------------------------------ <summary>Linear evaluation is clamped at the segment start and exact at the end.</summary>
[Fact]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_Linear(){// point0 (t=0,v=0) + linear segment to (t=1,v=10)
float[]raw={0f,0f,0f,1f,10f};Assert.Equal(0f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,0f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(5f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,0.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1f),FloatTolerance);// Times outside [0,1] clamp at the segment bounds.
Assert.Equal(0f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,-0.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1.5f),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary> Unrestricted bezier segments go through the Cardano closed-form solution (CubismMotion.cpp BezierEvaluateCardanoInterpretation). The mod result is compared against an independent transcription of the C++ Cardano solver (CubismMath.cpp CardanoAlgorithmForBezier + de Casteljau at the solved t). </summary>
[Theory][InlineData(0f)][InlineData(0.13f)][InlineData(0.5f)][InlineData(0.77f)][InlineData(1f)]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_BezierCardano_MatchesCppTranscription(float time){// ParseSegmentsInternal layout: start point (0,0), bezier marker, control points (0.2,0.8)/(0.8,0.8), end point (1,1).
float[]raw={0f,0f,1f,0.2f,0.8f,0.8f,0.8f,1f,1f};float actual=MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,time,false,1f,false);float expected=MotionTestHelper.BezierCardanoEvaluate(raw,time);Assert.Equal(expected,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary> When beziers are restricted the SDK falls back to binary search sampling (CubismMotion.cpp BezierEvaluateBinarySearch: 20 iterations, x error 0.01, interval halving of the control polygon). The mod result is compared to an exact transcription of that algorithm. </summary>
[Theory][InlineData(0.1f)][InlineData(0.5f)][InlineData(0.9f)]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_BezierRestricted_MatchesBinarySearchTranscription(float time){// Start point (0,0), bezier marker, control points (0.5,0)/(0.5,1), end point (1,1) — monotone in x as the binary search requires.
float[]raw={0f,0f,1f,0.5f,0f,0.5f,1f,1f,1f};float actual=MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,time,false,1f,true);float expected=MotionTestHelper.BezierBinarySearchEvaluate(raw,time);Assert.Equal(expected,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Stepped segments hold the previous value until the next key time.</summary>
[Fact]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_Stepped(){// (0,0) stepped to (1,10): value jumps exactly at t=1.
float[]raw={0f,0f,2f,1f,10f};Assert.Equal(0f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,0.999f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1f),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Inverse stepped segments jump to the target value immediately.</summary>
[Fact]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_InverseStepped(){float[]raw={0f,0f,3f,1f,10f};Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,0.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1f),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Evaluation past the last segment returns the last point value.</summary>
[Fact]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_BeyondLastSegment_ReturnsLastValue(){float[]raw={0f,0f,0f,1f,5f,2f,3f,10f};Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,3f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,42f),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>A three-segment curve evaluates each piece between its key times.</summary>
[Fact]public void EvaluateCurveInternal_MultiSegment(){// linear to (1,5), stepped to (2,7), linear to (3,0)
float[]raw={0f,0f,0f,1f,5f,2f,2f,7f,0f,3f,0f};Assert.Equal(2.5f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,0.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(5f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(5f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,1.9f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(7f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,2f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(3.5f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,2.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(raw,3f),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary> Loop correction (CubismMotion.cpp CorrectEndPoint, V2 behavior when the motion loops with isCorrection enabled): the segment is re-evaluated against the loop end time endTime = duration + deltaTimeSeconds. </summary>
[Fact]public void CorrectEndPointInternal_LoopCorrection(){float duration=1f;float frame=1f/60f;float endTime=duration+1f/30f;// Linear loop correction at the frame after the end interpolates from the last value (10) toward the looped first value (0) placed at endTime.
float[]linear={0f,0f,0f,1f,10f};float corrected=MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(linear,duration+frame,true,endTime,false);Assert.Equal(5f,corrected,FloatTolerance);// A stepped final segment keeps its value under correction.
float[]stepped={0f,0f,2f,1f,10f};Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(stepped,duration+frame,true,endTime,false),FloatTolerance);// An inverse stepped final segment has already jumped to the next value.
float[]inverse={0f,0f,3f,1f,10f};Assert.Equal(0f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(inverse,duration+frame,true,endTime,false),FloatTolerance);// At or after the loop end time the last value is returned.
Assert.Equal(10f,MotionTestHelper.Evaluate(linear,endTime,true,endTime,false),FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Expression motions default to a 1 second fade on both sides.</summary>
[Fact]public void CubismExpressionMotion_DefaultFadeTime(){var bytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Live2DTestRuntime.SdkResources,"Haru","expressions","F01.exp3.json"));var expression=new CubismExpressionMotion(bytes);Assert.Equal(1f,expression.FadeInSeconds,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,expression.FadeOutSeconds,FloatTolerance);}}/// <summary> Raw motion3.json access and independent transcriptions of the C++ math used by EvaluateCurveInternal (CubismMath.cpp + CubismMotion.cpp, SDK 5-r.5). </summary>
internal static class MotionTestHelper{private const float MathEpsilon=0.00001f;public static float Evaluate(float[]rawSegments,float time,bool isCorrection=false,float endTime=1f,bool restricted=false){var points=new List<CubismMotionPoint>();var segments=new List<MotionSegmentData>();var curve=new MotionCurveData();curve.SegmentCount=CubismMotion.ParseSegmentsInternal(rawSegments,points,segments);return CubismMotion.EvaluateCurveInternal(curve,points.ToArray(),segments.ToArray(),time,isCorrection,endTime,restricted);}private static float RangeF(float value,float min,float max){if(value<min)return min;if(value>max)return max;return value;}/// <summary>Transcription of CubismMath.cpp QuadraticEquation (lines 104-120).</summary>
private static float QuadraticEquation(float a,float b,float c){if(MathF.Abs(a)<MathEpsilon)return MathF.Abs(b)<MathEpsilon? -c:-c/b;return-(b+MathF.Sqrt(b*b-4f*a*c))/(2f*a);}/// <summary>Transcription of CubismMath.cpp CardanoAlgorithmForBezier (lines 128-191).</summary>
private static float CardanoAlgorithmForBezier(float a,float b,float c,float d){if(MathF.Abs(a)<MathEpsilon)return RangeF(QuadraticEquation(b,c,d),0f,1f);float ba=b/a;float ca=c/a;float da=d/a;float p=(3f*ca-ba*ba)/3f;float p3=p/3f;float q=(2f*ba*ba*ba-9f*ba*ca+27f*da)/27f;float q2=q/2f;float discriminant=q2*q2+p3*p3*p3;const float center=0.5f;const float threshold=center+0.01f;if(discriminant<0f){float mp3= -p/3f;float mp33=mp3*mp3*mp3;float r=MathF.Sqrt(mp33);float t= -q/(2f*r);float cosphi=RangeF(t,-1f,1f);float phi=MathF.Acos(cosphi);float crtr=MathF.Cbrt(r);float t1=2f*crtr;float root1=t1*MathF.Cos(phi/3f)-ba/3f;if(MathF.Abs(root1-center)<threshold)return RangeF(root1,0f,1f);float root2=t1*MathF.Cos((phi+2f*MathF.PI)/3f)-ba/3f;if(MathF.Abs(root2-center)<threshold)return RangeF(root2,0f,1f);float root3=t1*MathF.Cos((phi+4f*MathF.PI)/3f)-ba/3f;return RangeF(root3,0f,1f);}if(discriminant==0f){float u1=q2<0f?MathF.Cbrt(-q2):-MathF.Cbrt(q2);float root1=2f*u1-ba/3f;if(MathF.Abs(root1-center)<threshold)return RangeF(root1,0f,1f);float root2= -u1-ba/3f;return RangeF(root2,0f,1f);}float sd=MathF.Sqrt(discriminant);float uc=MathF.Cbrt(sd-q2);float vc=MathF.Cbrt(sd+q2);return RangeF(uc-vc-ba/3f,0f,1f);}/// <summary> Independent bezier evaluation: solve t with the C++ Cardano solver, then de Casteljau (mirrors BezierEvaluateCardanoInterpretation). raw = [x1,v1, 1, cx1,cy1, cx2,cy2, x2,v2] (ParseSegmentsInternal layout). </summary>
public static float BezierCardanoEvaluate(float[]raw,float time){float x1=raw[0];float cx1=raw[3];float cx2=raw[5];float x2=raw[7];float a=x2-3f*cx2+3f*cx1-x1;float b=3f*cx2-6f*cx1+3f*x1;float c=3f*cx1-3f*x1;float d=x1-time;float t=CardanoAlgorithmForBezier(a,b,c,d);return BezierDeCasteljau(raw,t);}/// <summary> Transcription of CubismMotion.cpp BezierEvaluateBinarySearch (20 iterations, x error tolerance 0.01, interval halving of the time control polygon, then de Casteljau over the original points). </summary>
public static float BezierBinarySearchEvaluate(float[]raw,float time){const float xError=0.01f;float x=time;float x1=raw[0];float x2=raw[7];float cx1=raw[3];float cx2=raw[5];float ta=0f;float tb=1f;float t=0f;int i=0;for(;i<20;i++){if(x<x1+xError){t=ta;break;}if(x2-xError<x){t=tb;break;}float centerx=(cx1+cx2)*0.5f;cx1=(x1+cx1)*0.5f;cx2=(x2+cx2)*0.5f;float ctrlx12=(cx1+centerx)*0.5f;float ctrlx21=(cx2+centerx)*0.5f;centerx=(ctrlx12+ctrlx21)*0.5f;if(x<centerx){tb=(ta+tb)*0.5f;if(centerx-xError<x){t=tb;break;}x2=centerx;cx2=ctrlx12;}else{ta=(ta+tb)*0.5f;if(x<centerx+xError){t=ta;break;}x1=centerx;cx1=ctrlx21;}}if(i==20)t=(ta+tb)*0.5f;if(t<0f)t=0f;if(t>1f)t=1f;return BezierDeCasteljau(raw,t);}/// <summary>De Casteljau over the 9-float ParseSegmentsInternal bezier layout.</summary>
private static float BezierDeCasteljau(float[]raw,float t){var p0=new CubismMotionPoint{Time=raw[0],Value=raw[1]};var p1=new CubismMotionPoint{Time=raw[3],Value=raw[4]};var p2=new CubismMotionPoint{Time=raw[5],Value=raw[6]};var p3=new CubismMotionPoint{Time=raw[7],Value=raw[8]};var p01=Lerp(p0,p1,t);var p12=Lerp(p1,p2,t);var p23=Lerp(p2,p3,t);var p012=Lerp(p01,p12,t);var p123=Lerp(p12,p23,t);return Lerp(p012,p123,t).Value;}private static CubismMotionPoint Lerp(CubismMotionPoint a,CubismMotionPoint b,float t){return new CubismMotionPoint{Time=a.Time+(b.Time-a.Time)*t,Value=a.Value+(b.Value-a.Value)*t,};}}/// <summary>Raw motion3.json reader for building independent expectations.</summary>
internal static class CubismMotionJsonTestsHelper{public sealed class RawCurve{public string Target{get;set;}public string Id{get;set;}public float[]Segments{get;set;}}public sealed class math (CubismPhysics.cpp). Kept in a separate static class so the test methods stay free of support noise. </summary>
internal static class PhysicsTranscription{private static float DegreesToRadian(float degrees)=>(degrees/180f)*MathF.PI;private static float DirectionToRadian(float fromX,float fromY,float toX,float toY){float ret=MathF.Atan2(toY,toX)-MathF.Atan2(fromY,fromX);while(ret< -MathF.PI)ret+=MathF.PI*2f;while(ret>MathF.PI)ret-=MathF.PI*2f;return ret;}private static void Normalize(ref float x,ref float y){float length=MathF.Pow(x*x+y*y,0.5f);x=x/length;y=y/length;}/// <summary>Transcription of CubismPhysics::UpdateParticles (lines 277-352).</summary>
public static void UpdateParticles(CubismPhysicsParticle[]strand,int baseParticleIndex,int strandCount,float totalTranslationX,float totalTranslationY,float totalAngle,float windX,float windY,float thresholdValue,float deltaTimeSeconds,float airResistance){strand[baseParticleIndex].PositionX=totalTranslationX;strand[baseParticleIndex].PositionY=totalTranslationY;float totalRadian=DegreesToRadian(totalAngle);float currentGravityX=MathF.Sin(totalRadian);float currentGravityY=MathF.Cos(totalRadian);Normalize(ref currentGravityX,ref currentGravityY);for(int i=1;i<strandCount;++i){int idx=baseParticleIndex+i;strand[idx].ForceX=currentGravityX*strand[idx].Acceleration+windX;strand[idx].ForceY=currentGravityY*strand[idx].Acceleration+windY;strand[idx].LastPositionX=strand[idx].PositionX;strand[idx].LastPositionY=strand[idx].PositionY;float delay=strand[idx].Delay*deltaTimeSeconds*30f;float directionX=strand[idx].PositionX-strand[idx-1].PositionX;float directionY=strand[idx].PositionY-strand[idx-1].PositionY;float radian=DirectionToRadian(strand[idx].LastGravityX,strand[idx].LastGravityY,currentGravityX,currentGravityY)/airResistance;// Native quirk: Y is rotated from the already-updated X.
float newDirectionX=MathF.Cos(radian)*directionX-directionY*MathF.Sin(radian);directionY=MathF.Sin(radian)*newDirectionX+directionY*MathF.Cos(radian);directionX=newDirectionX;strand[idx].PositionX=strand[idx-1].PositionX+directionX;strand[idx].PositionY=strand[idx-1].PositionY+directionY;float velocityX=strand[idx].VelocityX*delay;float velocityY=strand[idx].VelocityY*delay;float forceX=strand[idx].ForceX*delay*delay;float forceY=strand[idx].ForceY*delay*delay;strand[idx].PositionX=strand[idx].PositionX+velocityX+forceX;strand[idx].PositionY=strand[idx].PositionY+velocityY+forceY;float deltaX=strand[idx].PositionX-strand[idx-1].PositionX;float deltaY=strand[idx].PositionY-strand[idx-1].PositionY;Normalize(ref deltaX,ref deltaY);strand[idx].PositionX=strand[idx-1].PositionX+deltaX*strand[idx].Radius;strand[idx].PositionY=strand[idx-1].PositionY+deltaY*strand[idx].Radius;if(MathF.Abs(strand[idx].PositionX)<thresholdValue)strand[idx].PositionX=0f;if(delay!=0f){strand[idx].VelocityX=strand[idx].PositionX-strand[idx].LastPositionX;strand[idx].VelocityY=strand[idx].PositionY-strand[idx].LastPositionY;strand[idx].VelocityX/=delay;strand[idx].VelocityY/=delay;strand[idx].VelocityX*=strand[idx].Mobility;strand[idx].VelocityY*=strand[idx].Mobility;}strand[idx].ForceX=0f;strand[idx].ForceY=0f;strand[idx].LastGravityX=currentGravityX;strand[idx].LastGravityY=currentGravityY;}}/// <summary>Transcription of CubismPhysics::UpdateParticlesForStabilization (lines 354-396).</summary>
public static void UpdateParticlesForStabilization(CubismPhysicsParticle[]strand,int baseParticleIndex,int strandCount,float totalTranslationX,float totalTranslationY,float totalAngle,float windX,float windY,float thresholdValue){strand[baseParticleIndex].PositionX=totalTranslationX;strand[baseParticleIndex].PositionY=totalTranslationY;float totalRadian=DegreesToRadian(totalAngle);float currentGravityX=MathF.Sin(totalRadian);float currentGravityY=MathF.Cos(totalRadian);Normalize(ref currentGravityX,ref currentGravityY);for(int i=1;i<strandCount;++i){int idx=baseParticleIndex+i;strand[idx].ForceX=currentGravityX*strand[idx].Acceleration+windX;strand[idx].ForceY=currentGravityY*strand[idx].Acceleration+windY;strand[idx].LastPositionX=strand[idx].PositionX;strand[idx].LastPositionY=strand[idx].PositionY;strand[idx].VelocityX=0f;strand[idx].VelocityY=0f;float forceX=strand[idx].ForceX;float forceY=strand[idx].ForceY;Normalize(ref forceX,ref forceY);forceX*=strand[idx].Radius;forceY*=strand[idx].Radius;strand[idx].PositionX=strand[idx-1].PositionX+forceX;strand[idx].PositionY=strand[idx-1].PositionY+forceY;if(MathF.Abs(strand[idx].PositionX)<thresholdValue)strand[idx].PositionX=0f;strand[idx].ForceX=0f;strand[idx].ForceY=0f;strand[idx].LastGravityX=currentGravityX;strand[idx].LastGravityY=currentGravityY;}}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismPoseFadeTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Text;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Unit tests for the pose fade math and the part-visibility state machine against the Cubism SDK 5-r.5 source Framework/src/Effect/CubismPose.cpp (Phi = 0.5 crossing, back opacity threshold 0.15). The E2E tests run the real pose update on the native Haru model. </summary>
public class CubismPoseFadeTests{private const float FloatTolerance=0.0001f;/// <summary>Visible opacity grows by deltaTime / fadeTime and clamps at 1.</summary>
[Fact]public void ComputeVisibleOpacity_AddsDeltaTimeRatio(){float actual=CubismPose.ComputeVisibleOpacity(0.4f,0.1f,0.5f);Assert.Equal(0.6f,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Visible opacity saturates at 1.</summary>
[Fact]public void ComputeVisibleOpacity_ClampsAtOne(){Assert.Equal(1f,CubismPose.ComputeVisibleOpacity(1f,0.5f,0.5f),FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(1f,CubismPose.ComputeVisibleOpacity(0.9f,1f,0.5f),FloatTolerance);}/** * Hidden opacity hand-computed from the SDK piecewise formula: * a1 = newOpacity < Phi ? newOpacity * (Phi - 1) / Phi + 1 * : (1 - newOpacity) * Phi / (1 - Phi); * backOpacity = (1 - a1) * (1 - newOpacity); * if (backOpacity > 0.15) a1 = 1 - 0.15 / (1 - newOpacity); */[Theory][InlineData(0.25f,0.8f)][InlineData(0.5f,0.7f)][InlineData(0.75f,0.4f)][InlineData(0.9f,0.1f)][InlineData(1.0f,0.0f)][InlineData(0.4f,0.75f)]public void ComputeHiddenOpacity_MatchesSdkFormula(float newOpacity,float expected){float actual=CubismPose.ComputeHiddenOpacity(newOpacity);Assert.Equal(expected,actual,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>A synthetic pose file parses without throwing (fade time + groups).</summary>
[Fact]public void Ctor_ParsesSyntheticPose(){byte[]json=Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("{ \"FadeInTime\": 0.5, \"Groups\": [ [ { \"Id\": \"PartA\" }, { \"Id\": \"PartB\" } ] ] }");var pose=new CubismPose(json);Assert.NotNull(pose);}/// <summary> E2E on the native Haru model: the first UpdateParameters runs the SDK Reset — the first group part becomes fully visible, the second hidden. </summary>
[Fact]public void UpdateParameters_Reset_FirstGroupPartVisible(){using var model=Live2DTestRuntime.LoadHaruModel();var partIds=CubismCoreTestAccess.GetPartIds(model);Assert.True(model.GetPartCount()>=2,"Haru model needs at least two parts");byte[]json=Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("{ \"FadeInTime\": 0.5, \"Groups\": [ ["+" { \"Id\": \""+partIds[0]+"\" },"+" { \"Id\": \""+partIds[1]+"\" } ] ] }");var pose=new CubismPose(json);pose.UpdateParameters(model,0.1f);float[]opacities=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadPartOpacities(model);Assert.Equal(1f,opacities[0],FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,opacities[1],FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Parts referenced through the Link list copy the owner part opacity.</summary>
[Fact]public void UpdateParameters_Link_CopiesOpacity(){using var model=Live2DTestRuntime.LoadHaruModel();var partIds=CubismCoreTestAccess.GetPartIds(model);Assert.True(model.GetPartCount()>=3,"Haru model needs at least three parts");byte[]json=Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("{ \"FadeInTime\": 0.5, \"Groups\": [ ["+" { \"Id\": \""+partIds[0]+"\", \"Link\": [ \""+partIds[2]+"\" ] },"+" { \"Id\": \""+partIds[1]+"\" } ] ] }");var pose=new CubismPose(json);pose.UpdateParameters(model,0.1f);float[]opacities=CubismCoreTestAccess.ReadPartOpacities(model);Assert.Equal(1f,opacities[0],FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,opacities[1],FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(opacities[0],opacities[2],FloatTolerance);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/CubismTargetPointTests.cs =====
using System;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Unit tests for CubismTargetPoint against an exact transcription of the Cubism SDK 5-r.5 source Math/CubismTargetPoint.cpp (velocity follower with capped acceleration in 30 fps frame units). </summary>
public class CubismTargetPointTests{private const float FloatTolerance=0.0001f;/// <summary>The SDK Epsilon: motion below this distance is suppressed.</summary>
private const float Epsilon=0.01f;/// <summary>SDK MaxV per internal frame = (40 / 10) / FrameRate.</summary>
private const float MaxVPerFrame=4f/30f;/// <summary>The very first Update only anchors the clock and does not move.</summary>
[Fact]public void FirstUpdate_AnchorsClockOnly(){var point=new CubismTargetPoint();point.Set(1f,1f);point.Update(1f/30f);Assert.Equal(0f,point.X,FloatTolerance);Assert.Equal(0f,point.Y,FloatTolerance);}/// <summary>Per-frame displacement never exceeds the SDK maximum speed.</summary>
[Fact]public void PerFrameSpeed_CappedAtMaxV(){var point=new CubismTargetPoint();point.Set(1f,0f);float maxDelta=0f;float previousX=point.X;for(int frame=0;frame<300;frame++){point.Update(1f/30f);maxDelta=MathF.Max(maxDelta,MathF.Abs(point.X-previousX));previousX=point.X;}Assert.True(maxDelta<=MaxVPerFrame+0.0001f,$"per-frame displacement {maxDelta} exceeds MaxV {MaxVPerFrame}");}/// <summary>The follower converges onto the target and then stays still.</summary>
[Fact]public void ConvergesToTarget_ThenStays(){var point=new CubismTargetPoint();point.Set(1f,0f);for(int frame=0;frame<1200;frame++)point.Update(1f/30f);Assert.True(MathF.Abs(point.X-1f)<=Epsilon,$"X={point.X}");Assert.True(MathF.Abs(point.Y)<=Epsilon,$"Y={point.Y}");// With the target reached the update returns early and the point rests.
point.Set(0f,0f);for(int frame=0;frame<300;frame++)point.Update(1f/30f);Assert.True(MathF.Abs(point.X)<=Epsilon,$"rest X={point.X}");}/// <summary> Two hundred frames with target changes and irregular frame times follow the C++ transcription frame by frame (tolerance 1e-5). </summary>
[Fact]public void Lockstep_MatchesCppTranscription(){var point=new CubismTargetPoint();var reference=new TargetPointSim();float[]frameTimes=new float[200];for(int frame=0;frame<frameTimes.Length;frame++)frameTimes[frame]=1f/30f;frameTimes[80]=0.5f;// frame rate spike
frameTimes[150]=0.5f;// frame rate spike
for(int frame=0;frame<frameTimes.Length;frame++){if(frame==50){point.Set(0.8f,-0.6f);reference.Set(0.8f,-0.6f);}if(frame==120){point.Set(-0.4f,0.9f);reference.Set(-0.4f,0.9f);}point.Update(frameTimes[frame]);reference.Update(frameTimes[frame]);Assert.True(MathF.Abs(reference.X-point.X)<=0.00001f,$"frame {frame}: X {point.X} vs reference {reference.X}");Assert.True(MathF.Abs(reference.Y-point.Y)<=0.00001f,$"frame {frame}: Y {point.Y} vs reference {reference.Y}");}}}/// <summary> Exact transcription of CubismTargetPoint.cpp (SDK 5-r.5) used as the independent reference implementation in the lockstep test. </summary>
internal sealed class TargetPointSim{private const int FrameRate=30;private const float Epsilon=0.01f;private float _faceTargetX,_faceTargetY;private float _faceX,_faceY;private float _faceVX,_faceVY;private float _lastTimeSeconds;private float _userTimeSeconds;public float X=>_faceX;public float Y=>_faceY;public void Set(float x,float y){_faceTargetX=x;_faceTargetY=y;}public void Update(float deltaTimeSeconds){_userTimeSeconds+=deltaTimeSeconds;const float FaceParamMaxV=40f/10f;float MaxV=FaceParamMaxV*1f/FrameRate;if(_lastTimeSeconds==0f){_lastTimeSeconds=_userTimeSeconds;return;}float deltaTimeWeight=(_userTimeSeconds-_lastTimeSeconds)*FrameRate;_lastTimeSeconds=_userTimeSeconds;const float TimeToMaxSpeed=0.15f;const float FrameToMaxSpeed=TimeToMaxSpeed*FrameRate;float MaxA=deltaTimeWeight*MaxV/FrameToMaxSpeed;float dx=_faceTargetX-_faceX;float dy=_faceTargetY-_faceY;if(MathF.Abs(dx)<=Epsilon&&MathF.Abs(dy)<=Epsilon)return;float d=MathF.Sqrt((dx*dx)+(dy*dy));float vx=MaxV*dx/d;float vy=MaxV*dy/d;float ax=vx-_faceVX;float ay=vy-_faceVY;float a=MathF.Sqrt((ax*ax)+(ay*ay));if(a< -MaxA||a>MaxA){ax*=MaxA/a;ay*=MaxA/a;}_faceVX+=ax;_faceVY+=ay;{float maxV=0.5f*(MathF.Sqrt((MaxA*MaxA)+16f*MaxA*d-8f*MaxA*d)-MaxA);float curV=MathF.Sqrt((_faceVX*_faceVX)+(_faceVY*_faceVY));if(curV>maxV){_faceVX*=maxV/curV;_faceVY*=maxV/curV;}}_faceX+=_faceVX;_faceY+=_faceVY;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Live2D/Live2DTestRuntime.cs =====
using System;using System.IO;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;using Cthangover.Live2D.Cubism.Framework;namespace Engine.Tests.Live2D{/// <summary> Test environment for the Live2D unit tests. Locates the repository root on disk (the unit runner may start with an arbitrary working directory) and makes the native CubismCore library resolvable for P/Invoke calls: at game runtime Live2DCubismCore.dll is placed next to the mod binaries, while in the unit test process a DllImport resolver (registered from this module initializer for both the Live2D assembly and the test assembly) loads it directly from mods/live2d_base/native. Without the resolver, tests that exercise the real native core (Haru.moc3 fixture) would fail with DllNotFoundException. </summary>
internal static class Live2DTestRuntime{private static string _repoRoot;/// <summary>Absolute path of the repository root (the directory holding AGENTS.md).</summary>
public static string RepoRoot=>_repoRoot??=FindRepoRoot();/// <summary>Root of the Cubism SDK sample resources used as test fixtures.</summary>
public static string SdkResources=>Path.Combine(RepoRoot,"sdk","CubismSdkForNative-5-r.5","Samples","Resources");/// <summary> Absolute path of the native CubismCore library for the current OS: the one shipped with the mod when present, otherwise the SDK build (the mod ships only the Windows DLL; Linux/macOS take the SDK's). </summary>
public static string NativeCorePath{get{string fileName,sdkDir;if(OperatingSystem.IsWindows())(fileName,sdkDir)=("Live2DCubismCore.dll",Path.Combine("windows","x86_64"));else if(OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())(fileName,sdkDir)=("libLive2DCubismCore.dylib","macos");else(fileName,sdkDir)=("libLive2DCubismCore.so",Path.Combine("linux","x86_64"));var shipped=Path.Combine(RepoRoot,"mods","live2d_base","native",fileName);if(File.Exists(shipped))return shipped;return Path.Combine(RepoRoot,"sdk","CubismSdkForNative-5-r.5","Core","dll",sdkDir,fileName);}}[ModuleInitializer]internal static void Init(){// Registers the resolver for BOTH the Live2D assembly (mod P/Invokes) and the test assembly (CubismCoreTestAccess P/Invokes).
RegisterResolver(typeof(CubismNativeModel).Assembly);RegisterResolver(typeof(Live2DTestRuntime).Assembly);}private static void RegisterResolver(System.Reflection.Assembly assembly){try{NativeLibrary.SetDllImportResolver(assembly,(libraryName,assemblyPath,searchPath)=>{if(string.Equals(libraryName,"Live2DCubismCore",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)&&File.Exists(NativeCorePath)){return NativeLibrary.Load(NativeCorePath);}return IntPtr.Zero;// fall back to default probing
});}catch(InvalidOperationException){// A resolver is already registered for this assembly — nothing to do.
}}/// <summary> Loads the Haru sample model through the real mod pipeline (native CubismCore). The caller owns the returned instance; it is IDisposable. </summary>
public static CubismNativeModel LoadHaruModel(){var mocBytes=File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(SdkResources,"Haru","Haru.moc3"));var model=new CubismNativeModel();model.LoadMoc(mocBytes);return model;}private static string FindRepoRoot(){var dir=new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);while(dir!=null){if(File.Exists(Path.Combine(dir.FullName,"AGENTS.md")))return dir.FullName;dir=dir.Parent;}throw new InvalidOperationException("Repository root (AGENTS.md) not found walking up from "+AppContext.BaseDirectory);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Menu/MenuRegistryTests.cs =====
using System;using Cthangover.Core.Bootstrap;using Cthangover.Core.UI.Menu;using Godot;namespace Engine.Tests.Menu{/// <summary> Unit tests for <see cref="MenuRegistry"/>: runtime button/panel registration, validation and duplicate skipping, ordering, lookup and the Rebuild/Clear lifecycle. Every test publishes a fresh AppServices instance (Save → SetCurrent → restore) and clears the registry it touched, so no state leaks into other tests through the static facade. <para/> Manifest collection is not exercised here: without a loaded ModManager, Rebuild collects an empty manifest part, which the lifecycle tests rely on directly. </summary>
[Collection("appservices")]public class MenuRegistryTests{private static MenuButtonEntry Button(string id,int index=10,string section=null,string locale="ui/test",string scenePath="scenes/test/button.tscn")=>new MenuButtonEntry{Id=id,ModId="test_mod",Section=section,Locale=locale,Index=index,ScenePath=scenePath,};private static MenuPanelEntry Panel(string id,string scenePath="scenes/test/panel.tscn")=>new MenuPanelEntry{Id=id,ModId="test_mod",ScenePath=scenePath};// ---- IGameRegistry contract ----------------------------------------
[Fact]public void RegistryId_IsMenus(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{Assert.Equal("menus",((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Id);}finally{AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void FreshRegistry_IsEmptyAndRebuildIsIdempotent(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;// Cheap ctor: nothing collected, nothing registered.
Assert.Empty(menus.Buttons);Assert.Empty(menus.Panels);menus.Rebuild();menus.Rebuild();// No mods are loaded in unit tests, so the manifest part stays empty and Rebuild is safe to repeat.
Assert.Empty(menus.Buttons);Assert.Empty(menus.Panels);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}// ---- RegisterButton -------------------------------------------------
[Fact]public void RegisterButton_ValidEntry_IsListedAndFindable(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var entry=Button("test_mod/start");services.Menus.RegisterButton(entry);Assert.Single(services.Menus.Buttons);Assert.Same(entry,services.Menus.Buttons[0]);var found=services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/start");Assert.NotNull(found);Assert.Same(entry,found);// Lookup is case-insensitive like the other registries.
Assert.Same(entry,services.Menus.GetButton("TEST_MOD/START"));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetButton("missing"));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetButton(null));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetButton(""));}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void RegisterButton_DefaultsSectionAndModOrder(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{// Section null → the mod block; runtime entries keep order -1 so equal-index registrations sort before manifest entries.
var entry=Button("test_mod/no_section",section:null);services.Menus.RegisterButton(entry);Assert.Equal(MenuButtonEntry.SectionMods,entry.Section);Assert.Equal(-1,entry.ModOrderIndex);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void RegisterButton_HandlerOnlyEntry_IsValid(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{Action<Control>handler=control=>{};var entry=new MenuButtonEntry{Id="test_mod/exit",Locale="ui/test/exit",Handler=handler,};services.Menus.RegisterButton(entry);Assert.Same(handler,services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/exit")?.Handler);Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/exit").ScenePath);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void RegisterButton_InvalidEntries_AreSkipped(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;menus.RegisterButton(null);menus.RegisterButton(Button(null));menus.RegisterButton(Button("   "));menus.RegisterButton(Button("no_locale",locale:null));menus.RegisterButton(Button("no_locale_empty",locale:""));// Neither a scene nor a handler.
menus.RegisterButton(Button("no_binding",scenePath:null));Assert.Empty(menus.Buttons);Assert.Null(menus.GetButton("no_locale"));Assert.Null(menus.GetButton("no_binding"));}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void RegisterButton_DuplicateId_KeepsFirstRegistration(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var first=Button("test_mod/one",scenePath:"scenes/test/first.tscn");var second=Button("test_mod/one",scenePath:"scenes/test/second.tscn");services.Menus.RegisterButton(first);services.Menus.RegisterButton(second);Assert.Single(services.Menus.Buttons);Assert.Same(first,services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/one"));Assert.Equal("scenes/test/first.tscn",services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/one").ScenePath);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void Buttons_AreSortedByIndexThenId(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;menus.RegisterButton(Button("test_mod/middle",index:20));menus.RegisterButton(Button("test_mod/first",index:10));menus.RegisterButton(Button("test_mod/last",index:100));// Equal index breaks the tie on id, case-insensitively.

[Fact]public void RegisterPanel_ValidEntry_IsListedAndFindable(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var entry=Panel("test_mod/settings");services.Menus.RegisterPanel(entry);Assert.Single(services.Menus.Panels);Assert.Same(entry,services.Menus.GetPanel("test_mod/settings"));Assert.Same(entry,services.Menus.GetPanel("TEST_MOD/SETTINGS"));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetPanel("missing"));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetPanel(null));Assert.Null(services.Menus.GetPanel(""));}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void RegisterPanel_InvalidOrDuplicateEntries_AreSkipped(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;menus.RegisterPanel(null);menus.RegisterPanel(Panel(null));menus.RegisterPanel(Panel("no_scene",scenePath:null));menus.RegisterPanel(Panel("blank_scene",scenePath:" "));var first=Panel("test_mod/one",scenePath:"scenes/test/first.tscn");menus.RegisterPanel(first);menus.RegisterPanel(Panel("test_mod/one",scenePath:"scenes/test/second.tscn"));Assert.Single(menus.Panels);Assert.Equal("scenes/test/first.tscn",menus.GetPanel("test_mod/one").ScenePath);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void InstantiatePanel_UnknownId_ReturnsNull(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{Assert.Null(services.Menus.InstantiatePanel("missing"));Assert.Null(services.Menus.InstantiatePanel(null));}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}// ---- Lifecycle ------------------------------------------------------
[Fact]public void Rebuild_KeepsRuntimeRegistrations(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;var button=Button("test_mod/persistent");var panel=Panel("test_mod/panel");menus.RegisterButton(button);menus.RegisterPanel(panel);menus.Rebuild();// Rebuild re-reads only the manifest part; runtime entries registered by mod initializers survive it.
Assert.Same(button,menus.GetButton("test_mod/persistent"));Assert.Same(panel,menus.GetPanel("test_mod/panel"));Assert.Single(menus.Buttons);Assert.Single(menus.Panels);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}[Fact]public void Clear_DropsRuntimeRegistrationsToo(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var menus=services.Menus;menus.RegisterButton(Button("test_mod/button"));menus.RegisterPanel(Panel("test_mod/panel"));((IGameRegistry)menus).Clear();Assert.Empty(menus.Buttons);Assert.Empty(menus.Panels);Assert.Null(menus.GetButton("test_mod/button"));Assert.Null(menus.GetPanel("test_mod/panel"));// Clear returns the registry to the just-constructed state: it builds again cleanly afterwards.
menus.EnsureBuilt();menus.RegisterButton(Button("test_mod/button2"));Assert.Single(menus.Buttons);}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}// ---- Static facade --------------------------------------------------
[Fact]public void Facade_WorksExactlyOverAppServicesCurrentMenus(){var saved=AppServices.Current;var services=new AppServices();AppServices.SetCurrent(services);try{var button=Button("test_mod/facade_button");var panel=Panel("test_mod/facade_panel");// Registration through the static facade lands in the AppServices-owned registry...
MenuRegistry.RegisterMenuButton(button);MenuRegistry.RegisterMenuPanel(panel);Assert.Same(button,services.Menus.GetButton("test_mod/facade_button"));Assert.Same(panel,services.Menus.GetPanel("test_mod/facade_panel"));Assert.Same(button,MenuRegistry.GetButtonById("test_mod/facade_button"));Assert.Contains(MenuRegistry.AllButtons,b=>b.Id=="test_mod/facade_button");Assert.Contains(MenuRegistry.AllPanels,p=>p.Id=="test_mod/facade_panel");// ...and clearing the owned registry through IGameRegistry is observed by the facade: the facade and the owned instance are one and the same object.
((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();Assert.Empty(MenuRegistry.AllButtons);Assert.Empty(MenuRegistry.AllPanels);Assert.Null(MenuRegistry.GetButtonById("test_mod/facade_button"));Assert.Null(MenuRegistry.GetPanelById("test_mod/facade_panel"));}finally{((IGameRegistry)services.Menus).Clear();AppServices.SetCurrent(saved);}}}}

using Cthangover.Core.UI.Messages;namespace Engine.Tests.Messages{public class NotificationBufferTests{public NotificationBufferTests(){NotificationBuffer.Clear();}// ── Add ──
[Fact]public void Add_ValidText_EnqueuesAndIncreasesPendingCount(){NotificationBuffer.Add("Test notification");Assert.Equal(1,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void Add_MultipleTexts_AllQueued(){NotificationBuffer.Add("First");NotificationBuffer.Add("Second");NotificationBuffer.Add("Third");Assert.Equal(3,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void Add_NullText_IsIgnored(){NotificationBuffer.Add(null);Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void Add_EmptyString_IsIgnored(){NotificationBuffer.Add("");Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void Add_WhitespaceString_IsAccepted(){NotificationBuffer.Add("   ");Assert.Equal(1,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}// ── TryDequeue ──
[Fact]public void TryDequeue_BufferHasEntry_ReturnsTrueAndText(){NotificationBuffer.Add("Hello");var result=NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out var text);Assert.True(result);Assert.Equal("Hello",text);}[Fact]public void TryDequeue_BufferEmpty_ReturnsFalseAndNullText(){var result=NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out var text);Assert.False(result);Assert.Null(text);}[Fact]public void TryDequeue_PreservesFifoOrder(){NotificationBuffer.Add("A");NotificationBuffer.Add("B");NotificationBuffer.Add("C");NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out var first);NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out var second);NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out var third);Assert.Equal("A",first);Assert.Equal("B",second);Assert.Equal("C",third);}[Fact]public void TryDequeue_AfterDequeue_DecreasesPendingCount(){NotificationBuffer.Add("A");NotificationBuffer.Add("B");NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out _);Assert.Equal(1,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out _);Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}// ── PendingCount ──
[Fact]public void PendingCount_EmptyBuffer_ReturnsZero(){Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void PendingCount_AfterClear_ReturnsZero(){NotificationBuffer.Add("A");NotificationBuffer.Add("B");NotificationBuffer.Clear();Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}[Fact]public void PendingCount_AfterDequeueAll_ReturnsZero(){NotificationBuffer.Add("A");NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out _);Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}// ── Clear ──
[Fact]public void Clear_WithPendingItems_EmptiesBuffer(){NotificationBuffer.Add("A");NotificationBuffer.Add("B");NotificationBuffer.Clear();Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);Assert.False(NotificationBuffer.TryDequeue(out _));}[Fact]public void Clear_OnEmptyBuffer_DoesNotThrow(){NotificationBuffer.Clear();Assert.Equal(0,NotificationBuffer.PendingCount);}}}

using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Tests the online collection merge rules: catalogs publishing the same collection id collapse into the newest version, and every publisher is recorded so the UI can show provenance. </summary>
public class CatalogCollectionMergerTests{[Fact]public void TagSource_AddsUrlOnce(){var collection=new ModCollection{Id="x"};CatalogCollectionMerger.TagSource(collection,"https://a/catalog.json");CatalogCollectionMerger.TagSource(collection,"https://a/catalog.json");CatalogCollectionMerger.TagSource(collection,"https://b/catalog.json");Assert.Equal(2,collection.SourceUrls.Count);Assert.Equal("https://a/catalog.json",collection.SourceUrls[0]);Assert.Equal("https://b/catalog.json",collection.SourceUrls[1]);}[Fact]public void Merge_SameId_KeepsNewestVersionAndMergesSources(){var older=new ModCollection{Id="card_novel",Version="1.0.0",SourceUrls=new List<string>{"https://a/catalog.json"},};var newer=new ModCollection{Id="card_novel",Version="2.0.0",SourceUrls=new List<string>{"https://b/catalog.json"},};var merged=CatalogCollectionMerger.MergeAndDeduplicate(new List<ModCollection>{older,newer});var collection=Assert.Single(merged);Assert.Equal("2.0.0",collection.Version);Assert.Equal(2,collection.SourceUrls.Count);Assert.Contains("https://a/catalog.json",collection.SourceUrls);Assert.Contains("https://b/catalog.json",collection.SourceUrls);}[Fact]public void Merge_NewestFirst_StillKeepsNewest(){var newer=new ModCollection{Id="x",Version="3.0"};var older=new ModCollection{Id="x",Version="1.0"};var merged=CatalogCollectionMerger.MergeAndDeduplicate(new List<ModCollection>{newer,older});var collection=Assert.Single(merged);Assert.Equal("3.0",collection.Version);}[Fact]public void Merge_DifferentIds_AllKept(){var a=new ModCollection{Id="a",Version="1.0"};var b=new ModCollection{Id="b",Version="1.0"};var merged=CatalogCollectionMerger.MergeAndDeduplicate(new List<ModCollection>{a,b});Assert.Equal(2,merged.Count);}[Fact]public void Merge_NullEmptyAndIdlessEntries_AreIgnored(){var merged=CatalogCollectionMerger.MergeAndDeduplicate(new List<ModCollection>{null,new ModCollection{Name="no id"},new ModCollection{Id="",Version="1.0"},new ModCollection{Id="valid",Version="1.0"},});var collection=Assert.Single(merged);Assert.Equal("valid",collection.Id);}[Fact]public void Merge_NullInput_ReturnsEmpty(){Assert.Empty(CatalogCollectionMerger.MergeAndDeduplicate(null));}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/CatalogMergerTests.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Tests the catalog merge rules: provenance tagging, cross-catalog deduplication and — most importantly — the variant-conflict rule. When the same mod id/version/title appears with different content hashes, the variants must survive as separate entries so the manager can warn the player instead of silently merging (or picking) one of them. </summary>
public class CatalogMergerTests{private static CatalogExportEntry Entry(string id,string version,string name,string installHash,string source){return new using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Consumers;using Xunit;namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Consumers{/// <summary> Consumer registry: discovery of kernel consumers, order, id dedup and failure isolation for both fan-out directions. </summary>

{
    public class Marker
    {
        public string Hello() => ""world"";

        public static void Boom()
        {
            throw new System.InvalidOperationException(""boom"");
        }
