markerType=(Type)probe.GetMethod("MarkerType").Invoke(null,null);Assert.Same(production.GetType("TestBatchMod.Marker"),markerType);Assert.Equal("world",probe.GetMethod("Call").Invoke(null,null));}finally{ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}}[Fact]public void ArtifactsOf_TracksTheFilesAContextRuns(){var modId="test_batch_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0,12);try{var assembly=CompileInBatch(modId);Assert.Equal(new[]{assembly.Location},ModAssemblyLoader.ArtifactsOf(modId));Assert.True(ModAssemblyLoader.IsArtifactLoaded(assembly.Location));ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);Assert.Empty(ModAssemblyLoader.ArtifactsOf(modId));Assert.False(ModAssemblyLoader.IsArtifactLoaded(assembly.Location));}finally{ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}}[Fact]public void StagePrecompiledDlls_ContentAddressed_LoadStagedIntoTheModContext(){var modId="test_dll_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0,12);var root=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"cthangover_test_mod_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(root,"dll"));try{var mod=new ModInfo{Id=modId,Name=modId,Manifest=new ModManifest{Id=modId},FileProvider=new FolderModFileProvider(root,modId)};EmitLibrary(modId+"_lib","public class LibV1 { }",Path.Combine(root,"dll","Lib.dll"));var staged=ModAssemblyLoader.StagePrecompiledDlls(new[]{mod});var stagedAgain=ModAssemblyLoader.StagePrecompiledDlls(new[]{mod});Assert.Single(staged);Assert.Equal(modId,staged[0].ModId);Assert.StartsWith(modId+"_dll_",Path.GetFileName(staged[0].CachedPath));Assert.EndsWith("_Lib.dll",staged[0].CachedPath);Assert.True(File.Exists(staged[0].CachedPath));Assert.Equal(staged,stagedAgain);EmitLibrary(modId+"_lib","public class LibV2 { }",Path.Combine(root,"dll","Lib.dll"));Assert.NotEqual(staged[0].CachedPath,ModAssemblyLoader.StagePrecompiledDlls(new[]{mod})[0].CachedPath);using(ModAssemblyLoader.BeginBatch()){var loaded=ModAssemblyLoader.LoadStagedDlls(staged);Assert.Single(loaded);Assert.Equal(modId,loaded[0].ModId);Assert.True(loaded[0].Assembly.IsCollectible);Assert.NotNull(loaded[0].Assembly.GetType("LibV1"));}Assert.True(ModAssemblyLoader.IsArtifactLoaded(staged[0].CachedPath));}finally{ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);Directory.Delete(root,recursive:true);}}private static MetadataReference ToReference(string path)=>MetadataReference.CreateFromFile(path);private static void EmitLibrary(string assemblyName,string source,string path){var trustedPlatform=((string)AppContext.GetData("TRUSTED_PLATFORM_ASSEMBLIES")).Split(Path.PathSeparator).Select(ToReference);var compilation=CSharpCompilation.Create(assemblyName,new[]{CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source)},trustedPlatform,new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));var result=compilation.Emit(path);Assert.True(result.Success,string.Join("\n",result.Diagnostics));}private static System.Reflection.Assembly CompileInBatch(string modId){// The batch gate is global; this collection runs without parallelism, so no other test compiles inside the window.
using(ModAssemblyLoader.BeginBatch())return ModCompiler.Compile(modId,new[]{("Marker.cs",MarkerSource)});}private static string EmitTestAssembly(string modId,string productionPath){const string source=@"
namespace TestBatchMod.Tests
{
    public static class Probe
    {
        public static System.Type MarkerType() => typeof(TestBatchMod.Marker);
        public static string Call() => new TestBatchMod.Marker().Hello();
    }
}
";var trustedPlatform=((string)AppContext.GetData("TRUSTED_PLATFORM_ASSEMBLIES")).Split(Path.PathSeparator).Select(p=>MetadataReference.CreateFromFile(p));var compilation=CSharpCompilation.Create(modId+"__tests",new[]{CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source)},trustedPlatform.Append(MetadataReference.CreateFromFile(productionPath)),new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));var dir=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"cthangover_test_asm_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);var path=Path.Combine(dir,modId+"__tests_deadbeef.dll");var result=compilation.Emit(path);Assert.True(result.Success,string.Join("\n",result.Diagnostics));return path;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Consumers/ModLoadContextCollection.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Consumers{/// <summary> Non-parallel collection for collectible-ALC tests. The unload-verification phase does forced GC cycles; concurrent test threads (other collections allocating, JIT-compiling, holding stack frames into managed code) make the weak-reference collection check flaky, so these tests run alone. Same scheme as the existing "appservices" and "ConditionStateMonitor" collections. </summary>
[Xunit.CollectionDefinition("mod-load-context",DisableParallelization=true)]public class ModLoadContextCollectionDefinition{}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Consumers/ModLoadContextTests.cs =====
using System;using System.IO;using System.Linq;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Xunit;namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Consumers{/// <summary> Collectible context lifecycle: a compiled mod assembly loads into its own context, its types work, and the context releases once nothing references it. The whole cycle runs in one non-inlined frame because a Debug-JIT local would pin the ALC across the GC otherwise — the classic ALC test trap. The mod id is unique per run so parallel test classes compiling other mods never race on this cache artifact. </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class ModLoadContextTests{[Fact]public void LoadIntoContext_TypeWorks_AndUnloadReleases(){var modId="test_alc_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0,12);var sources=new[]{("Marker.cs",@"
namespace TestAlcMod
{
    public class Marker
    {
        public string Hello() => ""world"";
    }
}
")};var assembly=ModCompiler.Compile(modId,sources);Assert.NotNull(assembly);// Route it into a collectible context. dllPath taken without keeping the default-context assembly reference alive.
var dllPath=assembly.Location;Assert.True(File.Exists(dllPath));var weak=RunLoadAndUnload(modId,dllPath);// Verification runs in a frame with no live ALC-rooted locals: any surviving Assembly object from the context would keep the ALC instance alive and the weak reference would never die.
var diagnostic=ModLoadContext.CollectAndVerify(weak,modId+"_2");Assert.Null(diagnostic);}[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]private static WeakReference RunLoadAndUnload(string modId,string dllPath){// The test drives the collectible path directly (no boot pipeline here): the context is created explicitly, not through the loader batch gate.
var context=new ModLoadContext(modId+"_2",_=>null);context.StageAssembly(dllPath);var weak=new WeakReference(context);// Resolve by the internal assembly name (== modId for compiled mods), not by the cache file name.
var loaded=context.LoadStaged(modId);Assert.NotNull(loaded);var markerType=loaded.GetType("TestAlcMod.Marker");Assert.NotNull(markerType);var call=(string)markerType.GetMethod("Hello").Invoke(Activator.CreateInstance(markerType),null);Assert.Equal("world",call);context.TryUnload();return weak;}[Fact]public void UnloadMod_UnknownId_IsNoOp(){Assert.Null(ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod("no_such_mod_anywhere"));Assert.False(ModAssemblyLoader.IsModLoaded("no_such_mod_anywhere"));}[Fact]public void BeginBatch_EndBatch_IsBalanced(){// The gate is a simple boolean flag: the batch owner opens and closes it around the pipeline phases. After a balanced pair an unknown-id unload stays a no-op.
ModAssemblyLoader.BeginBatch();ModAssemblyLoader.EndBatch();Assert.Null(ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod("no_such_mod_anywhere"));}[Fact]public void TryUnload_MarksTheContextAndItsAssembliesReleased(){var modId=Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload.TestMods.NewId("test_released");var compiled=Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload.TestMods.Compile(modId,"namespace ReleasedMod { public class Marker { } }");var context=(ModLoadContext)System.Runtime.Loader.AssemblyLoadContext.GetLoadContext(compiled);Assert.False(context.IsReleased);Assert.False(ModLoadContext.IsReleasedAssembly(compiled));ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);Assert.True(context.IsReleased);Assert.True(ModLoadContext.IsReleasedAssembly(compiled));Assert.False(ModLoadContext.IsReleasedAssembly(typeof(ModLoadContext).Assembly));Assert.False(ModLoadContext.IsReleasedAssembly(null));}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Consumers/ModTypeConsumerTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Consumers{using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload;/// <summary> The typed consumer base a mod registry derives from: one instance per implementation of an assembly on load, the same instances back on unload. </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class ModTypeConsumerTests{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace ConsumerMod
{
    public class FirstInit : Cthangover.Core.Mods.IModInitializer
    {
        public void OnModLoaded(string modId) { }
        public void OnModResourcesReady() { }
    }

    public class SecondInit : Cthangover.Core.Mods.IModInitializer
    {
        public void OnModLoaded(string modId) { }
        public void OnModResourcesReady() { }
    }

    public abstract class NotCreated : Cthangover.Core.Mods.IModInitializer
    {
        public void OnModLoaded(string modId) { }
        public void OnModResourcesReady() { }
    }
}
";[Fact]public void LoadedThenUnloading_SameInstancesComeBack(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_consumer");var mod=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);var consumer=new RecordingInitializerConsumer();try{consumer.OnAssemblyLoaded(mod,modId);consumer.OnAssemblyLoaded(mod,modId);Assert.Equal(2,consumer.Added.Count);foreach(var(_,addedFor)in consumer.Added)Assert.Equal(modId,addedFor);consumer.OnAssemblyUnloading(mod,modId);Assert.Equal(consumer.Added,consumer.Removed);}finally{ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}}[Fact]public void Unloading_AnAssemblyNeverLoaded_RemovesNothing(){var consumer=new RecordingInitializerConsumer();consumer.OnAssemblyUnloading(typeof(object).Assembly,"core");Assert.Empty(consumer.Removed);}[Fact]public void Assembly_WithoutImplementations_AddsNothing(){var consumer=new RecordingInitializerConsumer();consumer.OnAssemblyLoaded(typeof(object).Assembly,"framework");Assert.Empty(consumer.Added);Assert.Equal(500,consumer.Order);Assert.Equal("tests.recording_initializers",consumer.Id);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/InstallHashCacheTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods{using System;using System.IO;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Providers;/// <summary>The install hash is reused while a mod's files are unchanged and recomputed after any change.</summary>
public class InstallHashCacheTests:IDisposable{private readonly string root=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"cthangover_hash_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));public InstallHashCacheTests(){Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(root,"src"));File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(root,"manifest.json"),"{ \"id\": \"m\" }");File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(root,"src","A.cs"),"class A { }");}public void Dispose()=>Directory.Delete(root,recursive:true);[Fact]public void Get_MatchesTheFullHash_AndFollowsChanges(){var provider=new FolderModFileProvider(root,"m");var first=InstallHashCache.Get(provider,null);Assert.Equal(ModHasher.ComputeInstallSha256(provider,null),first);Assert.Equal(first,InstallHashCache.Get(provider,null));File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(root,"src","A.cs"),"class A { int changed; }");var second=InstallHashCache.Get(provider,null);Assert.NotEqual(first,second);Assert.Equal(ModHasher.ComputeInstallSha256(provider,null),second);}[Fact]public void DeferredInstallHash_RunsOnce_OnFirstRead(){var calls=0;var info=new ModInfo();info.DeferInstallSha256(()=>(++calls).ToString());Assert.Equal(0,calls);Assert.Equal("1",info.InstallSha256);Assert.Equal("1",info.InstallSha256);Assert.Equal(1,calls);}[Fact]public void Stamp_ChangesWithFiles_NullForMissingRoot(){var before=InstallHashCache.Stamp(root);Assert.Equal(before,InstallHashCache.Stamp(root));File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(root,"src","B.cs"),"class B { }");Assert.NotEqual(before,InstallHashCache.Stamp(root));Assert.NotEqual(InstallHashCache.Stamp(root),InstallHashCache.Stamp(root,new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>{".*"}));Assert.Null(InstallHashCache.Stamp(Path.Combine(root,"missing")));}}}

              "id": "mods",
              "scene_path": "scenes/mods.tscn",
              "button": { "icon": "book", "locale": "menu/mods", "index": 30 }
            }
            """;var entry=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<MainMenuEntry>(json);Assert.Equal("mods",entry.Id);Assert.Equal("scenes/mods.tscn",entry.ScenePath);Assert.Equal("book",entry.Button.Icon);Assert.Equal("menu/mods",entry.Button.Locale);Assert.Equal(30,entry.Button.Index);}[Fact]public void ButtonIndex_DefaultsToTen(){Assert.Equal(10,new UiButtonEntry().Index);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Mocks/MockModFileProvider.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Text;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods.Mocks{public class MockModFileProvider:IModFileProvider{private readonly Dictionary<string,string>_textFiles=new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);private readonly Dictionary<string,byte[]>_binaryFiles=new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);private readonly HashSet<string>_directories=new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);private readonly string _modName;public string Mod=>_modName;public string RootPath=>null;public MockModFileProvider(string modName){_modName=modName;}public void AddFile(string path,string content){_textFiles[path.Replace('\\','/')]=content;var dir=Path.GetDirectoryName(path.Replace('\\','/'))?.Replace('\\','/');while(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)){_directories.Add(dir+"/");var parent=Path.GetDirectoryName(dir)?.Replace('\\','/');if(parent==dir)break;dir=parent;}}public void AddBinaryFile(string path,byte[]data){_binaryFiles[path.Replace('\\','/')]=data;}public void AddDirectory(string path){_directories.Add(path.Replace('\\','/').TrimEnd('/')+"/");}public bool FileExists(string path){return _textFiles.ContainsKey(path.Replace('\\','/'))||_binaryFiles.ContainsKey(path.Replace('\\','/'));}public IEnumerable<string>ListFiles(string directory=""){var prefix=string.IsNullOrEmpty(directory)?"":directory.Replace('\\','/').TrimEnd('/')+"/";var result=new List<string>();foreach(var dir in _directories){if(dir.StartsWith(prefix)&&dir!=prefix){var relative=dir.Substring(prefix.Length);var slashIdx=relative.IndexOf('/');if(slashIdx==relative.Length-1)result.Add(prefix+relative);}}foreach(var file in _textFiles.Keys){if(file.StartsWith(prefix)){var relative=file.Substring(prefix.Length);if(!relative.Contains("/"))result.Add(file);}}foreach(var file in _binaryFiles.Keys){if(file.StartsWith(prefix)){var relative=file.Substring(prefix.Length);if(!relative.Contains("/")&& !_textFiles.ContainsKey(file))result.Add(file);}}return result;}public string ReadFileText(string path){_textFiles.TryGetValue(path.Replace('\\','/'),out var content);return content;}public byte[]ReadFileBinary(string path){_binaryFiles.TryGetValue(path.Replace('\\','/'),out var data);return data;}public Stream OpenStream(string path){var content=ReadFileText(path);if(content==null)return null;return new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(content));}public string GetFileSystemPath(string path){return null;}public string ResolveIncludePath(string includePath,string currentPath){return ModPath.ResolveRelative(includePath,currentPath);}public void Dispose(){}}}

using System;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods.Mocks{public class MockModSaveable:IModSaveable{public bool OnSaveCalled{get;private set;}public ModSlot SavedSlot{get;private set;}public bool ShouldThrowOnSave{get;set;}public Action<ModSlot>OnSaveData{get;set;}public bool OnLoadCalled{get;private set;}public ModSlot LoadedSlot{get;private set;}public bool OnLoadResult{get;set;}=true;public string OnLoadError{get;set;}public bool ShouldThrowOnLoad{get;set;}public bool UseOverload{get;set;}public void OnSave(ModSlot slot){if(ShouldThrowOnSave)throw new Exception("Test save error");OnSaveCalled=true;SavedSlot=slot;OnSaveData?.Invoke(slot);}public bool OnLoad(ModSlot slot,out string errorMessage){if(UseOverload){if(ShouldThrowOnLoad)throw new Exception("Test load error");OnLoadCalled=true;LoadedSlot=slot;errorMessage=OnLoadError;return OnLoadResult;}OnLoadVoid(slot);errorMessage=null;return true;}public void OnLoadVoid(ModSlot slot){if(ShouldThrowOnLoad)throw new Exception("Test load error");OnLoadCalled=true;LoadedSlot=slot;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/ModCacheManagerTests.cs =====
using System;using System.IO;using System.Text;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Caches;using Engine.Tests.Mods.Mocks;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> The on-disk shader cache of zip mods (Godot loads shaders only from a file path): an extracted copy belongs to the mod that shipped it and follows its content, so an updated mod is not served its old shader and a same-named shader of another mod never overwrites it. </summary>
public class ModCacheManagerTests:IDisposable{private readonly string modA="__test_shader_a_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");private readonly string modB="__test_shader_b_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");public void Dispose(){foreach(var mod in new[]{modA,modB}){var dir=Path.GetDirectoryName(ModCacheManager.GetShaderCacheDir(mod,"glow"));if(Directory.Exists(dir))Directory.Delete(dir,recursive:true);}}[Fact]public void ExtractShaders_FollowsContent_AndKeepsModsApart(){ModCacheManager.ExtractShaders(modA,Shaders(modA,"shader_type canvas_item; // a1"));ModCacheManager.ExtractShaders(modA,Shaders(modA,"shader_type canvas_item; // a2"));ModCacheManager.ExtractShaders(modB,Shaders(modB,"shader_type canvas_item; // b"));Assert.True(ModCacheManager.IsShaderCached(modA,"glow"));Assert.Equal("shader_type canvas_item; // a2",File.ReadAllText(ModCacheManager.GetShaderCachePath(modA,"glow")));Assert.Equal("shader_type canvas_item; // b",File.ReadAllText(ModCacheManager.GetShaderCachePath(modB,"glow")));}private static MockModFileProvider Shaders(string mod,string glow){var provider=new MockModFileProvider(mod);provider.AddBinaryFile("shaders/glow.gdshader",Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(glow));return provider;}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/ModCollectionLayoutTests.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Round-trip tests for the scheme layout document. Layouts are pure UI state persisted to <c>user://collection_layout.json</c>: group frames and chip positions must survive serialization exactly, because a lost coordinate silently resets the player's arrangement on the next launch. </summary>
public class ModCollectionLayoutTests{[Fact]public void SerializeDeserialize_RoundTripsGroupsAndMods(){var original=new ModCollectionLayout{Groups=new Dictionary<string,ModCollectionLayoutRect>{["i:rpg_novella:rpg_2d"]=new(){X=10f,Y=20f,W=458f,H=390f},},Mods=new Dictionary<string,ModCollectionLayoutPoint>{["i:rpg_novella:rpg_2d|base_2d"]=new(){X=14f,Y=76f},},};var json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(original,JsonOptionsProvider.WriteIndentedOptions);var restored=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModCollectionLayout>(json,JsonOptionsProvider.ReadOptions);var rect=restored.Groups["i:rpg_novella:rpg_2d"];Assert.Equal(10f,rect.X);Assert.Equal(20f,rect.Y);Assert.Equal(458f,rect.W);Assert.Equal(390f,rect.H);var point=restored.Mods["i:rpg_novella:rpg_2d|base_2d"];Assert.Equal(14f,point.X);Assert.Equal(76f,point.Y);}[Fact]public void Deserialize_EmptyObject_ProducesEmptyDictionaries(){const string json="{}";var layout=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModCollectionLayout>(json,JsonOptionsProvider.ReadOptions);Assert.NotNull(layout.Groups);Assert.NotNull(layout.Mods);Assert.Empty(layout.Groups);Assert.Empty(layout.Mods);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/ModCollectionLocalizationTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Tests the '@key' localization convention at the resolver level: '@'-prefixed names stay raw without a translator, resolve through an injected translator, degrade gracefully when the translation is missing, and respect the locale[] / flat-field priority order. The translator is a test double — no Godot is involved. </summary>

using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.IO.Compression;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Providers;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Tests the collection store: collections are packages (a folder or zip with <c>collection.json</c>, cover and <c>locale/</c>); the player's packages persist and survive round-trips, a fork takes its source package's cover and texts along, broken packages never throw. </summary>
public class ModCollectionStoreTests:IDisposable{private readonly string _scratch=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"collection_store_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));public ModCollectionStoreTests(){ClearUser();Directory.CreateDirectory(_scratch);}public void Dispose(){ClearUser();try{Directory.Delete(_scratch,true);}catch{}}private static void ClearUser(){if(Directory.Exists(ModCollectionStore.UserPath))Directory.Delete(ModCollectionStore.UserPath,true);}private string Package(string name,string json){var dir=Path.Combine(_scratch,name);Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);if(json!=null)File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(dir,ModCollectionStore.DocumentName),json);return dir;}[Fact]public void List<string>{"card_battle"},Optional=true},},},new(){Id="tools",Name="Tools"},};ModCollectionStore.SaveUser(original);var loaded=ModCollectionStore.LoadUser();Assert.Equal(2,loaded.Count);var Novel",novel.Name);Assert.Equal("1.0.0",novel.Version);Assert.Equal(2,novel.Mechanics.Count);Assert.Equal("core",novel.Mechanics[0].Id);Assert.Equal("cthangover_game>=1",novel.Mechanics[0].Mods[0]);Assert.True(novel.Mechanics[1].Optional);Assert.All(loaded,c=>Assert.Equal(ModCollectionOrigin.User,c.Origin));Assert.All(loaded,c=>Assert.NotNull(c.Package));Assert.True(File.Exists(Path.Combine(ModCollectionStore.UserPath,"my_novel",ModCollectionStore.DocumentName)));}[Fact]public using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Round-trip tests for the collection JSON DTO. Collections are authored by hand, shipped inside mods and cached from catalogs, so every field must survive serialization exactly; <see cref="ModCollection.Origin"/> is runtime-only and must stay out of the file. </summary>
public class ModCollectionTests{[Fact]public void SerializeDeserialize_FullCollection_RoundTripsAllFields(){var original=new ModCollection{Id="card_novel",Name="Card Novel",Author="ct",Description="A visual novel with card battles.",Version="1.2.0",SourceUrls=new List<string>{"https://catalog.test/catalog.json"},Mechanics=new List<ModCollectionMechanic>{new(){Id="vn_core",Name="Novel Core",Description="Story engine.",Mods=new List<string>{"cthangover_game>=1"},},new(){Id="cards",Name="Card Game",Mods=new List<string>{"card_battle>=1.1","turn_battle_base>=1"},Optional=true,},},};var json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(original,JsonOptionsProvider.WriteIndentedOptions);var restored=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModCollection>(json,JsonOptionsProvider.ReadOptions);Assert.Equal(original.Id,restored.Id);Assert.Equal(original.Name,restored.Name);Assert.Equal(original.Author,restored.Author);Assert.Equal(original.Description,restored.Description);Assert.Equal(original.Version,restored.Version);Assert.Equal(original.SourceUrls,restored.SourceUrls);Assert.Equal(2,restored.Mechanics.Count);Assert.Equal("vn_core",restored.Mechanics[0].Id);Assert.Equal("Novel Core",restored.Mechanics[0].Name);Assert.Equal("Story engine.",restored.Mechanics[0].Description);Assert.Single(restored.Mechanics[0].Mods);Assert.Equal("cthangover_game>=1",restored.Mechanics[0].Mods[0]);Assert.False(restored.Mechanics[0].Optional);Assert.Equal("cards",restored.Mechanics[1].Id);Assert.Equal(2,restored.Mechanics[1].Mods.Count);Assert.True(restored.Mechanics[1].Optional);}[Fact]public void Serialize_Origin_IsNotWrittenToJson(){var collection=new ModCollection{Id="local",Origin=ModCollectionOrigin.User};var json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(collection,JsonOptionsProvider.WriteIndentedOptions);Assert.DoesNotContain("Origin",json);}[Fact]public void Deserialize_UnknownFields_AreIgnored(){const string json="""

System.Predicate<string>NamesSkippedDependent(string lib)=>new SkippedDependentMatcher(lib).Matches;private static ModInfo SourceMod(string root,string id,string code,params string[]depends){var dir=Path.Combine(root,id);Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(dir,"src"));File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(dir,"src","Code.cs"),code);var manifest=new ModManifest{Id=id,Name=id,Version="1.0.0",Sources=new List<string>{"src/*.cs"},Depends=new List<ModDependency>()};foreach(var dependency in depends)manifest.Depends.Add(ModDependency.Parse(dependency+">=1"));return new ModInfo{Id=id,Name=id,Manifest=manifest,FileProvider=new Cthangover.Core.Mods.Providers.FolderModFileProvider(dir,id)};}private static void Cleanup(string root,params string[]modIds){foreach(var id in modIds){Cthangover.Core.Mods.Compilation.ModAssemblyCache.CurrentArtifacts.TryRemove(id,out _);ModCompiler.InvalidateCache(id);}if(Directory.Exists(root))Directory.Delete(root,recursive:true);}}}

using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Discovery;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Resets the process-wide discovery registry through reflection, so it must not overlap with test classes that boot the mod registry in parallel (they would see emptied scanner/loader lists). </summary>
[CollectionDefinition("mod-discovery-registry",DisableParallelization=true)]public class ModDiscoveryRegistryCollectionDefinition{}[Collection("mod-discovery-registry")]public class scanners=(List<IModScanner>)scannersField.GetValue(null);scanners?.Clear();}var namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Pipeline;/// <summary>Disabling and dependency validation of the discovered mod set before compilation.</summary>

using System;using System.IO;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{public class ModSettingsRegistryTests:IDisposable{private const string ModSettingsDir="mod_settings";private static readonly System.Type RegisteredType=typeof(ModSettingsRegistry).GetNestedType("RegisteredModSettings",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static);private static readonly FieldInfo EntriesField=typeof(ModSettingsRegistry).GetField("_entries",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static);private static readonly FieldInfo RegistryInitializedField=typeof(ModRegistry).GetField("initialized",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Instance);public void Dispose(){try{if(Directory.Exists(ModSettingsDir))Directory.Delete(ModSettingsDir,true);}catch{}}private static void InitRegistry(){var registry=ModRegistry.Instance;if(!(bool)RegistryInitializedField.GetValue(registry))RegistryInitializedField.SetValue(registry,true);}private static void SetEntries(params(IModSettings instance,string modId)[]entries){var list=(System.Collections.IList)EntriesField.GetValue(null);list.Clear();foreach(var(instance,modId)in entries){var reg=System.Activator.CreateInstance(RegisteredType);RegisteredType.GetField("Instance").SetValue(reg,instance);RegisteredType.GetField("ModId").SetValue(reg,modId);list.Add(reg);}}// ─── Definitions ──────────────────────────────────────────
[Fact]public void GetDefinitions_returns_definitions_from_registered_mod(){var def=new ModSettingDefinition{Key="volume",Name="Volume",Type=SettingType.Integer,DefaultValue="50"};var settings=new Mocks.MockModSettings(new System.Collections.Generic.List<ModSettingDefinition>{def});SetEntries((settings,"audio_mod"));var result=ModSettingsRegistry.GetDefinitions("audio_mod");Assert.Single(result);Assert.Equal("volume",result[0].Key);Assert.Equal("Volume",result[0].Name);Assert.Equal(SettingType.Integer,result[0].Type);Assert.Equal("50",result[0].DefaultValue);}[Fact]public void GetDefinitions_unknown_mod_returns_empty(){SetEntries();Assert.Empty(ModSettingsRegistry.GetDefinitions("unknown"));}// ─── Write/Apply roundtrip in-memory ───────────────────────
[Fact]public void WriteCurrentValues_then_ApplyValues_roundtrip_preserves_data(){SetEntries((new Mocks.MockModSettings(),"test_mod"));var saved=ModSettingsRegistry.WriteCurrentValues("test_mod");Assert.Equal(42,saved.GetInt("test_int"));Assert.Equal("hello",saved.GetString("test_str"));saved.SetInt("test_int",99);saved.SetString("test_str","updated");var reader=new Mocks.MockModSettings();SetEntries((reader,"test_mod"));ModSettingsRegistry.ApplyValues("test_mod",saved);Assert.True(reader.ReadValuesCalled);Assert.Equal(99,reader.LastReadBlob.GetInt("test_int"));Assert.Equal("updated",reader.LastReadBlob.GetString("test_str"));}[Fact]public void WriteCurrentValues_unknown_mod_returns_empty_blob(){SetEntries();var blob=ModSettingsRegistry.WriteCurrentValues("unknown");Assert.NotNull(blob);Assert.Equal(1,blob.Version);}[Fact]public void ApplyValues_null_blob_skips_read(){var settings=new Mocks.MockModSettings();SetEntries((settings,"test_mod"));ModSettingsRegistry.ApplyValues("test_mod",null);Assert.False(settings.ReadValuesCalled);}[Fact]public void ApplyValues_unknown_mod_does_not_crash(){SetEntries();ModSettingsRegistry.ApplyValues("unknown",new DataBlob());}// ─── File I/O: Save / Load / Delete ────────────────────────

using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Testing;using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;using Xunit;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Pure parts of the mod test engine: how a test failure surfaces and how a run is aggregated into the CI contract and the exit code. </summary>
public class ModTestEngineTests{private static void FailingAssert()=>ModAssert.Equal(1,2,"one equals two");private static void ThrowsPlain(ModTestContext ctx)=>throw new InvalidOperationException("boom from the test");[Fact]public void Invoke_SyncAssertFailure_SurfacesAssertException(){// Reflection wraps a synchronous throw in TargetInvocationException; the report must show the assertion, not "Exception has been thrown by the target of an invocation".
var method=typeof(ModTestEngineTests).GetMethod(nameof(FailingAssert),BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static);var ex=Assert.Throws<ModAssertException>(()=>ModTestRunner.Invoke(method,null));Assert.Contains("one equals two",ex.Message);Assert.Contains(nameof(FailingAssert),ex.StackTrace);}[Fact]public void Invoke_SyncException_WithContextParameter_KeepsOriginalType(){var method=typeof(ModTestEngineTests).GetMethod(nameof(ThrowsPlain),BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static);var ex=Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(()=>ModTestRunner.Invoke(method,null));Assert.Equal("boom from the test",ex.Message);}[Fact]public void FormatDiagnostic_UsesPathLineColumn(){// Compile errors must be clickable "file(line,col)" locations, not Roslyn's default character span "SourceFile(path[3904..3923))".
var tree=CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText("class A\n{\n    void M() { Missing(); }\n}\n",path:"/mods/demo/tests/A.cs");var compilation=CSharpCompilation.Create("demo",new[]{tree},new[]{MetadataReference.CreateFromFile(typeof(object).Assembly.Location)},new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));var error=compilation.GetDiagnostics().First(d=>d.Severity==DiagnosticSeverity.Error);Assert.Equal("/mods/demo/tests/A.cs(3,16): error CS0103: "+error.GetMessage(),ModCompiler.FormatDiagnostic(error));}[Fact]public void ComputeTotals_SkipsDoNotFailTheRun(){var totals=ModTestReportWriter.ComputeTotals(new List<ModTestSuiteSummary>{new(){ModId="a",Passed=1,Skipped=3},});Assert.Equal(1,totals.Passed);Assert.Equal(0,totals.Failed);Assert.Equal(3,totals.Skipped);Assert.True(totals.Success);}[Fact]public void ComputeTotals_FailuresAndErrorsFailTheRun(){var totals=ModTestReportWriter.ComputeTotals(new List<ModTestSuiteSummary>{new(){ModId="a",Passed=2,Failed=1,Skipped=1},new(){ModId="b",Passed=0,Errors=1},});Assert.Equal(2,totals.Passed);Assert.Equal(2,totals.Failed);Assert.Equal(1,totals.Skipped);Assert.False(totals.Success);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/ModUninstallerTests.cs =====
using System.IO;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Providers;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> Tests the filesystem half of <see cref="ModUninstaller"/>. The registry lookup path needs a live mod set, so it is covered by integration; here the guarantees are: a mod directory is removed recursively, a zip mod is removed as a file, and a missing target reports failure instead of throwing. <see cref="ModRegistry.AllDiscoveredMods"/> is never seeded, so unknown ids must not be deletable. </summary>
public class ModUninstallerTests:IDisposable{private readonly string _testDir;public ModUninstallerTests(){_testDir=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),$"mod_uninstaller_test_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}");Directory.CreateDirectory(_testDir);}public void Dispose(){if(Directory.Exists(_testDir))Directory.Delete(_testDir,recursive:true);}[Fact]public void DeleteModFiles_Folder_RemovesRecursively(){var modDir=Path.Combine(_testDir,"some_mod");Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(modDir,"scenes"));File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(modDir,"manifest.json"),"{}");File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(modDir,"scenes","a.tscn"),"");var result=ModUninstaller.DeleteModFiles(modDir);Assert.True(result);Assert.False(Directory.Exists(modDir));}[Fact]public void DeleteModFiles_ZipFile_RemovesArchive(){var zipPath=Path.Combine(_testDir,"some_mod.zip");File.WriteAllBytes(zipPath,new byte[]{0x50,0x4B,0x03,0x04});var result=ModUninstaller.DeleteModFiles(zipPath);Assert.True(result);Assert.False(File.Exists(zipPath));}[Fact]public void DeleteModFiles_MissingPath_ReturnsFalse(){var result=ModUninstaller.DeleteModFiles(Path.Combine(_testDir,"nonexistent"));Assert.False(result);}[Fact]public void DeleteModFiles_EmptyPath_ReturnsFalse(){Assert.False(ModUninstaller.DeleteModFiles(""));Assert.False(ModUninstaller.DeleteModFiles(null));}[Fact]public void CanUninstall_NullModInfo_ReturnsFalse(){Assert.False(ModUninstaller.CanUninstall((IModInfo)null));}[Fact]public void CanUninstall_ModOutsideKnownRoots_ReturnsFalse(){var modDir=Path.Combine(_testDir,"external_mod");Directory.CreateDirectory(modDir);var info=new ModInfo{Id="external_mod",FileProvider=new FolderModFileProvider(modDir,"external_mod"),};Assert.False(ModUninstaller.CanUninstall(info));}}}

namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Pipeline{using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Pipeline;/// <summary> What a mod-set reload does with each mod that has code: release it, keep its context, or load a new one — from the files the contexts run now and the files the new set compiled to. </summary>

using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Resolvers;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{/// <summary> The scenario replacement rule: definitions are collected flat in load order and a later definition of the same name replaces the earlier one — wherever either of them lives — while different names live side by side. </summary>

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/ToolsModCompileTests.cs =====
namespace Engine.Tests.Mods{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.IO;using System.Linq;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;/// <summary> Repository-level compile guard for every mod that ships C# sources. Mod code is excluded from the main csproj and only compiled by Roslyn at runtime, so a syntax or reference error in <c>mods/*/src</c> would otherwise surface only when the game boots. The test mirrors production order (ModCompiler.CompileAll): manifest dependencies first, so mod-to-mod references resolve through the compilation cache the same way, and the real mod ids are passed to the compiler so the reference scan sees exactly the set the game would see. Mods without sources are skipped, and the whole check is skipped when the checkout has no mods. </summary>
public class ToolsModCompileTests{[Fact]public void Compile_RealMods_InDependencyOrder_Succeeds(){var mods=DiscoverMods();if(mods.Count==0)return;var knownIds=mods.Keys.ToList();foreach(var modId in TopologicalOrder(mods)){var sourceRoot=mods[modId].SourceRoot;if(sourceRoot==null)continue;var files=Directory.GetFiles(sourceRoot,"*.cs",SearchOption.AllDirectories);if(files.Length==0)continue;var sources=files.Select(file=>(file,File.ReadAllText(file))).ToList();// Compile under the REAL mod id: the cache artifact name <modId>_<hash>.dll is how dependent mods resolve mod-to-mod references (TryParseCacheDllName), so a "<id>_compile_test" prefix would make every dependency invisible to its dependents and break exactly the mods that have depends.
var assembly=ModCompiler.Compile(modId,sources,null,default,knownIds);if(assembly==null)Assert.Fail($"Mod '{modId}' failed to compile:\n"+string.Join("\n",GameLogger.CompilationErrors));}}private sealed record ModGuardInfo(string Id,string SourceRoot,List<string>Depends);/// <summary> Finds the repository root (walks up from the test binaries until Cthangover.csproj shows up) and reads every <c>mods/*/manifest.json</c>. </summary>
private static Dictionary<string,ModGuardInfo>DiscoverMods(){var repoRoot=FindRepoRoot();if(repoRoot==null)return new Dictionary<string,ModGuardInfo>();var result=new Dictionary<string,ModGuardInfo>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);var modsRoot=Path.Combine(repoRoot,"mods");if(!Directory.Exists(modsRoot))return result;foreach(var dir in Directory.GetDirectories(modsRoot).OrderBy(path=>path,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)){var manifestPath=Path.Combine(dir,"manifest.json");if(!File.Exists(manifestPath))continue;string id;var depends=new List<string>();try{using var document=JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(manifestPath));id=document.RootElement.GetProperty("id").GetString();if(document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("depends",out var dependsElement)&&dependsElement.ValueKind==JsonValueKind.Array){foreach(var dep in dependsElement.EnumerateArray()){var raw=dep.GetString();if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(raw))continue;var separator=raw.IndexOfAny(new[]{'>','=','<'});depends.Add(separator>0?raw.Substring(0,separator):raw);}}}catch(Exception ex){Assert.Fail($"mods/{Path.GetFileName(dir)}/manifest.json is not readable: {ex.Message}");return result;}var sourceRoot=Path.Combine(dir,"src");result[id]=new ModGuardInfo(id,Directory.Exists(sourceRoot)?sourceRoot:null,depends);}return result;}/// <summary> Kahn's algorithm over manifest depends edges, mirroring ModCompiler.CompileAll: a dependency always precedes its dependents. </summary>
private static List<string>TopologicalOrder(Dictionary<string,ModGuardInfo>mods){var pending=mods.Keys.ToDictionary(id=>id,id=>mods[id].Depends.Where(dep=>mods.ContainsKey(dep)).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList(),StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);var order=new List<string>();while(pending.Count>0){var ready=pending.Where(kvp=>kvp.Value.Count==0).Select(kvp=>kvp.Key).ToList();if(ready.Count==0)Assert.Fail("Mod dependency cycle: "+string.Join(", ",pending.Keys.OrderBy(id=>id,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));foreach(var id in ready){order.Add(id);pending.Remove(id);}foreach(var id in ready)foreach(var remaining in pending)remaining.Value.Remove(id);}return order;}private static string FindRepoRoot(){var directory=new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);while(directory!=null){if(File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName,"Cthangover.csproj")))return directory.FullName;directory=directory.Parent;}return null;}}}

namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Unload;/// <summary> System.Text.Json caches type metadata and emitted accessors of every serialized type; for a mod type they pin the mod's context. After the reset the context of a serialized mod type can be collected. </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class JsonCacheResetTests{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace JsonMod
{
    public class Payload
    {
        public int Value { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }
}
";private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions SharedOptions=new();[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]private static WeakReference SerializeInCollectibleContext(string modId){var compiled=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);var dllPath=compiled.Location;ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);var context=new ModLoadContext(modId+"_json",null);context.StageAssembly(dllPath);var type=context.LoadStaged(modId).GetType("JsonMod.Payload");var payload=Activator.CreateInstance(type);type.GetProperty("Value").SetValue(payload,42);var json=JsonSerializer.Serialize(payload,type,SharedOptions);Assert.Contains("42",json);Assert.Equal(42,type.GetProperty("Value").GetValue(JsonSerializer.Deserialize(json,type,SharedOptions)));var weak=new WeakReference(context);context.TryUnload();return weak;}[Fact]public void IsAvailable_OnThisRuntime(){Assert.True(JsonCacheReset.IsAvailable);}[Fact]public void Clear_ReleasesSerializedModTypes(){var weak=SerializeInCollectibleContext(TestMods.NewId("test_json"));Assert.True(JsonCacheReset.Clear());Assert.Null(ModLoadContext.CollectAndVerify(weak,"test_json"));}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/ModUnloadTicketTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Unload;/// <summary>Verification of released contexts through weak references only.</summary>
public class ModUnloadTicketTests{private readonly object kept=new();[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]private static WeakReference Garbage()=>new(new object());[Fact]public void Collect_ReturnsOnlyStillReferencedEntries_InOrder(){var ticket=new ModUnloadTicket(new[]{("gone",Garbage()),("kept",new WeakReference(kept))});Assert.Equal(new[]{"gone","kept"},ticket.ModIds);Assert.Equal(new[]{"kept"},ticket.Collect());Assert.Equal(new[]{"kept"},ticket.Alive);GC.KeepAlive(kept);}[Fact]public void NullContexts_IsAnEmptyTicket(){var ticket=new ModUnloadTicket(null);Assert.Empty(ticket.ModIds);Assert.Empty(ticket.Alive);Assert.Empty(ticket.Collect());}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/ModUnloaderTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Reflection;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Unload;/// <summary> The unload protocol end to end on a compiled mod: the context is released and collected, references from surviving statics are swept first. Everything touching the mod assembly runs in non-inlined helpers so no test-method local keeps the context alive. </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class ModUnloaderTests:IDisposable{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace UnloadMod
{
    public static class Api
    {
        public static void Ping() { }
    }
}
";public ModUnloaderTests()=>SweepHostSlots.Reset();public void Dispose()=>SweepHostSlots.Reset();[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]private static void LoadMod(string modId,bool parkDelegateInHost){var assembly=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);if(parkDelegateInHost)SweepHostSlots.Callback=(Action)Delegate.CreateDelegate(typeof(Action),assembly.GetType("UnloadMod.Api").GetMethod("Ping"));}[Fact]public void Unload_ReleasesAndCollectsTheContext(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_unload");LoadMod(modId,parkDelegateInHost:false);Assert.True(ModAssemblyLoader.IsModLoaded(modId));var ticket=ModUnloader.Unload(new[]{modId},null,Array.Empty<Assembly>());Assert.Equal(new[]{modId},ticket.ModIds);Assert.False(ModAssemblyLoader.IsModLoaded(modId));Assert.Empty(ticket.Collect(30));}[Fact]public void Unload_SweepsHostStatics_ThenTheContextIsCollected(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_unload_host");LoadMod(modId,parkDelegateInHost:true);Assert.NotNull(SweepHostSlots.Callback);var ticket=ModUnloader.Unload(new[]{modId},null,new[]{typeof(SweepHostSlots).Assembly});Assert.Null(SweepHostSlots.Callback);Assert.Empty(ticket.Collect(30));}[Fact]public void Unload_UnknownMod_ReturnsAnEmptyTicket(){var ticket=ModUnloader.Unload(new[]{"no_such_mod_anywhere"},null,Array.Empty<Assembly>());Assert.Empty(ticket.ModIds);}[Fact]public void UnregisterKept_LeavesTheModLoaded(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_unload_kept");LoadMod(modId,parkDelegateInHost:false);var artifacts=ModAssemblyLoader.ArtifactsOf(modId);ModUnloader.UnregisterKept(new[]{modId});Assert.True(ModAssemblyLoader.IsModLoaded(modId));Assert.Equal(artifacts,ModAssemblyLoader.ArtifactsOf(modId));ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/StaticReferenceSweeperTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Reflection;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Unload;/// <summary> The static reference sweep against a real compiled mod: references to mod code leave the surviving statics (and the objects behind them), everything else stays. </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class StaticReferenceSweeperTests:IDisposable{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace SweepMod
{
    public class Marker { }

    public static class Api
    {
        public static void Ping() { }
    }
}
";private readonly string modId=TestMods.NewId("test_sweep");private readonly Assembly mod;public StaticReferenceSweeperTests(){SweepHostSlots.Reset();mod=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);}public void Dispose(){SweepHostSlots.Reset();ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);}private object Marker()=>Activator.CreateInstance(mod.GetType("SweepMod.Marker"));private Action Ping()=>(Action)Delegate.CreateDelegate(typeof(Action),mod.GetType("SweepMod.Api").GetMethod("Ping"));[Fact]public void Sweep_ClearsModReferences_KeepsForeignOnes(){Action foreign=GC.Collect;SweepHostSlots.Callback=(Action)Delegate.Combine(Ping(),foreign);SweepHostSlots.Slot=Marker();SweepHostSlots.Items.Add(Marker());SweepHostSlots.Items.Add("keep");SweepHostSlots.ByName["mod"]=Marker();SweepHostSlots.ByName["type"]=mod.GetType("SweepMod.Marker");SweepHostSlots.ByName["keep"]=1;SweepHostSlots.Set.Add(Marker());SweepHostSlots.Set.Add("keep");SweepHostSlots.Registry.Add("x",Marker());SweepHostSlots.Registry.Add("x","keep");var report=StaticReferenceSweeper.Sweep(new AssemblyOwnership(new[]{mod}),new[]{typeof(SweepHostSlots).Assembly});Assert.Equal(new Delegate[]{foreign},SweepHostSlots.Callback.GetInvocationList());Assert.Null(SweepHostSlots.Slot);Assert.Equal(new object[]{"keep"},SweepHostSlots.Items);Assert.Equal(new[]{"keep"},SweepHostSlots.ByName.Keys.ToArray());Assert.Equal(new object[]{"keep"},SweepHostSlots.Set.ToArray());Assert.Equal(1,SweepHostSlots.Registry.Count("x"));Assert.Contains(report,NamesSlotField);}private static bool NamesSlotField(string line)=>line.Contains(nameof(SweepHostSlots)+"."+nameof(SweepHostSlots.Slot));[Fact]public void Sweep_EmptyOwnership_ChangesNothing(){SweepHostSlots.Slot=Marker();SweepHostSlots.Items.Add(Marker());var report=StaticReferenceSweeper.Sweep(new AssemblyOwnership(null),new[]{typeof(SweepHostSlots).Assembly});Assert.Empty(report);Assert.NotNull(SweepHostSlots.Slot);Assert.Single(SweepHostSlots.Items);}[Fact]public void Sweep_ExplicitInstances_AreWalkedWithoutHostAssemblies(){var registry=new SweepHostRegistry();registry.Add("x",Marker());registry.Add("x","keep");StaticReferenceSweeper.Sweep(new AssemblyOwnership(new[]{mod}),Array.Empty<Assembly>(),new object[]{registry});Assert.Equal(1,registry.Count("x"));}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/SweepHostRegistry.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System.Collections.Generic;/// <summary>A registry object behind a static readonly field: the sweep must walk into it.</summary>
public sealed class SweepHostRegistry{private readonly Dictionary<string,List<object>>entries=new();public void Add(string key,object value){if(!entries.TryGetValue(key,out var list))entries[key]=list=new List<object>();list.Add(value);}public int Count(string key)=>entries.TryGetValue(key,out var list)?list.Count:0;public void Clear()=>entries.Clear();}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/SweepHostSlots.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Collections.Generic;/// <summary> Long-lived static slots of a "surviving" assembly, the kind of kernel state a mod fills from its initializer (bridges, lists, maps, a registry singleton) — the targets of the static reference sweep. </summary>
public static class SweepHostSlots{public static Action Callback;public static object Slot;public static List<object>Items=new();public static Dictionary<string,object>ByName=new();public static HashSet<object>Set=new();public static readonly SweepHostRegistry Registry=new();public static void Reset(){Callback=null;Slot=null;Items.Clear();ByName.Clear();Set.Clear();Registry.Clear();}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Mods/Unload/TestMods.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload{using System;using System.Reflection;using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;/// <summary> Compiles a throw-away mod from source into its own collectible <see cref="ModLoadContext"/>, exactly as the boot pipeline does (cache artifact + file-backed load inside a loader batch). The batch gate is process-wide, so every test using this belongs to the "mod-load-context" collection (no parallel compile inside the window). Callers must keep the returned assembly out of locals of the method that later verifies collection — a Debug-JIT local pins the context. </summary>
public static class TestMods{/// <summary>A unique mod id, so parallel test classes never share cache artifacts.</summary>
public static string NewId(string prefix)=>prefix+"_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0,12);/// <summary>Compiles <paramref name="source"/> as mod <paramref name="modId"/>; fails the test on a compile error.</summary>
[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]public static Assembly Compile(string modId,string source){using(ModAssemblyLoader.BeginBatch()){var assembly=ModCompiler.Compile(modId,new[]{("Probe.cs",source)});Assert.NotNull(assembly);return assembly;}}}}

using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Nodes.Impls;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenarios{/// <summary> Unit coverage for the core condition grammar: flag comparisons, boolean literals, and/or/not structure, precedence, and error cases. Quest grammar (Quest.hasTag / Quest.Status / Quest.IsActive) is provided by the quests_base mod's QuestParser leaf parser and covered by the integration coverage_quests test - the core kernel must not auto-register it (see Parse_QuestGrammar_NotRegisteredInCore_Throws at the bottom). </summary>
public class ConditionParserTests{[Fact]public void Parse_Null_ReturnsNull(){Assert.Null(ConditionParser.Parse(null));}[Fact]public void Parse_EmptyString_ReturnsNull(){Assert.Null(ConditionParser.Parse(""));}[Fact]public void Parse_Whitespace_ReturnsNull(){Assert.Null(ConditionParser.Parse("   \t  "));}[Fact]public void Parse_FlagEqualString_ReturnsFlagCondition(){var node=ConditionParser.Parse("myFlag == \"done\"");var fc=Assert.IsType<FlagCondition>(node);Assert.Equal("myFlag",fc.Key);Assert.Equal("==",fc.Op);Assert.Equal("done",fc.Value);}[Fact]public void Parse_FlagNotEqualNumber_ReturnsFlagCondition(){var node=ConditionParser.Parse("counter != 42");var fc=Assert.IsType<FlagCondition>(node);Assert.Equal("counter",fc.Key);Assert.Equal("!=",fc.Op);Assert.Equal("42",fc.Value);}[Fact]public void Parse_FlagGreater_ReturnsFlagCondition(){var node=ConditionParser.Parse("x > 3");var namespace Engine.Tests.Scenarios{/// <summary> Marks the ConditionStateMonitor tests as non-parallel. The monitor's <c>Version</c> is a global monotonic counter shared across the whole process, so its delta-based assertions must not run concurrently with other test classes that also publish events (e.g. ScenarioConditionTests). </summary>
[CollectionDefinition("ConditionStateMonitor",DisableParallelization=true)]public class ConditionStateMonitorCollection{}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Scenarios/ConditionStateMonitorTests.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Events;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenarios{/// <summary> Cache-invalidation monitor of <see cref="ScenarioCondition"/>: any quest/flag/map-zone change must bump the version so cached condition ASTs re-evaluate. Quest state mutation (tag/status/data changes) is owned by the quests_base engine mod; the core keeps the event contracts and the subscription — those are covered here via direct event publishes. End-to-end quest mutations are covered by the integration coverage test (coverage_quests) with mods loaded. </summary>
[Collection("ConditionStateMonitor")]public class ConditionStateMonitorTests{[Fact]public void SetFlag_NewValue_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ScenarioCondition.SetFlag("monFlag1","a");Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void SetFlag_SameValue_DoesNotBump(){ScenarioCondition.SetFlag("monFlag2","a");var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ScenarioCondition.SetFlag("monFlag2","a");Assert.Equal(before,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void Notify_AnyEvent_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ConditionStateMonitor.Notify(new QuestTagChangedEvent{QuestId="x",Tag="t",Added=true});Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void QuestStatusChangedEvent_Published_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ModEventBus.Publish(new QuestStatusChangedEvent{QuestId="x",OldStatus="NotStarted",NewStatus="Progress"});Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void QuestDataStatusChangedEvent_Published_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ModEventBus.Publish(new QuestDataStatusChangedEvent{Old=1,New=2});Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void QuestStateRestoredEvent_Published_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ModEventBus.Publish(new QuestStateRestoredEvent());Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}[Fact]public void MarkStateChanged_Bumps(){var before=ConditionStateMonitor.Version;ConditionStateMonitor.MarkStateChanged();Assert.Equal(before+1,ConditionStateMonitor.Version);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Scenarios/ScenarioConditionTests.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Scenes;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenarios{/// <summary> Flag/boolean grammar of <see cref="ScenarioCondition"/>. The quest condition grammar (<c>QuestId.method(...)</c>, property comparisons) is owned by the quests_base engine mod — its QuestParser registers at mod load and is covered by the integration coverage test (coverage_quests), which runs with mods loaded. </summary>
public class ScenarioConditionTests{[Fact]public void Evaluate_Null_ReturnsTrue(){Assert.True(ScenarioCondition.Evaluate(null));}[Fact]public void using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Patching;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.TscnParsing;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class ConnectionSelectorTests{private static ParsedConnection Connection(string from,string to,string signal,string method,int flags=0)=>new(){From=from,To=to,Signal=signal,Method=method,Flags=flags};[Fact]public void TryParse_FullSelector_ExtractsEveryField(){var ok=ConnectionSelector.TryParse("from=MenuContainer/LoadBtn;to=.;signal=pressed;method=_on_pressed;flags=1",out var selector,out var error);Assert.True(ok,error);Assert.Equal("MenuContainer/LoadBtn",selector.From);Assert.Equal(".",selector.To);Assert.Equal("pressed",selector.Signal);Assert.Equal("_on_pressed",selector.Method);Assert.Equal(1,selector.Flags);}[Fact]public _));Assert.True(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("to=.;signal=pressed",out _,out _));Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("signal=pressed",out _,out _));Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("from=Panel/Btn",out _,out _));}[Fact]public void TryParse_InvalidInput_ReportsError(){Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("",out _,out _));Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("pressed",out _,out _));Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("bogus=x;from=a;signal=s",out _,out _));Assert.False(ConnectionSelector.TryParse("from=a;signal=s;flags=abc",out _,out _));}[Fact]public void Matches_ByFromAndSignal(){var selector=Parse("from=Panel/Btn;signal=pressed");Assert.True(selector.Matches(Connection("Panel/Btn",".","pressed","_on_pressed")));Assert.False(selector.Matches(Connection("Panel/Other",".","pressed","_on_pressed")));Assert.False(selector.Matches(Connection("Panel/Btn",".","toggled","_on_pressed")));}[Fact]public void Matches_ByToAndFlags(){var void Matches_ByMethodWildcard(){var selector=Parse("from=Panel/Btn;signal=pressed");Assert.True(selector.Matches(Connection("Panel/Btn",".","pressed","whatever")));}private static ConnectionSelector using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Patching;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class MaskMatcherTests{[Fact]public void ExactPattern_MatchesOnlySameText(){Assert.True(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("MenuContainer/LoadBtn","MenuContainer/LoadBtn"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("MenuContainer/LoadBtn","MenuContainer/ExitBtn"));}[Fact]public void Star_MatchesAcrossHierarchySeparators(){Assert.True(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("MenuContainer/*","MenuContainer/Sub/Deep"));Assert.True(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("*Btn","MenuContainer/LoadBtn"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("*Btn","MenuContainer/LoadButton"));}[Fact]public void QuestionMark_MatchesExactlyOneCharacter(){Assert.True(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("Btn?","Btn1"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("Btn?","Btn12"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("Btn?","Btn"));}[Fact]public void PatternWithoutMatch_ReturnsFalse(){Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("theme_override_colors/*","text"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("Panel","Panel/Child"));}[Fact]public void Matching_IsCaseSensitive(){Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("panel","Panel"));Assert.True(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("*","Panel"));}[Fact]public void EmptyOrNull_ReturnsFalse(){Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch(null,"Panel"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("","Panel"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.IsMatch("*",null));}[Fact]public void HasMask_DetectsWildcards(){Assert.True(MaskMatcher.HasMask("a*b"));Assert.True(MaskMatcher.HasMask("a?b"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.HasMask("plain"));Assert.False(MaskMatcher.HasMask(null));}}}

id=\"1_target\"]\n\n"+string.Join("\n",bodyLines);Assert.True(ScenePatchParser.TryParse(text,"test_mod","patches/scenes/target.tscn",out var patch,out var error),error);return patch;}private static ParsedNode Find(ParsedScene scene,string path)=>scene.Nodes.FirstOrDefault(n=>PathOf(n)==path);private static string PathOf(ParsedNode node){if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(node.Parent)||node.Parent=="."||node.Parent=="..")return node.Name;return node.Parent.TrimEnd('/')+"/"+node.Name;}}}

using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Patching;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class ScenePatchParserTests{private const string ValidPatch=@"
[gd_scene format=3]

[ext_resource type=""PackedScene"" path=""res://mods/my_mod/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"" id=""1_target""]

[node name=""MainMenuScene"" instance=ExtResource(""1_target"")]
";[Fact]public void TryParse_ValidInheritedScene_ResolvesTarget(){var ok=ScenePatchParser.TryParse(ValidPatch,"my_mod","patches/scenes/main_menu.tscn",out var patch,out var error);Assert.True(ok,error);Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",patch.TargetPath);Assert.Equal("my_mod",patch.ModId);Assert.Equal("patches/scenes/main_menu.tscn",patch.SourcePath);Assert.Single(patch.Scene.Nodes);Assert.Equal("MainMenuScene",patch.Scene.Nodes[0].Name);}[Fact]public void TryParse_NoInstance_Rejected(){const string text=@"

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]
";Assert.False(ScenePatchParser.TryParse(text,"m","p.tscn",out _,out var error));Assert.Contains("instance",error);}[Fact]public void TryParse_UnknownExtResource_Rejected(){const string text=@"

using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Patching;using Engine.Tests.Mods.Mocks;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class ScenePatchResolverTests{[Fact]public void CollectFromProviders_GroupsSameTargetAcrossModsInLoadOrder(){var modA=new MockModFileProvider("mod_a");modA.AddFile("patches/scenes/main_menu.tscn",PatchText("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","A"));var modB=new MockModFileProvider("mod_b");modB.AddFile("patches/scenes/main_menu.tscn",PatchText("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","B"));var result=ScenePatchResolver.CollectFromProviders(new[]{("mod_a",(IModFileProvider)modA),("mod_b",(IModFileProvider)modB),});Assert.True(result.TryGetValue("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",out var layers));Assert.Equal(2,layers.Count);Assert.Equal("mod_a",layers[0].ModId);Assert.Equal("mod_b",layers[1].ModId);}[Fact]public void CollectFromProviders_SortsFilesInsideOneMod(){var mod=new MockModFileProvider("mod_a");mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/20-second.tscn",PatchText("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","second"));mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/00-first.tscn",PatchText("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","first"));var result=ScenePatchResolver.CollectFromProviders(new[]{("mod_a",(IModFileProvider)mod)});var layers=result["scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"];Assert.EndsWith("00-first.tscn",layers[0].SourcePath);Assert.EndsWith("20-second.tscn",layers[1].SourcePath);}[Fact]public void CollectFromProviders_NestedDirectoriesAreScanned(){var mod=new MockModFileProvider("mod_a");mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",PatchText("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","nested"));var result=ScenePatchResolver.CollectFromProviders(new[]{("mod_a",(IModFileProvider)mod)});Assert.True(result.ContainsKey("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));}[Fact]public void CollectFromProviders_NonPatchFilesAreSkipped(){var mod=new MockModFileProvider("mod_a");mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/broken.tscn","[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Root\" type=\"Control\"]");mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/readme.txt","not a scene");var result=ScenePatchResolver.CollectFromProviders(new[]{("mod_a",(IModFileProvider)mod)});Assert.Empty(result);}[Fact]public void CollectFromProviders_ModTargetPrefixNormalized(){var mod=new MockModFileProvider("mod_a");mod.AddFile("patches/scenes/panel.tscn",PatchText("res://mods/mod_a/scenes/ui/panel.tscn","x"));var result=ScenePatchResolver.CollectFromProviders(new[]{("mod_a",(IModFileProvider)mod)});Assert.True(result.ContainsKey("scenes/ui/panel.tscn"));}private static string PatchText(string targetPath,string text){return string.Join("\n","[gd_scene format=3]","",$"[ext_resource type=\"PackedScene\" path=\"{targetPath}\" id=\"1_target\"]","","[node name=\"Root\" instance=ExtResource(\"1_target\")]","[node name=\"Added\" type=\"Label\" parent=\".\"]",$"text = \"{text}\"");}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Scenes/ScenePatchTargetResolverTests.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.Patching;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class ScenePatchTargetResolverTests{[Fact]public void Normalize_StripsSchemeAndLeadingSlashes(){Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize("/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));}[Fact]public void Normalize_StripsModsPrefix(){Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize("res://mods/my_mod/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));Assert.Equal("scenes/ui/panel.tscn",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize("mods/other_mod/scenes/ui/panel.tscn"));}[Fact]public void Normalize_ConvertsBackslashesAndEmptyInput(){Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize(@"res://mods\my_mod\scenes\menu\main_menu.tscn"));Assert.Equal("",ScenePatchTargetResolver.Normalize(null));}[Fact]public void MatchesSceneKey_ExactAndSuffix(){Assert.True(ScenePatchTargetResolver.MatchesSceneKey("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));Assert.True(ScenePatchTargetResolver.MatchesSceneKey("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","res://mods/my_mod/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"));Assert.False(ScenePatchTargetResolver.MatchesSceneKey("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn","res://scenes/menu/settings_menu.tscn"));}[Fact]public void Resolve_PrefersExactKeyOverSuffix(){var keys=new[]{"menu/main_menu.tscn","scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"};var result=ScenePatchTargetResolver.Resolve("res://scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",keys);Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",result);}[Fact]public void Resolve_SuffixMatchWhenKeyHasExtraPrefix(){var keys=new[]{"scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn"};var result=ScenePatchTargetResolver.Resolve("res://mods/my_mod/scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",keys);Assert.Equal("scenes/menu/main_menu.tscn",result);Assert.Null(ScenePatchTargetResolver.Resolve("res://scenes/menu/missing.tscn",keys));}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Scenes/TscnParserTests.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using Cthangover.Core.Scenes.TscnParsing;namespace Engine.Tests.Scenes{public class TscnLexerTests{[Fact]public void Tokenize_Empty(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("");Assert.Empty(tokens);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_CommentLine(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("; comment line");Assert.Empty(tokens);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_GdSceneHeader(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("[gd_scene format=3]");Assert.Single(tokens);Assert.Equal(TscnTokenType.SectionHeader,tokens[0].Type);Assert.Equal("gd_scene",tokens[0].SectionName);Assert.Equal("3",tokens[0].SectionAttrs["format"]);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_ExtResource(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("[ext_resource type=\"Script\" path=\"res://Core/MyScript.cs\" id=\"1_script\"]");Assert.Single(tokens);var header=tokens[0];Assert.Equal("ext_resource",header.SectionName);Assert.Equal("Script",header.SectionAttrs["type"]);Assert.Equal("res://Core/MyScript.cs",header.SectionAttrs["path"]);Assert.Equal("1_script",header.SectionAttrs["id"]);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_NodeHeader(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("[node name=\"Root\" type=\"Control\"]");Assert.Single(tokens);var header=tokens[0];Assert.Equal("node",header.SectionName);Assert.Equal("Root",header.SectionAttrs["name"]);Assert.Equal("Control",header.SectionAttrs["type"]);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_NodeWithParent(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("[node name=\"Child\" parent=\".\" type=\"TextureRect\"]");Assert.Single(tokens);var header=tokens[0];Assert.Equal("Child",header.SectionAttrs["name"]);Assert.Equal(".",header.SectionAttrs["parent"]);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_PropertyLine(){var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize("anchor_right = 1.0");Assert.Single(tokens);Assert.Equal(TscnTokenType.Property,tokens[0].Type);Assert.Equal("anchor_right",tokens[0].PropertyKey);Assert.Equal("1.0",tokens[0].PropertyValue);}[Fact]public void Tokenize_FullScene(){var source=@"
[gd_scene format=3]
[ext_resource type=""Script"" path=""res://test.cs"" id=""1""]

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]
anchor_right = 1.0
script = ExtResource(""1"")

[node name=""Child"" parent=""."" type=""Label""]
text = ""Hello""
".Trim();var lexer=new TscnLexer();var tokens=lexer.Tokenize(source);Assert.Equal(7,tokens.Count);Assert.Equal("gd_scene",tokens[0].SectionName);Assert.Equal("ext_resource",tokens[1].SectionName);Assert.Equal("node",tokens[2].SectionName);Assert.Equal(TscnTokenType.Property,tokens[3].Type);Assert.Equal(TscnTokenType.Property,tokens[4].Type);Assert.Equal("node",tokens[5].SectionName);Assert.Equal(TscnTokenType.Property,tokens[6].Type);}}public class TscnParserTests{[Fact]public void Parse_MinimalScene(){var source=@"
[gd_scene format=3]

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]
anchor_right = 1.0
".Trim();var parser=new TscnParser();var scene=parser.Parse(source);Assert.NotNull(scene);Assert.Equal("3",scene.Format);Assert.Single(scene.Nodes);Assert.Equal("Root",scene.Nodes[0].Name);Assert.Equal("Control",scene.Nodes[0].Type);Assert.Single(scene.Nodes[0].Properties);Assert.Equal(1.0f,scene.Nodes[0].Properties["anchor_right"]);}[Fact]public void Parse_WithExtResources(){var source=@"
[gd_scene format=3]
[ext_resource type=""Script"" path=""res://test.cs"" id=""1""]
[ext_resource type=""PackedScene"" path=""res://child.tscn"" id=""2""]

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]
script = ExtResource(""1"")

[node name=""Child"" parent=""."" instance=ExtResource(""2"")]
visible = false
".Trim();var parser=new TscnParser();var scene=parser.Parse(source);Assert.Equal(2,scene.ExtResources.Count);Assert.Equal("Script",scene.ExtResources[0].Type);Assert.Equal("res://test.cs",scene.ExtResources[0].Path);Assert.Equal("1",scene.ExtResources[0].Id);Assert.Equal("res://child.tscn",scene.ExtResources[1].Path);Assert.Equal(2,scene.Nodes.Count);Assert.Equal("ExtResource(\"1\")",scene.Nodes[0].ScriptRef);Assert.Equal("ExtResource(\"2\")",scene.Nodes[1].InstanceRef);Assert.Equal(false,scene.Nodes[1].Properties["visible"]);}[Fact]public void Parse_NodeHierarchy(){var source=@"
[gd_scene format=3]

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]

[node name=""Panel"" parent=""."" type=""Control""]

[node name=""Button"" parent=""Panel"" type=""Button""]
text = ""Click""
".Trim();var parser=new TscnParser();var scene=parser.Parse(source);Assert.Equal(3,scene.Nodes.Count);Assert.Equal("Root",scene.Nodes[0].Name);Assert.Null(scene.Nodes[0].Parent);Assert.Equal("Panel",scene.Nodes[1].Name);Assert.Equal(".",scene.Nodes[1].Parent);Assert.Equal("Button",scene.Nodes[2].Name);Assert.Equal("Panel",scene.Nodes[2].Parent);}[Fact]public void Parse_Groups(){var source="[node name=\"Root\" type=\"Control\" groups=[\"test_group\"]]";var parser=new TscnParser();var scene=parser.Parse(source);Assert.Single(scene.Nodes);Assert.NotNull(scene.Nodes[0].Groups);Assert.Single(scene.Nodes[0].Groups);Assert.Equal("test_group",scene.Nodes[0].Groups[0]);}[Fact]public void Parse_Connections(){var source=@"
[gd_scene format=3]

[node name=""Root"" type=""Control""]

".Trim();var parser=new TscnParser();var scene=parser.Parse(source);Assert.Single(scene.Connections);Assert.Equal("pressed",scene.Connections[0].Signal);Assert.Equal("Btn",scene.Connections[0].From);Assert.Equal(".",scene.Connections[0].To);Assert.Equal("OnPressed",scene.Connections[0].Method);}[Fact]public void Parse_IgnoresNonScriptConnections(){// Connection with to="Light" should be ignored (not a script method)

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Settings/SaveDormancyTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using Cthangover.Core.Settings;namespace Engine.Tests.Settings{/// <summary> A save survives composition changes of its game: slots of mods the game runs without stay dormant and come back with the mod; nothing is dropped. </summary>
public class SaveDormancyTests{private static readonly DateTime Now=new(2026,10,16,12,0,0,DateTimeKind.Utc);[Fact]public void LoadedMods_AreLive_MissingMod_GoesDormant(){var data=new Dictionary<string,ModSlot>{[SavePackage.CoreSlotKey]=Slot("1.0.0"),["cards"]=Slot("1.0.0"),["recruiting"]=Slot("1.1.0"),};var result=SaveDormancy.Classify(data,null,Installed(("cards","1.0.0")),"0.1.0",Now);Assert.True(result.Live.ContainsKey("cards"));Assert.False(result.Live.ContainsKey(SavePackage.CoreSlotKey));var dormant=result.Dormant["recruiting"];Assert.True(dormant.Metadata.Dormant);Assert.Equal(nameof(SaveDormancy.Reason.NotLoaded),dormant.Metadata.DormantReason);Assert.Equal(Now,dormant.Metadata.DormantSince);Assert.Contains(("recruiting",SaveDormancy.Reason.NotLoaded),result.NewlyDormant);}[Fact]public void DormantSlot_IsRevived_WhenModReturns(){var dormant=new Dictionary<string,ModSlot>{["recruiting"]=SaveDormancy.MarkDormant(Slot("1.1.0"),SaveDormancy.Reason.NotLoaded,Now.AddDays(-3)),};var void StillMissingDormantSlot_KeepsFirstDormancyDate(){var since=Now.AddDays(-10);var dormant=new void LiveSlot_WinsOverOlderDormantCopy_ButDormantCopyIsKept(){var data=new Dictionary<string,ModSlot>{["cards"]=Slot("1.0.0")};var dormant=new void SlotNeedingNewerCore_GoesDormant(){var slot=Slot("1.0.0");slot.Metadata.MinCoreVersion="2.0.0";var result=SaveDormancy.Classify(new static ModSlot Slot(string version)=>new(){Metadata=new ModMetadata{ModVersion=version}};private static Func<string,string>Installed(params(string Id,string Version)[]mods){var map=new ===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Utils/BoundedCacheTests.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Utils;namespace Engine.Tests.Utils{/// <summary> Контракты LRU-кэша с callback'ом вытеснения: вытеснение по ёмкости, порядок LRU, сброс через Invalidate/Clear и поведение на null-значениях загрузчика. Проверяются данные (что именно и сколько раз вытеснено), а не факты внутренних вызовов. </summary>
public class BoundedCacheTests{/// <summary>Loader, возвращающий для каждого ключа значение "v-ключ" — удобно проверять, какой именно payload вытеснен.</summary>
private sealed class KeyedLoader:ICacheLoader<string,string>{public int LoadCalls;public string Load(string key){LoadCalls++;return"v-"+key;}}/// <summary>Loader с настраиваемым значением на каждый промах; null означает «не кэшировать».</summary>
private sealed class FixedLoader:ICacheLoader<string,string>{public int LoadCalls;public string NextValue;public string Load(string key){LoadCalls++;return NextValue;}}[Fact]public void Get_Miss_LoadsThroughLoader(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader);Assert.Equal("v-a",cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(1,loader.LoadCalls);}[Fact]public void Get_Hit_DoesNotReload(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader);cache.Get("a");Assert.Equal("v-a",cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(1,loader.LoadCalls);}[Fact]public void Get_CapacityExceeded_EvictsLeastRecentlyUsedWithCallback(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Get("b");cache.Get("c");Assert.Equal(2,cache.Count);Assert.Equal(new[]{"v-a"},evicted);Assert.Equal("v-b",cache.Get("b"));Assert.Equal("v-c",cache.Get("c"));Assert.Equal(3,loader.LoadCalls);}[Fact]public void Get_TouchRefreshesLruOrder_EvictsNotRecentlyUsed(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Get("b");cache.Get("a");cache.Get("c");Assert.Equal(2,cache.Count);Assert.Equal(new[]{"v-b"},evicted);Assert.Equal("v-a",cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(3,loader.LoadCalls);}[Fact]public void Get_LoaderReturnsNull_NotCachedAndNoEviction(){var loader=new FixedLoader{NextValue=null};var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader,evicted.Add);Assert.Null(cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(0,cache.Count);Assert.Null(cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(2,loader.LoadCalls);Assert.Empty(evicted);}[Fact]public void Invalidate_ExistingKey_NotifiesCallbackAndReloads(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Invalidate("a");Assert.Equal(0,cache.Count);Assert.Equal(new[]{"v-a"},evicted);Assert.Equal("v-a",cache.Get("a"));Assert.Equal(2,loader.LoadCalls);}[Fact]public void Invalidate_MissingKey_DoesNotNotify(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Invalidate("missing");Assert.Empty(evicted);Assert.Equal(1,cache.Count);}[Fact]public void Clear_NotifiesForEveryEntry(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(0,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Get("b");cache.Get("c");cache.Clear();Assert.Equal(0,cache.Count);Assert.Equal(3,evicted.Count);Assert.Contains("v-a",evicted);Assert.Contains("v-b",evicted);Assert.Contains("v-c",evicted);}[Fact]public void EvictionWithoutCallback_DoesNotThrow(){var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(2,new KeyedLoader());cache.Get("a");cache.Get("b");cache.Get("c");Assert.Equal(2,cache.Count);}[Fact]public void MaxSize_Zero_UnboundedAndNoEviction(){var loader=new KeyedLoader();var evicted=new List<string>();var cache=new BoundedCache<string,string>(0,loader,evicted.Add);cache.Get("a");cache.Get("b");cache.Get("c");Assert.Equal(0,cache.MaxSize);Assert.Equal(3,cache.Count);Assert.Empty(evicted);}}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Utils/JsonPatchMergerTests.cs =====
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;using System.Text.Json;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Mods.Patches;namespace Engine.Tests.Utils{public class JsonPatchMergerTests{[Fact]public void Merge_NoPatches_ReturnsOriginalJson(){var merger=new JsonPatchMerger();var json="{\"Id\":\"Test\",\"Value\":42}";var result=merger.Merge(json,"Test",patches);Assert.Contains("\"Health\":50",result);Assert.Contains("\"Strength\":10",result);Assert.Contains("\"Magic\":5",result);}[Fact]public void Merge_SpecificOverridesWildcard(){var merger=new JsonPatchMerger();var json="{\"Id\":\"Test\",\"Value\":10}";var patches=new Dictionary<string,List<PropertyPatch>>{["*"]=new(){Patch("*",("Value",ParseJson("100")))},["Test"]=new(){Patch("Test",("Value",ParseJson("200")))}};var key=JsonPatchMerger.DetectIdentityKey(arr);Assert.Null(key);}[Fact]public void BuildMergeKeys_WithIMergeable_ReturnsMapping(){var keys=JsonPatchMerger.BuildMergeKeys<TestMergeEntity>();Assert.True(keys.ContainsKey("Loot"));Assert.Equal("ItemId",keys["Loot"]);Assert.False(keys.ContainsKey("SimpleList"));}private class TestMergeEntity{public List<TestMergeable>Loot{get;set;}public List<string>SimpleList{get;set;}}private class TestMergeable:IMergeable{public string MergeKeyProperty=>"ItemId";public string ItemId{get;set;}}private static JsonElement ParseJson(string json){return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<JsonElement>(json);}private static Dictionary<string,List<PropertyPatch>>Patches(string targetId,params(string key,JsonElement value)[]props){return new Dictionary<string,List<PropertyPatch>>{[targetId]=new List<PropertyPatch>{Patch(targetId,props)}};}private static Dictionary<string,List<PropertyPatch>>Pad(string targetId,params(string key,JsonElement value)[]props){var patch=new PropertyPatch{ID=targetId,ExtraProperties=new Dictionary<string,object>()};foreach(var(key,value)in props)patch.ExtraProperties[key]=value;return new Dictionary<string,List<PropertyPatch>>{[targetId]=new List<PropertyPatch>{patch}};}private static PropertyPatch Patch(string id,params(string key,JsonElement value)[]props){var patch=new PropertyPatch{ID=id,ExtraProperties=new Dictionary<string,object>()};foreach(var(key,value)in props)patch.ExtraProperties[key]=value;return patch;}private static string JsonArr(string key,string arrJson){return"{\"Id\":\"Test\","+"\""+key+"\":"+arrJson+"}";}}}
