===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Utils/ReflectionsTests.cs =====
namespace Cthangover.Tests.Utils{using System;using Cthangover.Core.Mods;using Cthangover.Core.Utils;using Cthangover.Tests.Mods.Unload;/// <summary> Whole-domain scans see the running game only: an unloaded mod's assembly lingers in the AppDomain until collected and must not be rediscovered (it re-registered a released battle core once). </summary>
[Collection("mod-load-context")]public class ReflectionsTests{private const string ModSource=@"
namespace ScanMod
{
    public class ScanInit : Cthangover.Core.Mods.IModInitializer
    {
        public void OnModLoaded(string modId) { }
        public void OnModResourcesReady() { }
    }
}
";[Fact]public void LoadedAssemblies_ExcludesReleasedModAssemblies(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_scan");var mod=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);Assert.Contains(mod,Reflections.LoadedAssemblies());Assert.Contains(typeof(Reflections).Assembly,Reflections.LoadedAssemblies());ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);Assert.DoesNotContain(mod,Reflections.LoadedAssemblies());}[Fact]public void FindAndCreate_WholeDomain_SkipsReleasedModAssemblies(){var modId=TestMods.NewId("test_scan_create");var mod=TestMods.Compile(modId,ModSource);var scanType=mod.GetType("ScanMod.ScanInit");Assert.Contains(Reflections.FindAndCreate<IModInitializer>(),IsInstanceOf(scanType));ModAssemblyLoader.UnloadMod(modId);Assert.DoesNotContain(Reflections.FindAndCreate<IModInitializer>(),IsInstanceOf(scanType));}private static Predicate<IModInitializer>IsInstanceOf(Type type)=>type.IsInstanceOfType;}}

===== FILE tests/Cthangover.Tests/Utils/VersionHelperTests.cs =====
using Cthangover.Core.Utils;namespace Engine.Tests.Utils{public class VersionHelperTests{// ─── Semver (strict numeric) ───
[Fact]public void Compare_SemverMajor_AGreater(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("2.0","1.0")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_SemverMajor_ALess(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1.0","2.0")<0);}[Fact]public void Compare_SemverMinor_Different(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1.2","1.1")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_SemverBuild_Different(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1.0.3","1.0.2")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_SemverRevision_Different(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1.0.0.5","1.0.0.4")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Semver_Equal(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare("1.2.3","1.2.3"));}[Fact]public void Compare_Semver_LexicographicTrap_9vs10(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("10.0","9.0")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Semver_ZeroVersions(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("0.1","0.0")>0);}// ─── Free-form (string fallback) ───
[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_SimpleGreater(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("Enigma2.0","Enigma1.5")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_SimpleLess(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("Enigma1.0","Enigma2.0")<0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_AlphaTags(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("alpha-2","alpha-1")>0);Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("beta-1","alpha-9")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_Equal(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare("Enigma1.5","Enigma1.5"));}[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_PrefixOnly(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("v2","v1")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_Freeform_CaseSensitive(){int cmp=VersionHelper.Compare("Enigma","enigma");Assert.True(cmp<0);}// ─── Letter in middle of version ───
[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_Equal(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare("1b.2.0","1b.2.0"));}[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_MinorBump(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1b.2.1","1b.2.0")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_PatchBump(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1b.2.5","1b.2.1")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_VersionJump(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("2b.0.0","1b.9.9")>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_DifferentLetter(){int cmp=VersionHelper.Compare("1c.0.0","1b.0.0");Assert.True(cmp>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_LetterInMiddle_ShorterWins(){int cmp=VersionHelper.Compare("1b.2","1b.2.0");Assert.True(cmp==0);}// ─── Mixed: semver vs free-form (both unparseable as Version) ───
[Fact]public void Compare_SingleComponent(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("2","1")>0);Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1","2")<0);}[Fact]public void Compare_MajorMinor_AsNumbers(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("2.0","1.0")>0);}// ─── Edge cases ───
[Fact]public void Compare_BothNull_Equal(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare(null,null));}[Fact]public void Compare_ANull_ALess(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare(null,"1.0")<0);}[Fact]public void Compare_BNull_AGreater(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("1.0",null)>0);}[Fact]public void Compare_BothEmpty_Equal(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare("",""));}[Fact]public void Compare_Whitespace_Trimmed(){Assert.Equal(0,VersionHelper.Compare("  1.0  ","1.0"));}[Fact]public void Compare_EmptyVsSemver(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.Compare("","1.0")<0);}// ─── Helpers ───
[Fact]public void IsGreaterThan_True(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.IsGreaterThan("2.0","1.0"));}[Fact]public void IsGreaterThan_False(){Assert.False(VersionHelper.IsGreaterThan("1.0","2.0"));}[Fact]public void IsLessThan_True(){Assert.True(VersionHelper.IsLessThan("1.0","2.0"));}[Fact]public void IsLessThan_False(){Assert.False(VersionHelper.IsLessThan("2.0","1.0"));}[Fact]public void IsGreaterThan_Equal(){Assert.False(VersionHelper.IsGreaterThan("1.0","1.0"));}[Fact]public void IsLessThan_Equal(){Assert.False(VersionHelper.IsLessThan("1.0","1.0"));}}}
